### [63] [ProcBench: Evaluating Process-Level Defects and Control Preservation in LLM Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20251)
*Jiawei He,Jie Jia,Chenbo Liu,Chaoyi Xue,Yapeng Song,Xikai Yang,Dong Sun*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ProcBench 是一个面向编码智能体轨迹评估的基准框架，通过构建可复用的缺陷本体、统一轨迹表示和校准风险评分卡，提供对多步执行过程中过程级缺陷的细粒度诊断能力，弥补了传统结果导向评估的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体基准主要关注最终结果（如任务完成率、编译成功率、测试通过率），无法揭示多步操作中的过程级缺陷和重复性故障模式，缺乏对执行轨迹的可见性和诊断能力。

Method: 提出 ProcBench 框架：(1) 构建可复用的执行缺陷本体论；(2) 将异构日志标准化为统一的轨迹表示；(3) 基于校准风险生成评分卡，而非仅依赖最终结果。在 200 条标注轨迹上实例化，并在三个基准（AndroidBench、TerminalBench、SWE-bench-Verified）上验证。

Result: ProcBench 可被可靠实例化；校准方法相比直接阈值法提高了缺陷发现的经验可解释性；过程感知评分卡能提供超越传统结果评估的诊断性区分。

Conclusion: ProcBench 为编码智能体评估提供了过程感知的新范式，但存在标注依赖、部分缺陷类别可观测性有限、需更广泛外部验证等局限性。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks for LLM coding agents mainly evaluate final outcomes, such as task completion, compilation success, and test pass rates. While these metrics are useful for measuring end-task capability, they provide limited visibility into how an execution unfolds and often miss recurrent process-level failures that arise during multi-step operation. We present ProcBench, a benchmark-oriented framework for evaluating coding-agent trajectories through process defects and control preservation. ProcBench organizes execution failures into a reusable ontology, standardizes heterogeneous logs into a unified trajectory representation, and reports calibrated risk-based scorecards instead of relying only on final outcomes. We instantiate ProcBench on an annotated set of 200 trajectories and apply it across three coding-agent benchmarks: AndroidBench, TerminalBench, and SWE-bench-Verified. Our results suggest that ProcBench can be instantiated with useful reliability, that calibration improves the empirical interpretability of defect findings relative to direct thresholding, and that process-aware scorecards provide diagnostic distinctions beyond conventional outcome-based evaluation. We also discuss limitations, including annotation dependence, partial observability for some defect classes, and the need for broader external validation.

</details>


### [64] [Combined Program Analysis Techniques: A Systematic Mapping Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20310)
*Pietro Braione,Giovanni Denaro,Luca Gugliemo,Elson Kurian,Enea Raffaele Ilario Papaleo,Martino Tessaro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对248篇关于程序分析技术组合的研究进行了系统综述，提出了一个分类法来归类这些组合技术，揭示了不同分析技术之间交互和协同的模式与目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自80年代以来，程序分析技术的组合被认为是有前景的方向，但缺乏对其交互模式和协同效应的系统性理解。本文旨在通过文献综述建立结构化的分类体系，回答为何以及如何组合程序分析技术，以及能带来哪些益处。

Method: 对248篇主要研究进行系统调查，提出一个原创分类法，根据协同效应目标、分析间工作流和交互模式（映射函数）对组合技术进行分类，并将主要研究映射到该分类法上以回答研究问题。

Result: 建立了包含协同效应目标、分析间工作流和映射函数三个维度的分类体系，系统揭示了现有组合程序分析技术的共性和差异。

Conclusion: 该分类法和文献映射提供了一个概念框架，能够帮助研究人员和从业者理解现有组合程序分析技术，并指导未来新型组合技术和分析框架的研发。

Abstract: Context. Since the eighties, the combination of program analysis techniques has been increasingly recognized as a promising approach to overcome the limitations of standalone methods. While individual techniques, based on either static or dynamic analysis, address important challenges in software dependability, their integration often yields synergistic effects on precision, coverage and insights.
  Objective. This paper surveys a significant portion of the modern literature on combining program analysis techniques, consisting of 248 primary studies, with the aim of cataloging the types of interactions and synergies that were exploited to define combined-program-analysis techniques so far. The goal is to provide a structured understanding of why and how program analysis techniques can be conjoined, and which benefits can arise from their interactions.
  Method. We devise an original taxonomy that classifies combined-program-analysis techniques according to their aimed synergistic effects, inter-analysis workflows and interaction schemata (to which we refer to as mapping functions). We then map the primary studies to the taxonomy, answering research questions on which synergistic effects those studies pursued via the combination of analysis techniques, which inter-analysis workflows they embodied, and which types of mapping functions they exploited.
  Conclusion. Our taxonomy and literature mapping reveal the commonalities and the differences, in terms of goals and patterns, in the design of combined-program-analysis techniques. Thereby we provide a framework of concepts that can foster the ability of researchers and practitioners to reason on existing combined-program-analysis techniques, and steer further research on new useful combined-program-analysis techniques and analysis frameworks.

</details>


### [65] [A Semantic-Web Oriented Competency Model for Engineering Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20401)
*Nicolas Evain,Ernesto Exposito,Philippe Arnould*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将知识体系（BoKs）映射到能力框架的课程设计方法，并通过ISANUM五年制工程学位项目（含23项能力、494个知识主题、34个知识领域及三条专业路径）验证了其可行性，同时搭建了支持协作的语义Wiki基础设施（ISANUMpedia）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管软件工程、计算机科学、信息系统等领域已拥有全面的知识体系（BoKs），但缺乏将这些知识整合为连贯的、基于能力的专业课程的方法论，导致毕业生难以获得充分的职业准备。

Method: 提出一种能力映射方法论，将知识体系（BoKs）与能力框架桥接。通过ISANUM五年制工程项目进行案例验证：设计23项能力，划分为5个主题模块，显式映射到Computing Curricula 2020中34个计算知识领域的494个知识主题；整合三条专业路径（软件工程、数据工程与数据科学、信息技术），并融入强制性工学结合项目。

Result: 成功构建了ISANUM学位项目，实现了知识体系到可评估能力框架的系统性转化；开发了ISANUMpedia语义Wiki基础设施，支持课程的理解、维护与协同演化。

Conclusion: 提供了一种可复现的方法论，帮助计算教育者将知识体系转化为可评估的能力框架，并配套了支持协作的底层基础设施。

Abstract: Despite comprehensive Bodies of Knowledge (BoKs) documenting core knowledge across software engineering, computer science, information systems, and emerging computing fields, a critical gap persists: methodologies for integrating this knowledge into coherent competency-based curricula that prepare graduates for professional careers remain underdeveloped. This paper presents a competency-mapping methodology that bridges Bodies of Knowledge and competency frameworks to design computing curricula. We demonstrate this methodology through ISANUM, a five-year engineering degree program featuring 23 competencies organized into five thematic blocks, each with explicit mappings to 494 knowledge topics from 34 Computing Knowledge areas defined in Computing Curricula 2020. The program integrates three specialized pathways (Software Engineering, Data Engineering \& Data Science, and Information Technology) with mandatory work-study programs, ensuring graduates develop both theoretical foundations and practical workplace competencies. Our contribution provides computing educators with a replicable methodology for translating Bodies of Knowledge into assessable competency frameworks, supported by a semantic wiki infrastructure (ISANUMpedia) enabling collaborative curriculum understanding, maintenance and evolution.

</details>


### [66] [Agentic Agile-V: From Vibe Coding to Verified Engineering in Software and Hardware Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20456)
*Christopher Koch*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出自主AI编码系统的主要问题已不再是提示工程，而是工程过程控制；并提出了Agentic Agile-V框架，通过SCOPE-V循环将对话意图转化为结构化的工程工件和验收证据，强调自主AI并未消除工程纪律，反而增加了对需求、约束、可追溯性、独立验证和人工审批的要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自主AI编码系统虽能加速开发，但现有证据表明其并非总能自动改进工程成果——在成熟开源项目中甚至出现效率下降，且在仓库配置、依赖管理、权限控制和硬件验证等方面持续失败。因此需要从提示工程转向工程过程控制。

Method: 综合来自自主软件工程、GitHub规模采纳研究、仓库级智能体配置、生产力试验、问题解决基准测试以及硬件/RTL验证研究的证据；提出Agentic Agile-V框架，以Agile-V为生命周期主干，结合SCOPE-V循环（Specify, Constrain, Orchestrate, Prove, Evolve, Verify）将对话意图转化为结构化工程工件与验收证据。

Result: 贡献了四项成果：(i) 自主软件、固件和硬件工作所需的最小输入工件分类法；(ii) 区分探索性对话与实施的对话-契约门控；(iii) 风险自适应的特性、修复、测试和硬件工作流；(iv) 面向智能体生成工件的证据包验收模型。

Conclusion: 自主AI并未消除工程纪律，反而提升了需求、约束、可追溯性、独立验证和人工审批的价值。

Abstract: Agentic AI coding systems can inspect repositories, plan implementation steps, edit files, call tools, run tests, and submit pull requests. These capabilities make software and hardware development faster in some settings, but current evidence does not support the simple claim that autonomous code generation automatically improves engineering outcomes. Controlled studies report productivity gains in some enterprise tasks, slowdowns in mature open-source work, moderate but heterogeneous meta-analytic effects, and persistent failures in repository setup, dependency handling, permission gating, and hardware verification. This paper argues that the central problem is no longer prompt engineering; it is engineering process control. It synthesizes evidence from agentic software engineering, GitHub-scale adoption studies, repository-level agent configuration, productivity trials, issue-resolution benchmarks, and hardware/RTL verification research. It proposes Agentic Agile-V, a process framework that uses Agile-V as the lifecycle backbone and a task-level SCOPE-V loop - Specify, Constrain, Orchestrate, Prove, Evolve, and Verify - to convert conversational intent into structured engineering artifacts and acceptance evidence. The paper contributes: (i) a taxonomy of minimum input artifacts for agentic software, firmware, and hardware work; (ii) a conversation-to-contract gate that separates exploratory dialogue from implementation; (iii) risk-adaptive feature, bug-fix, testing, and hardware workflows; and (iv) an evidence-bundle acceptance model for agent-generated artifacts. The paper concludes that agentic AI does not eliminate engineering discipline; it increases the value of requirements, constraints, traceability, independent verification, and human approval.

</details>


### [67] [Code Generation by Differential Test Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20473)
*Yifeng He,Ethan Wang,Jicheng Wang,Xuanxin Ouyang,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DiffCodeGen 是一种基于覆盖引导差分分析的新型测试时扩展方法，通过生成多样化代码候选项、覆盖引导模糊测试合成输入、基于行为相似性聚类，最终选择最大簇的medoid作为输出。该方法无需额外LLM推理调用，完全异步且高效可扩展，在4个LLM上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时扩展方法依赖实践中不可用的公开测试用例，或需要大量LLM推理进行候选选择，导致严重的token消耗和时间开销。

Method: DiffCodeGen 首先使用多种采样和提示策略生成多样化代码候选项；然后应用覆盖引导模糊测试合成输入（无需现有测试用例或LLM）；在所有候选上执行这些输入以捕获动态行为；基于行为相似性对候选进行聚类；选择最大簇的medoid作为最终输出。整个过程无需额外模型调用。

Result: 在4个大型语言模型上的评估显示，DiffCodeGen 相较于基线方法取得一致改进。与最先进的测试时扩展方法相比，在仅使用少量时间和token的情况下达到具有竞争力或更优的性能。

Conclusion: DiffCodeGen 是一种模型无关、高效且高度可扩展的测试时扩展方法，可与推理模型结合进一步提升性能，特别适合当前智能体编码趋势。

Abstract: Test-time scaling has emerged as a promising approach for improving code generation by exploring large solution spaces at inference time. However, existing methods often rely on public test cases that are unavailable in practice, or require extensive LLM inference for candidate selection, leading to significant token consumption and time overhead. We present DiffCodeGen, a novel test-time scaling method for code generation based on coverage-guided differential analysis. DiffCodeGen generates diverse code candidates using various sampling and prompting strategies, then applies coverage-guided fuzzing to synthesize inputs without requiring any existing tests or large language models. By executing all candidates on these inputs, DiffCodeGen captures their dynamic behavior and clusters candidates based on behavioral similarity. DiffCodeGen selects the medoid of the largest cluster as the final output. Unlike prior test-time scaling methods that invoke additional LLM inference for candidate selection, DiffCodeGen performs selection without any extra model calls, incurring little to no additional token consumption. DiffCodeGen is fully asynchronous, naturally suited to the current trend of agentic coding, and is thus efficient and highly scalable. We evaluate DiffCodeGen across 4 large language models, demonstrating consistent improvements over baselines. Compared to state-of-the-art test-time scaling methods, DiffCodeGen achieves competitive or superior performance while using only a fraction of time and tokens. DiffCodeGen is model-agnostic and can be combined with reasoning models to further boost performance.

</details>


### [68] [A Multi-Layer Testing Framework for Automated Data Quality Assurance in Cloud-Native ELT Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20500)
*Ismail Gargouri,Hassan Reza*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向云原生ELT管道的多层测试框架，集成了编排层验证、声明式dbt测试、LLM生成的语义测试以及跨存储一致性检查。实验表明，LLM增强配置检测率达100%（16/16），相比手动基线（7/16）提升了128.57%，且整个工作流在106.58秒内完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云原生ELT管道面临异构数据源、模式演变和多后端执行环境带来的数据质量挑战，现有测试方法难以全面覆盖。

Method: 提出统一多层测试框架，集成Apache Airflow编排、dbt声明式测试、LLM生成的语义测试断言，以及DuckDB与Snowflake之间的跨存储一致性校验。通过受控异常注入实验评估检测率。

Result: 手动基线检测7/16异常；手动扩展比较器和LLM增强配置均检测全部16个异常（提升128.57%）。跨存储验证确认三张表完全一致。25个LLM断言中9个有用、4个冗余、12个可执行但低价值。完整工作流耗时106.58秒。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的语义测试合成能够有效增强生产级ELT环境中的验证覆盖率，同时在操作上保持可行性。

Abstract: Ensuring data quality in cloud-native Extract-Load-Transform (ELT) pipelines is increasingly challenging due to heterogeneous data sources, evolving schemas, and multi-backend execution environments. This paper presents a unified, multi-layer testing framework that integrates orchestration-level validation, declarative dbt tests, large language model (LLM)-generated semantic tests, and cross-store consistency checking between DuckDB and Snowflake, orchestrated through Apache Airflow. Controlled anomaly-injection experiments demonstrate that a manual-only baseline detected 7 of 16 injected anomalies. In contrast, both a manually expanded comparator and the proposed LLM-augmented configuration detected all 16, representing a 128.57% relative improvement in detection rate over the baseline. Post-migration cross-store validation confirmed exact agreement across all three curated tables. Of 25 LLM-generated test assertions, 9 were classified as useful, 4 as redundant, and 12 as executable but low-value. The complete workflow executed in 106.58 seconds across eight instrumented pipeline stages. These results demonstrate that LLM-driven semantic test synthesis can materially strengthen validation coverage while remaining operationally practical for production ELT environments.

</details>


### [69] [An Event-Driven Tool for Context-Aware Code Smell Detection Using SmellDSL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20675)
*Matheus dos Santos Viegas,Adrian Gabriel Keller dos Santos,Kleinner Farias,Robson Keemps da Silva*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SmellHunter是一个上下文感知的代码异味检测工具，通过SmellDSL领域特定语言脚本集成静态指标与团队特征、项目阶段等上下文信息，采用事件驱动架构，通过Eclipse插件和移动应用提供可操作的分析结果，支持重构决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码异味检测工具仅依赖静态度量指标，忽略了异味产生和解决过程中的开发上下文，导致误报率高且对重构决策支持不足。

Method: 提出SmellHunter工具，其核心是SmellDSL领域特定语言编写的脚本，用于定义和检测代码异味；采用事件驱动架构，通过服务总线协调验证、解释和持久化服务；集成静态代码度量与团队特征、项目阶段、地理位置等上下文信息；通过Eclipse插件和移动应用提供可视化分析。

Result: 实现了上下文感知的代码异味检测，能够按类型、严重程度和位置聚合分析结果，支持将重构任务分配给具有相关专长的开发者，减少对开发者工作流的干扰。

Conclusion: SmellHunter通过将异味检测与上下文数据和协作可视化相结合，帮助团队识别特定位置反复出现的质量问题，支持更明智的重构决策，适用于现代软件开发环境。

Abstract: Code smells signal violations of design principles that degrade the internal quality of evolving software systems. Although many tools detect such anomalies using static metrics, they often ignore the development context in which smells arise and are resolved. This limitation can lead to misleading warnings and weak support for refactoring decisions. To address this problem, we present SmellHunter, a context-aware tool that interprets scripts written in the SmellDSL domain-specific language to detect and contextualize code smells. SmellHunter integrates static code metrics with contextual information (such as team characteristics, project stage, and geographic metadata) to produce richer, more actionable analyses. The tool adopts an event-driven architecture in which a service bus orchestrates validation, interpretation, and persistence services through asynchronous events. This architecture enables scalable analysis while minimizing disruption to developers' workflows. SmellHunter is integrated into the Eclipse development environment via a dedicated plugin and provides aggregated insights via a mobile application, allowing developers to explore smell occurrences by type, severity, and location. By linking smell detection with contextual data and collaborative visualization, SmellHunter supports developers acting as smell hunters, helping teams identify recurring quality issues emerging from a particular location and assign refactoring tasks to developers with relevant expertise. We describe the architecture of SmellHunter, the interpretation process of SmellDSL scripts, and the integration of contextual data to support more informed refactoring decisions in modern software development environments.

</details>


### [70] [BioDefect: The First Dataset for Defect Detection in Bioinformatics Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20788)
*Tianxiang Xu,Xiaoyan Zhu,Xin Lai,Xin Lian,Hangyu Cheng,Jiayin Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个面向生物信息学软件的缺陷检测数据集BioDefect，通过提供完整源代码仓库并解决标签不一致和数据泄露问题，在9个语言模型上实现了F1-score平均提升29.61%-38.04%，填补了该领域的研究空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件缺陷检测研究未专门针对生物信息学软件，已有数据集在该领域存在局限性，无法准确反映真实的缺陷场景。

Method: 构建了BioDefect数据集，包含完整的源代码仓库以保留缺陷代码的上下文信息，解决了标签不一致和数据泄露问题，并在9个语言模型上进行了系统评估。

Result: BioDefect在所有模型上实现了缺陷检测性能的显著提升，F1-score相比现有数据集平均提升29.61%至38.04%。

Conclusion: BioDefect填补了生物信息学软件缺陷检测的研究空白，为提高该领域软件质量保证提供了新的思路和基础。

Abstract: Software defect detection is a critical task in software engineering. However, no prior studies have specifically addressed defect detection in bioinformatics software. Given that the performance of defect detection tasks is primarily influenced by both models and datasets, our experiments controlled for model-related factors and confirmed the limitations of existing datasets in bioinformatics software. To address this issue, we introduce BioDefect, the first dataset specifically designed for defect detection in bioinformatics software, aiming to overcome the limitations of existing datasets in this context. Unlike prior datasets, BioDefect includes complete source code repositories, preserving the actual contextual information of defective code, thereby more accurately reflecting real-world defect scenarios in bioinformatics software. Additionally, BioDefect mitigates issues related to label inconsistency and data leakage, ensuring high data quality and experimental reliability. To evaluate the effectiveness of BioDefect, we conduct a systematic assessment on nine language models (LMs), including DeepSeek-R1. The results demonstrate that BioDefect significantly enhances defect detection performance for bioinformatics software. Compared to existing datasets, BioDefect achieves an average F1-score improvement of 29.61% to 38.04% across all models, highlighting its superior advantages. This study fills a critical research gap in bioinformatics software defect detection, laying a foundation for future studies in this field and offering new insights for improving bioinformatics software quality assurance.

</details>


### [71] [Transforming Privacy Artifacts into Accessible Reports for Non-Technical Stakeholders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21269)
*Zoe Pfister,Clemens Sauerwein,Benedikt Dornauer,Tina Mersch,Christian Wolf,Ruth Breu,Michael Vierhauser*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个概念框架，利用大语言模型将工业5.0中人机协作系统的隐私威胁与缓解措施转化为非技术利益相关者可理解的隐私报告，以促进知情决策和信任建立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业5.0背景下人机协作系统需要监控人类操作员，涉及敏感数据，引发隐私担忧；工人和工会因缺乏透明度而拒绝协作功能；现有需求工程实践缺乏为非技术利益相关者（如工人、工会）提供可理解的隐私威胁与缓解措施的方法论支持。

Method: 提出一个概念框架，从监控相关用例和需求出发，基于Privacy by Design原则，利用大语言模型将技术性需求工程工件转化为非技术利益相关者可访问的隐私报告。

Result: 通过两个工业用例展示初步见解，并评估了生成的隐私报告质量。

Conclusion: 该框架有望将隐私透明度整合到需求工程流程中，促进早期利益相关者参与、建立信任并支持知情决策；未来研究方向包括进一步优化报告生成机制。

Abstract: The transition toward Industry 5.0 is reshaping industrial work environments with an emphasis on human-centricity, enabling close collaboration between humans and machines to enhance productivity and flexibility. However, such systems typically require monitoring of human workers and operators, often involving sensitive data, raising significant privacy concerns. As a result, affected workers and unions frequently reject human-machine collaboration features due to a lack of transparency regarding privacy threats and implemented mitigation strategies. To enable early stakeholder involvement, establish trust, and support informed decision-making, privacy implications must be communicated in a way understandable to non-technical stakeholders. Yet, current Requirements Engineering (RE) practices provide limited methodological support for making privacy threats and mitigations accessible to non-technical stakeholders (e.g., individual workers or their representative unions). In this RE@Next paper, we propose a conceptual framework that guides software design from human monitoring-related use cases and requirements to informed decision-making guidance focusing on non-technical stakeholders. Building on principles such as Privacy by Design, the framework leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) to transform technical artifacts into accessible privacy reports. We share initial insights from two industry use cases, evaluate the quality of the generated reports, and outline future research directions toward integrating privacy transparency into RE processes for human-centric industrial systems.

</details>


### [72] [Beyond the Tip of the Iceberg: Understanding SATD in Dockerfiles through the Lens of Co-evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21238)
*Wei Minn,Yan Naing Tun,Biniam Fesseha Demissie,Rui'ang Hu,Jiakun Liu,Mariano Ceccato,Lwin Khin Shar,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究Dockerfile中的自我承认技术债务（SATD）与相关源代码的耦合演化关系，发现约27%的承认事件和40%的偿还事件与非Dockerfile工件耦合，且耦合SATD总体上偿还更快，但关于缺失功能的耦合SATD持续更久；外部依赖问题和架构重构分别是耦合SATD的主要触发和偿还原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅从Dockerfile单文件视角分析SATD，忽略了代码演化涉及生产、测试、构建等不同软件工件之间的变更耦合，导致对技术债务的理解不完整。

Method: 通过分析Dockerfile及相关源代码中的SATD事件，统计耦合与非耦合事件的比例及偿还时间差异，并采用开放编码和轴向编码对耦合SATD事件进行定性分类。

Result: 27%的SATD承认事件和40%的偿还事件与非Dockerfile工件耦合；耦合SATD整体偿还速度显著更快（p=0.0201），但关于缺失功能的耦合SATD持续更久；外部依赖问题（尤其是未发布的上游包和错误修复）是源代码侧最常见的SATD触发原因，架构重构是偿还的最常见前提。

Conclusion: 研究者和从业者应将源代码侧的协同演化（而非单文件视图）作为分析SATD的主要单元。

Abstract: Dockerfiles enable the creation of portable container-based execution environments for the application code, and have become an important part of the modern software development process. As Dockerfiles are a form of Infrastructure-as-Code (IaC), they can include temporary workarounds and other suboptimal implementations, leading to the accrual of technical debt that affects their reliability, security, and maintainability in the future. Prior work characterized self-admitted technical debt (SATD) in Dockerfile comments and the surrounding file chunks. This single-file view is incomplete since source code evolution involves changes across different types of software artifacts such as production, test, build, and other configuration files. Thus, we address this gap by studying SATD events in Dockerfiles alongside the related source code.
  We find that approximately 27% of admission events and 40% of repayment events are coupled to non-Dockerfile artifacts, and coupling sources are subtype-specific. We also observed that coupled SATD in general are repaid significantly faster overall (p = 0.0201), while coupled SATD regarding missing functionalities persists longer than its isolated counterparts; Lastly, we conducted open and axial coding of coupled SATD events, and we observe that external dependency issues, more particularly regarding unreleased upstream packages and bug fixes, are the most common cause of admission triggers in the source code; we also observe that architectural refactoring is the most common prerequisite for the repayment of SATD in Dockerfiles. These findings indicate that both practitioners (e.g. developers and project managers) and SATD researchers should integrate the source code-side co-evolution, rather than the single-file view, as the primary unit of analysis.

</details>


### [73] [RSE of a Quantum Transport Code and its Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21334)
*Christoph Conrads,Edoardo Di Napoli*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文总结了在量子输运代码libNEGF上两年来的RSE实践，包括代码质量保障（持续集成、自动化测试、编译器警告修正）和性能工程（持续基准测试）。系统性实践揭示了关键缺陷：未初始化内存读取、越界写入，以及边界条件处理中误解的数学模型。持续基准测试还暴露了HPC系统配置变化导致的性能回归。研究表明，危险缺陷（等价于C/C++未定义行为和Fortran处理器相关行为）在Fortran科学代码中同样普遍存在。尽管libNEGF使用Fortran实现，但大多数建议适用于任何语言的科学软件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究软件工程（RSE）实践在科学计算代码中的应用效果，特别是针对Fortran实现的大型量子输运代码libNEGF，探索系统化的质量保障和性能工程方法能否有效发现并修复缺陷。

Method: 在libNEGF上实施了为期两年的系统性RSE实践，包括：1) 代码质量保障：持续集成、自动化测试、编译器警告修正；2) 性能工程：持续基准测试。通过实际代码缺陷检测和性能回归分析来评估这些实践的有效性。

Result: 发现了多个关键缺陷：未初始化内存读取、越界写入，以及一个被误解的边界条件数学模型。持续基准测试揭示了HPC系统配置变化导致的性能回归。提供了数据证明危险缺陷（等价于未定义行为）在Fortran科学代码中与在其他语言中一样普遍。

Conclusion: 系统化的RSE实践对科学计算代码至关重要，能够有效发现传统方法难以检测的深层缺陷。建议科学软件项目（无论使用何种语言）选择性或全面地采用这些实践。

Abstract: This paper presents our research software engineering (RSE) experiences over two years with libNEGF, a quantum transport code. We describe practical approaches to code quality assurance--including continuous integration, automated testing, and compiler warning correction--and performance engineering through continuous benchmarking. Our systematic application of these practices revealed critical defects: uninitialized memory reads, out-of-bounds writes, and notably, a misunderstood mathematical model in our boundary condition handling. We also document how continuous benchmarking exposed performance regressions caused by HPC system configuration changes. Our findings provide data points suggesting that a dangerous class of defects--equivalent to undefined behavior in C/C++ and processor-dependent behavior in Fortran--is as prevalent in Fortran scientific codes as elsewhere. While libNEGF is implemented in Fortran, most recommendations are applicable to scientific software regardless of implementation language, and they can be implemented selectively or in their entirety for both new and existing projects.

</details>


### [74] [Validating Navmesh using Geometry: Voxel-Based Analysis with Prioritized Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21397)
*Ramesh Raghavan,Ojas Sharma,Sebastien Larrue,Alan Isaac Kunder,Aakash Sai,Rishi Mathur*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于几何驱动的导航网格验证框架，通过体素表示重建可通行空间，结合强化学习引导的优先搜索，检测导航网格与环境几何之间的不一致性。实验表明该方法在降低探索成本的同时保持相似的缺陷检测覆盖率，可集成到自动化质量保证流程中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航网格验证方法依赖于导航数据本身或间接评估导航行为，无法明确验证导航表示是否准确反映了底层几何定义的可行走空间。随着开发过程中环境不断变化，导航网格与环境几何之间容易出现不一致，影响NPC导航系统进而损害玩家体验。

Method: 该方法首先利用体素表示从环境几何中直接重建可行走空间，随后进行约束感知的遍历和连通性评估。验证过程被建模为体素空间上的优先搜索问题，通过强化学习引导采样向更可能存在不一致的区域聚焦。在每个采样位置，比较体素表示的可达性与通过引擎级查询从导航网格获得的可达性。

Result: 在多个大规模开放世界游戏环境中的实验表明，该方法在保持相似缺陷检测覆盖率的同时，持续降低了探索成本。该框架可在游戏引擎内离线运行，并能以最小改动适配不同游戏引擎。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种独立于导航数据的几何驱动验证方案，能够有效检测导航网格不一致问题，适用于生产环境部署，可集成到自动化质量保证管线中。

Abstract: Navigation mesh (Navmesh) inconsistencies affect the player experience by directly impacting the navigation systems used by non-playable characters (NPCs) in game environments. While navmeshes are generated from world geometry using well-established algorithms, environments change throughout development as terrain is adjusted and assets are moved or replaced, resulting in mismatches between the navmesh and the actual environment. Existing automated approaches attempt to detect navigation issues using exploration agents and reinforcement learning techniques. However, since these methods rely on the navigation data itself or evaluate navigation behavior indirectly, they do not explicitly verify whether the navigation representation reflects the walkable space defined by underlying geometry.
  This paper presents a framework for validating navigation meshes through an independent, geometry-driven analysis of navmesh correctness. The approach reconstructs walkable space directly from environment geometry using a voxel-based representation, followed by constraint-aware traversal and connectivity evaluation. Validation is formulated as a prioritized search problem over the voxel space, where reinforcement learning guides sampling toward regions more likely to exhibit inconsistencies. At each sampled location, reachability derived from the voxel representation is compared against reachability obtained from the navmesh via engine-level queries. Experiments across multiple large-scale open-world game environments show that the approach consistently lowers exploration effort while maintaining similar defect detection coverage. The framework runs offline within the game engine and can be integrated into automated quality assurance pipelines. Since the method relies on geometry, it can be adapted across game engines with minimal changes, making it suitable for production deployment.

</details>


### [75] [Agentic Model Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21434)
*Youcheng Sun,Jiawen Liu,Daniel Kroening,Jason Xue*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种"智能体模型检测"范式，将LLM智能体与有界模型检测后端相结合，遵循"智能体提议，求解器验证"的原则，用于验证LLM生成的系统代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证LLM生成的系统代码非常困难：bug普遍存在，缺乏形式化规约，安全契约隐式编码在调用点而非在函数边界上强制执行。

Method: 提出agentic model checking范式，LLM智能体处理需要语义判断的任务（规约推断、检查选择、反例分类、改进提议），BMC负责所有与可靠性相关的决策。规约从调用者上下文自顶向下推断，验证是组合式的，反例经过验证流水线处理。

Result: 在LLM生成的内核和编译器代码（C和Rust）以及经过OSS-Fuzz强化的成熟库上进行了评估，确认了真实缺陷，在高度模糊测试的代码上产生了有界完全验证，在选定的算法函数上建立了功能等价性。

Conclusion: Agentic model checking能有效验证LLM生成的系统代码，通过智能体与求解器的分工协作，在发现真实bug的同时实现对高模糊测试覆盖代码的有界验证。

Abstract: Verifying LLM-generated systems code is hard: bugs are prevalent, formal specifications are missing, and safety contracts are encoded implicitly at call sites rather than enforced at function boundaries. We propose agentic model checking, a paradigm that couples LLM agents with a bounded model checking backend under the principle agents propose, solvers verify: agents handle tasks requiring semantic judgment (spec inference, check selection, counterexample classification, refinement proposal) while BMC discharges every soundness-relevant decision. The paradigm rests on three commitments. Specifications are inferred top-down from caller context in a restricted DSL that translates deterministically into the backend's assume/assert primitives, with optional functional-correctness clauses lifting verification from panic-freeness to behavioural faithfulness. Verification is compositional: each function is checked in isolation against its spec with callees replaced by postcondition-constrained stubs, so per-query cost scales with a single function's state space and refinements propagate automatically to callers. Counterexamples are not bug reports: they pass through a validation pipeline (reachability, callee feasibility, dynamic replay, realism audit) that distinguishes active in-tree crashes from latent public-API failures, while modelling artifacts drive a refinement loop rather than being suppressed. We instantiate the approach in BMC-Agent and evaluate it on LLM-generated kernel and compiler code in C and Rust alongside mature OSS-Fuzz-hardened libraries, confirming real defects, producing bounded clean verifications on heavily-fuzzed surfaces, and establishing functional equivalence on selected algorithmic functions.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [76] [What Do Agents Communicate? Characterizing Information Exchange in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20548)
*Yong Jin Chun,Iftekhar Ahmed*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究了多智能体系统中信息传播对性能的影响，发现推理和验证信息的缺失会导致性能下降，并提出了类别感知恢复增强技术，可恢复高达86.2%的失败案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体协作系统虽然通过多轮推理和迭代优化提升了性能，但仍然容易受到错误传播的影响，即早期信息会损害下游推理过程。

Method: 首先对智能体间通信进行系统分析，识别影响多智能体性能的关键信息类型；然后提出类别感知恢复增强技术，强制在通信中包含关键信息。

Result: 该方法可恢复高达86.2%的失败案例。

Conclusion: 信息质量在多智能体协作中起着关键作用。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have enabled collaborative Multi-Agent (MA) systems, where interacting agents improve performance through diverse reasoning and iterative refinement. However, these systems remain vulnerable to error propagation, where early-stage information degrades downstream reasoning. To address this, we conduct a systematic analysis of inter-agent communication to identify which information drives MA performance. We find that the absence of reasoning and verification in inter-agent communication significantly degrades performance. Based on these insights, we propose Category-Aware Recovery Augmentation (technique), which enforces the presence of critical information during communication. recovers up to 86.2% of failed cases. Our results highlight the key role of information quality in effective MA collaboration. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/cara_mas

</details>


### [77] [Multi-agent Collaboration with State Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20563)
*Mengyang Liu,Taozhi Chen,Zhenhua Xu,Xue Jiang,Yihong Dong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: STORM提出了一种基于状态管理的多智能体协作框架，通过中介智能体与共享工作区的交互，在写入时检测和解决冲突，相比传统的Git工作区隔离方法，在Commit0和PaperBench上分别提升+18.7和+1.4的得分，并实现了更优的成本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体系统在处理共享代码库并发编辑时，依赖工作区隔离（如每个智能体一个Git工作树）来避免冲突，但这种方式将冲突解决推迟到事后合并步骤，导致恢复成本高昂且容易引发集成失败。

Method: STORM通过状态管理机制，在智能体与共享工作区交互时进行中介，确保每个智能体操作在一致的代码库视图上，并在写入时实时检测和解决冲突，而非事后合并。

Result: 在Commit0-Lite和PaperBench上分别比基于Git工作树的多智能体基线提升+18.7和+1.4；结合单智能体运行后，在两个基准上分别达到87.6和78.2的最高分，且成本效率相当或更优。

Conclusion: 显式状态管理比工作区隔离是多智能体协作更有效的基础，且STORM可以无缝集成到任何多智能体系统中。

Abstract: Recent advances in multi-agent systems have shown great potential for solving complex tasks. However, when multiple agents edit a shared codebase concurrently, their changes can silently conflict and inconsistent views lead to integration failures. Existing multi-agent systems address this through workspace isolation (e.g., one git worktree per agent), but this defers conflict resolution to a post-hoc merge step where recovery is expensive. In this paper, we propose STORM, i.e., STate-ORiented Management for multi-agent collaboration. Specifically, STORM manages agent states by mediating their interactions with the shared workspace, ensuring that each agent operates on a consistent view of the codebase and that conflicting edits are detected and resolved at write time. We evaluate STORM on Commit0 and PaperBench across multiple LLMs. STORM outperforms the git-worktree-based multi-agent baseline by +18.7 on Commit0-Lite and +1.4 on PaperBench, while achieving comparable or better cost efficiency. Combined with single-agent runs, STORM reaches highest scores of 87.6 and 78.2 on the two benchmarks respectively, suggesting that explicit state management is a more effective foundation for multi-agent collaboration than workspace isolation. STORM can also be plugged into any multi-agent system seamlessly.

</details>


### [78] [ProCrit: Self-Elicited Multi-Perspective Reasoning with Critic-Guided Revision for Multimodal Sarcasm Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20867)
*Yingjia Xu,Jiulong Wu,Bowen Zhang,Baokui Guo,Siyuan Chai,Min Cao*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出ProCrit框架，通过提案-批评双智能体实现自 elicitation 的多视角推理，用于多模态讽刺检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖固定预设视角和手工路由规则，无法适应讽刺机制的多样性，需要模型自主生成并整合多视角分析。

Method: ProCrit包含提案智能体（多视角推理）和批评智能体（外部评估与修订指导）。通过动态角色智能体 rollout 合成过程级推理标注，采用草稿-批评-修订范式提升推理可靠性，并通过双阶段强化学习联合优化。

Result: 在三个广泛使用的基准上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 自 elicitation 的多视角推理框架能有效提升多模态讽刺检测性能，批评机制和过程级标注合成是关键贡献。

Abstract: Multimodal sarcasm detection requires reasoning over cross-modal incongruities between literal expression and intended meaning, yet the specific analytical perspectives needed vary across samples due to the diversity of sarcastic mechanisms. While recent methods make this analytical process explicit, they still rely on fixed, predefined perspectives that operate independently under hand-crafted routing rules. We argue that multimodal sarcasm detection instead calls for self-elicited multi-perspective reasoning, where a model autonomously generates the perspectives needed for each sample and progressively integrates them into a coherent analysis. To realize this goal, we propose ProCrit, a Proposal-Critic two-agent framework with a proposal agent for multi-perspective reasoning and a critic agent for external evaluation and targeted revision guidance. First, to overcome the lack of process-level supervision in existing sarcasm datasets, ProCrit synthesizes process-level reasoning annotations through a dynamic-role agentic rollout: a strong vision-language model sequentially spawns analytical roles within a shared context, and the resulting multi-role trajectories are flattened into sequences that preserve cross-perspective dependencies while enabling efficient autoregressive generation. Second, to improve reasoning reliability, ProCrit adopts a draft-critique-revise paradigm in which an independent critic identifies reasoning deficiencies and provides targeted natural-language feedback for directed revision. Finally, we develop a mutual-refinement training framework that jointly optimizes proposal drafting and feedback-guided revision via dual-stage reinforcement learning, while refining the critic agent according to the actual effectiveness of its feedback. Experiments on three widely used benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of ProCrit.

</details>


### [79] [Decoupling Communication from Policy: Robust MARL under Bandwidth Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21085)
*Alexi Canesse,Benoît Goupil,Jesse Read,Sonia Vanier*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出SLIM架构，通过解耦通信路径与策略潜在表示，在带宽受限条件下实现多智能体强化学习的高效协调，并引入标准化带宽预算β来统一衡量通信约束，实验表明该方法在部分可观测MARL基准中达到SOTA性能，且带宽降低时性能仅轻微下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通信架构存在耦合瓶颈：共享潜在表示同时用于策略执行和智能体间通信，导致减小消息尺寸直接限制策略的潜在空间，造成显著性能下降。需要解耦通信与策略表示，并建立统一的带宽约束度量标准。

Method: 1) 引入归一化每智能体带宽预算β，统一稀疏性、轮次和消息维度为单一可比约束；2) 提出SLIM最小架构，解耦通信通路与策略潜在表示，在隔离带宽影响与策略容量的同时实现步内通信。

Result: 在多个部分可观测MARL基准（通信至关重要）上达到SOTA性能，展现出在有限通信下的可扩展性和鲁棒性，带宽降低时性能仅边际下降。

Conclusion: 解耦通信路径与策略表示是应对带宽约束的有效设计原则，SLIM架构能在极低带宽下保持良好性能，为真实世界带宽受限的MARL应用（如无人机群搜救）提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Communication enables coordination in multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), but many real-world applications, e.g., search-and-rescue with drone swarms, operate under severe bandwidth constraints. Many communication architectures still expose a coupled bottleneck in which a shared latent representation is used for both policy execution and inter-agent communication. Consequently, reducing message size directly limits the policy's latent space, often leading to significant performance degradation. We address this with two contributions. First, we introduce $β$, a normalised per-agent bandwidth budget that unifies sparsity, rounds, and message dimension into a single comparable constraint. Second, we provide SLIM, a minimal architecture that decouples the communication pathway from the policy's latent representation, allowing us to isolate the effect of bandwidth from the effect of policy capacity while benefiting from in-step communication. We evaluate our method on several partially-observable MARL benchmarks, where communication is essential. Our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance and exhibits scalability and robustness under limited communication, with only marginal degradation as bandwidth is reduced.

</details>

### [80] [Multicategorical Semantics for Untyped Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21337)
*Ariel Grunfeld,Liron Cohen*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文针对无类型效应性按值调用语言中计算替换的语义问题，提出了一种基于Freyd操作语（Freyd operad）和Freyd PROP的范畴语义框架，将计算替换表示为有限顺序列表，并通过弱封闭Freyd操作语解释无类型计算λ演算，证明了合理性、初始性和完备性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无类型效应性按值调用语言的范畴语义中，传统通过构造语法范畴进行完备性证明时遇到根本性障碍：由于求值顺序在语义上有意义，不存在规范的计算同时替换概念。这阻碍了对此类语言的范畴语义完备性证明。

Method: 1) 以单计算替换（绑定步）为原语，用有限顺序列表（通过连接组合）表示计算替换；2) 建立单对象Freyd多范畴（Freyd-multicategory）设置；3) 引入Freyd操作语，分离值的笛卡尔操作语和计算的对称Ren-笛卡尔前操作语，通过Freyd函子连接；4) 从Freyd操作语构造相应的Freyd PROP（替换结构）；5) 证明该构造是可表示的，且在严格单对象设置中是限制到陪域1的左伴随；6) 利用诱导项模型，在弱封闭Freyd操作语中解释无类型计算λ演算（含过程和更高阶函数）。

Result: 建立了Freyd操作语到Freyd PROP的表示性构造，证明了严格单对象情况下的左伴随性，在弱封闭Freyd操作语中给出了无类型计算λ演算的语义解释，并证明了合理性、初始性和完备性。该语义框架足够广泛，能够涵盖如单子组合代数等可实现性导向的模型。

Conclusion: 提出的Freyd操作语框架成功解决了无类型效应性按值调用语言中计算替换的范畴语义完备性问题，为无类型效应计算提供了一种定制的范畴语义，拓展了可实现性模型的适用范围。

Abstract: Completeness proofs in categorical semantics usually proceed by building a syntactic category whose composition is given by substitution. For untyped effectful Call-by-Value languages, this runs into a basic obstacle: there is no canonical notion of simultaneous substitution of computations, since evaluation order is semantically meaningful. We address this by taking single computation substitutions, that is, binding steps, as primitive, and representing computation substitution by finite sequential lists composed by concatenation. We formalize this idea in a one-object Freyd-multicategorical setting. We introduce Freyd operads, separating a cartesian operad of values from a symmetric Ren-cartesian preoperad of computations, connected by a Freyd functor, and from any Freyd operad we construct a corresponding Freyd PROP of substitutions. We prove that this construction is representable and, in the strict one-object setting, left adjoint to restriction to codomain 1. Using the induced term model, we interpret untyped computational lambda-calculus with procedures and higher-order functions in weakly closed Freyd operads, and prove soundness, initiality, and completeness. This yields a categorical semantics tailored to untyped effectful computation and broad enough to encompass realizability-oriented models such as monadic combinatory algebras.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [81] [Classification of Single and Mixed Partial Discharges under Switching Voltage Using an AWA-CNN Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21352)
*Md Rafid Kaysar Shagor,Zannatul Ferdousy Mouri,Farhina Haque,Anindya Bijoy Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于开关电压激励下局部放电(PD)源分类的幅值-宽度-面积(AWA)模式表示方法。该方法将时域PD脉冲通过幅值、宽度和面积三个特征进行表征，并映射为视觉模式（幅值和面积定义坐标轴，宽度编码为颜色）。基于AWA模式，可区分电晕、内部、表面、电晕+内部、电晕+表面、内部+表面六种单一及混合PD源。使用InceptionV3和ResNet-18两种CNN模型进行分类，测试准确率超过96%，显著优于随机森林的73.33%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开关电压激励下的PD分析因活动集中在电压转换瞬间而比正弦条件下更具挑战性，现有方法难以有效区分多类PD源，亟需一种适用于该场景的源导向PD分析表征方法。

Method: 提出AWA模式表示法：提取PD脉冲的幅值、宽度和面积三个时域特征，以幅值和面积为坐标轴、宽度为颜色编码构建视觉模式；采用Random Forest、InceptionV3和ResNet-18进行六类PD源分类实验。

Result: AWA模式显示出可区分的源依赖分布；CNN模型（InceptionV3和ResNet-18）测试准确率均超过96%，而Random Forest仅为73.33%。

Conclusion: AWA模式提供了一种适用于开关电压激励下多类PD源分类的视觉表示方法，结合深度学习可实现高精度分类。

Abstract: The growing use of fast-switching power electronics has made partial discharge (PD) analysis under switching-voltage excitation increasingly important, yet more challenging than under sinusoidal conditions due to activity concentrated at voltage transitions. This work presents an Amplitude-Width-Area (AWA) pattern representation for source-oriented PD analysis under switching-voltage excitation. In the proposed method, time domain PD pulses are characterized using pulse amplitude, width, and area, and mapped into a visual pattern where amplitude and area define the coordinate axes and width is encoded by color. The generated AWA patterns are used to distinguish six single and mixed PD source conditions: corona, internal, surface, corona+internal, corona+surface, and internal+surface. To evaluate the classification capability of the proposed representation, a Random Forest baseline and two Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) models, InceptionV3 and ResNet-18, are compared. The AWA patterns show distinguishable source-dependent distributions, and CNN-based classification achieves testing accuracy above 96%, compared with 73.33% for Random Forest. The results indicate that AWA patterns provide a visual representation of PD pulses suitable for multi-class PD source classification under switching-voltage excitation.

</details>


### [82] [Dynamic Shapley Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20620)
*Xuan Yang,Hsi-Wen Chen,Ming-Syan Chen,Jian Pei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出动态Shapley值估值框架D-Shap，将数据估值建模为结构化矩阵维护问题，利用任务-玩家的局部性实现高效增量更新，在毫秒级完成任务更新并将玩家更新成本降低三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Shapley数据估值方法计算成本高且为一次性过程，在任务和数据参与者动态变化的场景下需要完全重新计算，缺乏可重用性。

Method: 将Shapley值表示为玩家×任务矩阵，利用效用局部性和联盟局部性（任务仅依赖少量训练玩家、相似任务产生相似估值）进行结构化维护；新任务通过结构感知插值推断，新玩家仅修改局部矩阵块；提出自估值机制通过可扩展子集复用和覆盖感知锚点选择直接从训练数据构建初始矩阵。

Result: 任务更新在毫秒级完成，玩家更新成本降低最多三个数量级，估值质量与完全重新计算相当。

Conclusion: D-Shap通过利用任务-玩家关系的局部性结构，实现了动态环境下数据估值的高效增量更新，克服了传统Shapley方法计算昂贵且不可重用的根本限制。

Abstract: Shapley-based data valuation provides a principled way to quantify the contribution of training data, but its high computational cost makes it impractical in dynamic settings where tasks and training players evolve. Existing methods treat Shapley computation as a one-shot process and collapse contributions into aggregated scores, preventing reuse and requiring recomputation under any change. We introduce a new perspective that represents Shapley values as a player-by-task matrix and formulates dynamic valuation as a structured matrix maintenance problem. We exploit the fact that each task depends on a small subset of training players and that similar tasks yield similar valuations, leading to utility locality and coalition locality. Based on these insights, we propose D-Shap, a dynamic valuation framework that enables efficient updates by modifying only a small portion of the matrix: new task valuations are inferred via structure-aware interpolation, while updates induced by new players are confined to affected local matrix blocks. To eliminate the need for pre-specified evaluation tasks, we introduce self-valuation, which constructs the initial matrix directly from training data, supported by scalable subset reuse and coverage-aware anchor selection. Experiments across diverse models show that D-Shap performs task updates in milliseconds and reduces the cost of player updates by up to three orders of magnitude, while achieving valuation quality competitive with full recomputation.

</details>


### [83] [GraphDiffMed: Knowledge-Constrained Differential Attention with Pharmacological Graph Priors for Medication Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20188)
*Krati Saxena,Tomohiro Shibata*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GraphDiffMed 提出了一种基于双尺度差分注意力 v2 的用药推荐框架，在就诊内和就诊间两个层面过滤噪声，并结合药理约束（如药物相互作用），在 MIMIC-III 上实现了更优的推荐质量和安全性能平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以同时处理好时序建模（跨就诊）和药理知识整合（如药物相互作用），且患者轨迹长、噪声大、临床异质性强，导致推荐质量与安全性难以兼得。

Method: 提出 GraphDiffMed，核心是双尺度差分注意力 v2：在 intra-visit（就诊内）和 inter-visit（就诊间）两个层面应用差分注意力来过滤虚假信号；同时将药理约束（如 DDI）作为学习目标的一部分进行联合优化。

Result: 在 MIMIC-III 上的实验和消融研究表明，GraphDiffMed 在推荐质量和排序指标上持续优于强基线，并实现了更有利的安全性能平衡。最强配置仅使用人口统计学辅助特征。

Conclusion: 噪声感知的注意力机制与药理约束相结合能够产生更可靠、更具临床意义的用药推荐。

Abstract: Recommending safe and effective medication combinations from electronic health records (EHRs) is a core clinical AI problem, yet it remains difficult because patient trajectories are long, noisy, and clinically heterogeneous. Existing methods typically excel at either temporal modeling across visits or pharmacological knowledge integration (e.g., drug-drug interactions, DDIs), but rarely achieve both while robustly suppressing noise. We present GraphDiffMed, a knowledge-constrained medication recommendation framework built on dual-scale Differential Attention v2. Differential attention is applied at both intra-visit and inter-visit levels to filter spurious signals within encounters and across longitudinal history, while pharmacological constraints are incorporated during learning. Experiments on MIMIC-III and ablation studies show that this design consistently improves recommendation quality and ranking over strong baselines while achieving a more favorable safety performance balance. We further find that the strongest-performing configuration uses only demographic auxiliary features under our experimental setting. Overall, GraphDiffMed demonstrates that combining noise-aware attention with pharmacological constraints yields more reliable and clinically meaningful medication recommendation. We open-source our code at https://github.com/saxenakrati09/GraphDiffMed.

</details>


### [84] [Runtime-Certified Bounded-Error Quantized Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20868)
*Dean Calver*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种层级式KV缓存架构，通过对INT8键和INT4值进行运行时认证，结合FP16原始备份的确定性回退机制，实现注意力计算的可验证近似。系统利用每头每步的误差界驱动自适应精度选择和分级回退，在长上下文推理中匹配FP16质量，并恢复朴素量化方法中的灾难性失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存量化仅凭经验验证平均鲁棒性，缺乏运行时检测和恢复机制，无法保证在严格质量约束下安全部署激进压缩策略。

Method: 提出层级式KV缓存架构：GPU存储INT8键和INT4值，系统RAM保留FP16原始备份；设计两项误差分解（注意力分布畸变界和值重建误差界），在线计算并驱动自适应精度选择和分级回退阶梯，确保必要时可恢复至精确密集注意力输出。

Result: 在PG-19、NIAH、RULER基准测试中，系统在LLaMA 3.1-8B模型上支持128K上下文，匹配FP16质量；恢复朴素INT8/INT4基线中的灾难性失败；短上下文中价值敏感任务可调控压缩-保真度权衡。

Conclusion: 将KV缓存量化从固定近似重构为运行时验证的计算，提供每头每步的局部认证而非端到端保证，旨在在严格质量约束下实现安全的激进KV压缩部署。

Abstract: KV cache quantization reduces the memory cost of long-context LLM inference, but introduces approximation error that is typically validated only empirically. Existing systems rely on average-case robustness, with no mechanism to detect or recover from failures at runtime. We present a tiered KV cache architecture that enables runtime-certified attention: INT8 keys and INT4 values are stored in GPU memory, while FP16 originals are retained in system RAM for deterministic fallback. A two-term error decomposition yields per-head, per-step bounds on (i) attention distribution distortion from key quantization and (ii) value reconstruction error. These bounds are computed online and used to drive adaptive precision selection and a multi-stage fallback ladder, which guarantees recovery to the exact dense attention output when required. Across PG-19, NIAH, and RULER benchmarks on LLaMA~3.1-8B with contexts up to 128K, the system matches dense FP16 KV quality within noise for language modelling and retrieval tasks, while recovering catastrophic failures observed in naive INT8/INT4 baselines. Value-sensitive tasks at short context expose a controlled trade-off between compression and fidelity, which can be eliminated via tighter value tolerances or FP16-value fallback. The certification is local (per-head, per-step) and does not guarantee end-to-end model correctness, but ensures that each attention computation is either bounded relative to an FP16 reference or exactly recovered via fallback. This reframes KV cache quantization as a runtime-verified computation rather than a fixed approximation. The goal is not raw speedups, but enabling safe deployment of aggressive KV compression under strict quality constraints.

</details>


### [85] [TabPFN-MT: A Natively Multitask In-Context Learner for Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20234)
*Cormac Cureton,Narges Armanfard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabPFN-MT扩展了Prior-Data Fitted Networks，通过多目标合成先验训练和共享解码器结构，实现多任务表格数据的上下文学习与并行推理，在少于1000样本的小到中型数据集上达到新SOTA，并将推理成本从O(T)降至O(1)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PFN仅支持单任务推理，预测多个目标值时需重复前向传播，无法在上下文中实现任务间信息共享，推理效率低下。

Method: 提出TabPFN-MT，采用扩展的y编码器和共享解码器头，在扩展的多目标合成先验上训练，实现多任务上下文学习与同时推理。

Result: 在344个数据集上（平均少于1000样本），TabPFN-MT在深度表格多任务学习中达到新SOTA，多任务数据集上整体Accuracy排名为4.89（所有模型中最高平均排名），且推理成本从O(T)降至O(1)。

Conclusion: TabPFN-MT证明了通过上下文学习而非梯度训练，可以在小到中型多任务表格数据上实现高效、高性能的多目标预测，大幅降低计算开销。

Abstract: Prior-Data Fitted networks (PFNs) have been very successful in tabular contexts, handling prediction tasks in context. However, they are designed for single-task inference, meaning that predicting several target values within a context requires repeated forward calls and precludes inter-task information sharing. We propose TabPFN-MT, which is trained on an expanded multi-target synthetic prior to capture inter-task dependencies in context. This model uses an expanded $y$-encoder and a shared decoder head to enable multitask in-context learning and simultaneous inference. The model is uniquely specialized for small-to-medium datasets by relying on in-context learning rather than traditional gradient-based training. Within this regime (averaging fewer than 1,000 samples), extensive evaluations across 344 datasets demonstrate that TabPFN-MT establishes a new state-of-the-art for deep tabular multitask learning. Furthermore, despite the inherent compute asymmetry of joint optimization, our model remains highly competitive with the latest state-of-the-art single-task ensembles. Notably, on multitask datasets it achieves an overall Accuracy rank of 4.89, the highest average rank among all models tested. Crucially, TabPFN-MT delivers this highly competitive performance while reducing the inference cost for $T$ tasks from $O(T)$ to $O(1)$ forward passes, offering a massive computational efficiency improvement for multi-target tabular applications.

</details>


### [86] [Provably Learning Diffusion Models under the Manifold Hypothesis: Collapse and Refine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20235)
*Wei Huang,Andi Han,Mingyuan Bai,Huanjian Zhou,Qixin Zhang,Taiji Suzuki,Kenji Fukumizu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了扩散模型训练中通过“坍缩-精炼”机制实现高维数据高效学习的原理，并提出了基于分数诱导的潜在扩散模型（SiLD），证明其样本复杂度取决于内在维度而非环境维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽能生成高质量高维数据，但其训练如何有效学习分数函数、在数据支撑于低维流形时绕过维度灾难的问题，在理论上仍未得到解释。

Method: 识别出由分数函数几何性质驱动的“坍缩-精炼”机制：小噪声尺度下分数函数的发散奇异性导致去噪映射快速维度坍缩到数据流形投影上；中等噪声尺度下训练精炼学习到的流形内在密度。基于此提出SiLD两阶段框架，通过单一去噪分数匹配目标同时实现流形学习和密度估计，替代VAE潜在扩散模型中启发式的KL正则化。

Result: 证明了样本复杂度取决于内在维度而非环境维度。在Stacked MNIST、CelebA变体和分子生成基准上的实验表明，SiLD在生成质量上匹配或优于基于VAE的LDM，并持续改进重建效果，验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: SiLD框架通过理论驱动的分数函数几何机制，实现了无需启发式正则化的高效潜在扩散建模，为高维数据生成提供了理论保障与实用验证。

Abstract: Diffusion models generate high-dimensional data with remarkable quality, yet how their training efficiently learns the score function, bypassing the curse of dimensionality when data is supported on low-dimensional manifolds, remains theoretically unexplained. We identify a collapse-and-refine mechanism driven by the geometry of the score function itself: at small noise scales, the diverging singularity of the score drives a rapid dimensional collapse of the induced denoising map onto the data manifold projection; at moderate noise scales, training refines the intrinsic density on the learned manifold. We instantiate this principle as Score-induced Latent Diffusion (SiLD), a two-stage framework in which both manifold learning and density estimation emerge from a single denoising score matching objective, replacing the heuristic KL regularization of VAE-based latent diffusion models. We prove that the resulting sample complexity depends on the intrinsic dimension rather than the ambient dimension. Experiments on Stacked MNIST, CelebA variants, and molecular generation benchmarks show that SiLD matches or outperforms VAE-based LDMs in generation quality and consistently improves reconstruction, validating our theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [87] [MagBridge-Battery: A Synthetic Bridge Dataset for Li-ion Magnetometry and State-of-Health Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20240)
*Sakthi Prabhu Gunasekar,Prasanna Kumar Rangarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发布 MagBridge-Battery v1.0 数据集，包含 6,760 个磁场信号样本，通过将真实磁场形态与电池健康状态标签桥接，为基于磁传感的电池诊断提供首个公开基准，涵盖回归、分类和异常检测三大任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前电池健康诊断主要依赖端子电化学信号，磁传感虽能补充遗漏信息，但缺乏公开的电池磁场测量数据集及其对应的退化标签，限制了该方法的发展。

Method: 通过桥接 Mohammadi-Jerschow OSF 档案中的真实磁场形态与 PulseBat 数据集的 SOH 标签，生成合成数据集。包含 5,600 个 PulseBat 条件样本、600 个合成传感器异常样本和 560 个低电压外推样本。采用无细胞重叠、无跨分割亲子对、无样本 ID 重叠的主基准分割，并设计了标签洗牌消融实验验证桥接有效性。

Result: SOH 回归任务 R² 约 0.77，标签洗牌消融后 R² 降至约 0，证实桥接编码了输入 SOH 的非平凡信息而非标签对齐伪影。数据集以 CC-BY-4.0 发布，代码和基准套件以 Apache-2.0 发布。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了磁传感电池诊断的公共基准，弥补了配对磁-电化学测量数据稀缺的现状，为后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Battery health diagnostics today rely overwhelmingly on electrochemical signals measured at the cell terminals. A parallel literature has shown that magnetic sensing can resolve information that terminal-only measurements miss, but method development is limited by the absence, to the best of our knowledge, of public battery magnetic-measurement datasets paired with degradation labels. We release MagBridge-Battery v1.0, a synthetic dataset of 6,760 magnetic-field signatures that bridges real magnetic morphology from the Mohammadi-Jerschow Open Science Framework (OSF) archive with state-of-health (SOH) labels from the PulseBat dataset. The release contains 5,600 PulseBat-conditioned grounded samples, 600 synthetic sensor-anomaly samples derived from clean parents, and 560 low-voltage Regime-B extrapolation samples. A cell-disjoint, parent-child-leakage-free primary benchmark split is verified to contain zero overlapping cells, zero cross-split parent-child pairs, and zero sample-ID overlap. We define three primary benchmark tasks: SOH regression, second-life classification, and anomaly detection, plus an auxiliary anomaly-subtype classification task. A controlled label-shuffle ablation collapses SOH regression from R^2 approximately 0.77 to approximately 0, confirming that the bridge encodes input SOH non-trivially rather than producing label-aligned artifacts. The dataset is released on Zenodo under CC-BY-4.0, and the bridge code and benchmark suite are released under Apache-2.0. This work provides a public benchmark for magnetic-sensing battery diagnostics while paired magnetic-electrochemical measurements remain scarce.

</details>


### [88] [Geometry-Lite: Interpretable Safety Probing via Layer-Wise Margin Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20241)
*Woo Seob Sim,Yu Rang Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Geometry-Lite，一种紧凑的提示级安全探针，通过分析多层隐藏状态的边界几何特征来检测大语言模型中的不安全提示。研究发现安全证据主要表现为持久性的边界位置几何（最终或极值边界及不安全侧的层占用），而非层间运动信号，且在基准偏移下类条件均值几何比优化线性边界更具鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示级安全探针在平均检测性能上表现良好，但其几何分离机制尚不明确：安全证据如何在层间形成、哪些几何特征支撑低假阳性决策、以及基准偏移下存在何种几何偏差。作者希望将这些几何因素进行实证分解。

Method: 提出Geometry-Lite框架：提取每一层最终提示token的隐藏表示，在质心、局部邻域和监督线性边界三种读出器下计算带符号边界（signed margins），然后通过边界位置、层间变化和粗粒度形状三个维度汇总得到边界剖面（margin profiles）。在9个指令微调主干模型（1.2B-70B）和7个安全基准上进行评估。

Result: Geometry-Lite性能优于单层探针，接近原始多层得分堆叠。分解显示：最终或极值边界以及不安全侧层占用是主要检测信号；有限差分漂移和结构汇总对聚合AUROC贡献很小，但漂移可在低FPR阈值下提供小幅召回提升；基准偏移下，优化线性边界在训练混合上尖锐，而类条件均值几何在困难保留子集上更可靠。

Conclusion: 提示级安全证据主要不是层间运动信号，而是持久的层间边界几何特征。其有用组件和读出级偏差在决策关键区域（如低假阳性率阈值）变得可见。

Abstract: Prompt-level safety probes for large language models use hidden-state representations to separate safe from unsafe prompts, but strong average detection performance does not explain the geometry of this separation. In particular, it remains unclear how safety evidence is formed across layers, which aspects of that layer-wise geometry support low-false-positive decisions, and which geometric biases remain stable under benchmark shift. We study this as an empirical decomposition problem and introduce Geometry-Lite, a compact prompt-level probe that maps each layer's final prompt-token representation to signed margins under centroid, local-neighborhood, and supervised linear-boundary readouts, then summarizes the resulting margin profiles by boundary position, layer-to-layer change, and coarse shape. Across nine instruction-tuned backbones ($1.2$B--$70$B) and seven safety benchmarks, Geometry-Lite improves over single-layer probes while remaining close to raw multi-layer score stacking, making it a useful instrument for analyzing the multi-layer safety signal. The decomposition shows that safety evidence is expressed primarily through persistent boundary-position geometry: final or extremal margins and unsafe-side layer occupancy dominate aggregate detection performance. In contrast, finite-difference drift and structural summaries add little to pooled AUROC, although drift can provide small recall-oriented corrections under shifted low-FPR thresholds. Under benchmark shift, optimized linear boundaries are sharp on the training mixture, whereas class-conditional mean geometry retains separation more reliably on a predefined hard held-out subset. Overall, prompt-level safety evidence is not primarily a layer-to-layer motion signal, but a persistent layer-wise margin geometry whose useful components and readout-level biases become visible in decision-critical regimes.

</details>


### [89] [Weight Decay Regimes in Grokking Transformers: Cheap Online Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20441)
*Lucky Verma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Transformer在模算术任务上的训练动力学，发现权重衰减作为标量控制参数调控记忆、泛化与崩溃三个相变区域，并提出了两种低成本在线诊断指标（注意力头余弦相似度均值与熵标准差）。实验覆盖11种条件、三个模型规模（0.82M-85M参数），确定了相变临界点λ_c=0.0158，经验幂律指数ν=0.757，并通过多任务复制、注意力头重初始化及三种跨架构探针验证了结论的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示Transformer在模算术任务中从记忆到泛化再到崩溃的相变机制，寻找控制这些区域的关键参数，并开发低成本、可在线监测的训练动力学诊断工具，以替代计算成本较高的损失景观分析。

Method: 使用权重衰减作为标量控制参数，提出注意力头间余弦相似度均值和熵标准差两种在线诊断指标；通过11种实验条件、三种模型规模（0.82M-85M参数）进行系统实验；采用Logistic拟合定位相变边界，幂律拟合计算经验指数；进行多任务复制（n=280）、注意力头重初始化（n=10）和跨架构探针（MLP/LSTM/Mamba，各n=70）验证。

Result: 确定了记忆-发展性grokking边界在λ_c=0.0158（95% CI [0.0109, 0.0200], N=210）；经验幂律指数ν=0.757（CI [0.725, 0.799]），该值偏离参考指数ν=1/2和3D Ising ν≈0.63；多任务复制保持权重衰减控制模式；注意力头重初始化改变Phase-2振幅（Cohen's d=-1.190, p_t=4.5×10^{-3}），而权重范数裁剪无此效果；三种跨架构探针均复现了权重衰减控制的相变但具有架构特定的λ_c值。

Conclusion: 权重衰减是Transformer在模算术任务中调控记忆-泛化-崩溃相变的有效标量控制参数；所提在线诊断指标可低成本监测训练动力学；相变具有普适性但临界点与架构相关；经验指数ν=0.757的普适类归属需更密集的有限尺度标度研究；结论范围限于小Transformer注意力模型模算术任务，非注意力架构实验仅为范围探针。

Abstract: Transformers trained on modular arithmetic exhibit sharp transitions between memorization, generalization, and collapse. We show that weight decay acts as a scalar empirical control parameter for these regimes, and introduce two cheap online diagnostics, mean pairwise attention-head cosine similarity and entropy standard deviation, that track training dynamics from attention activations alone and complement loss-landscape diagnostics at lower compute cost. Across eleven experimental conditions and three model scales (0.82M to 85M parameters), the weight-decay axis separates memorization, developmental grokking, and collapse. A near-transition logistic fit localizes the memorization-to-developmental boundary at $λ_c=0.0158$ (95% CI [0.0109, 0.0200], N=210); a power-law fit gives an empirical exponent $ν=0.757$ (CI [0.725, 0.799]). Reference exponents $ν=1/2$ and 3D Ising $ν\approx 0.63$ lie outside this empirical CI under our four-bin grid, so we report $ν$ as empirical and defer universality-class identification to denser finite-size-scaling work. A horizon-matched multi-task replication (n=280, four modular operations) preserves the weight-decay control pattern; a paired attention-head re-initialization experiment at $λ=0.05$ changes Phase-2 amplitude (Cohen's $d=-1.190$, n=10, $p_t=4.5 \times 10^{-3}$), while matched weight-norm clipping does not. Three cross-architecture probes (4L MLP, 4L LSTM, and 4L Mamba; each n=70) replicate the weight-decay-controlled transition with architecture-specific $λ_c$ values. Main diagnostic claims are scoped to modular arithmetic in small transformer attention models; the non-attention experiments are scope probes, and architecture-wide, language-model, and universality-class claims are out of scope.

</details>


### [90] [LEAP: A closed-loop framework for perovskite precursor additive discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20242)
*Xin-De Wang,Zhi-Rui Chen,Ze-Feng Gao,Peng-Jie Guo,Cheng Mu,Zhong-Yi Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LEAP框架，将领域专用大语言模型与主动学习结合，通过提取钙钛矿添加剂文献中的机制知识并转化为可解释描述符，再集成贝叶斯优化进行不确定性感知的添加剂优先级排序。实验验证表明该方法可在低数据条件下实现机制感知的添加剂筛选，将器件PCE从19.25%提升至20.87%，冠军效率达21.32%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钙钛矿太阳能电池前驱体添加剂的发现依赖试错法效率低下，化学空间巨大导致传统高通量筛选成本过高，迫切需要一种能够利用文献知识进行机制感知、低数据条件下高效筛选的计算方法。

Method: LEAP框架包含三个核心组件：1) 领域专用LLM，经钙钛矿添加剂文献微调，可提取机制相关知识并将候选分子编码为可解释描述符；2) 贝叶斯优化工作流，基于这些描述符进行不确定性感知的优先级排序；3) 专家介入的闭环验证流程，结合实验反馈迭代优化筛选结果。

Result: 领域专用模型在未见文献的基准测试中机制推理一致性优于通用模型。三轮专家参与的概念验证实验中，6-CDQ和2-CNA处理组平均PCE分别达20.13%和20.87%，高于对照组的19.25%，冠军器件PCE达21.32%。

Conclusion: 文献驱动的机制描述符结合贝叶斯优化与专家可行性评估，可支持钙钛矿光伏中机制感知的添加剂优先级排序，为低数据条件下的高效材料发现提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Efficient discovery of precursor additives is essential for improving the performance of perovskite solar cells, yet the large chemical space makes conventional trial-and-error screening inefficient. We develop LEAP(LLM-driven Exploration via Active Learning for Perovskites), an expert-in-the-loop closed framework that couples a domain-specialized large language model(LLM) with active learning for iterative additive prioritization. The LLM is trained to extract mechanism-relevant knowledge from the perovskite additive literature and to represent candidate molecules through interpretable descriptors, which are further integrated into a Bayesian optimization workflow for uncertainty-aware prioritization under low-data conditions. Benchmark results on unseen literature show that the domain-specialized model outperforms general-purpose models in mechanism-consistent reasoning. Experimental validation in an expert-in-the-loop proof-of-concept study suggests improved additive prioritization across three screening rounds, leading to average device PCEs of 20.13% and 20.87% for the later-round 6-CDQ- and 2-CNA-treated devices, respectively, compared with 19.25% for the control, with a champion PCE of 21.32%. These results provide preliminary evidence that literature-grounded mechanistic descriptors, when coupled with Bayesian optimization and expert feasibility review, can support mechanism-aware additive prioritization in perovskite photovoltaics.

</details>


### [91] [GROW: Aligning GRPO with State-Action Modeling for Open-World VLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20246)
*Xiongbin Wu,Zhihao Luo,Shanzhe Lei,Lechao Zhang,Xuhong Wang,Jie Yang,Zhonglong Zheng,Yuanjie Zheng,Xin Tan,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GROW提出了一种针对开放世界VLM智能体的强化学习框架，通过将完整轨迹分解为状态-动作样本并在样本间计算优势，解决了标准GRPO因长轨迹导致上下文过长和噪声的问题，在800+ Minecraft任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM智能体主要依赖监督微调（SFT）使用专家演示，而先进的强化学习算法GRPO因需要完整轨迹作为训练样本，导致上下文过长和噪声问题，无法有效用于多轮强化学习。

Method: 提出GROW框架，将收集的轨迹分解为状态-动作样本，并在样本间计算优势而非将完整轨迹视为单一实体；提供了替代分析表明，即使分组样本基于不同局部状态而非相同提示上下文，该目标在简化假设下仍能保留GRPO的核心相对策略优化信号。

Result: 在800+ Minecraft任务上的实验表明，GROW达到了最先进（SOTA）性能。

Conclusion: 提出的GROW强化学习框架能有效用于开放世界VLM智能体，通过轨迹分解和样本间优势计算解决了标准GRPO的局限性。

Abstract: Recently, vision-language model (VLM) agents have shown promising progress in open-world tasks, where successful task completion often requires multiple turns of visual perception and action execution. However, existing methods still rely primarily on Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) with expert demonstrations, while the advanced reinforcement learning (RL) algorithm, specifically Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), has not been effectively employed for multi-turn RL in these tasks because standard GRPO requires full trajectories as training samples which leads to excessively long context and noise. To address this issue, we propose GROW, a RL framework for open-world VLM agents that decomposes collected trajectories into state-action samples, and computes advantages between these samples rather than treating a full trajectory as a single entity. We further provide a surrogate analysis indicating that, even though the grouped samples are conditioned on different local states rather than an identical prompt context, the objective can preserve the core relative policy optimization signal of GRPO under simplifying assumptions. Experiments on more than 800 Minecraft tasks show that our method achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance, demonstrating the effectiveness of our proposed RL framework for open-world VLM agents.

</details>


### [92] [CP-MoE: Consistency-Preserving Mixture-of-Experts for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20247)
*Yang Liu,Toan Nguyen,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CP-MoE是一个面向大规模语言模型和视觉-语言模型的持续学习框架，通过引入"瞬态专家"机制，在MoE架构中平衡任务间知识迁移与灾难性遗忘。该框架包含一致性保持路由偏置和瞬态专家引导正则化两个核心组件，在SuperNI和VQA v2基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LoRA的MoE持续学习方法存在根本性权衡：要么过度隔离专家，限制任务间知识迁移；要么允许任务特定更新覆盖重要参数，导致严重遗忘。亟需一种能同时兼顾知识迁移与遗忘抑制的方法。

Method: 提出CP-MoE框架，核心是"瞬态专家"机制：(1) 一致性保持路由偏置——利用瞬态专家估计与稳定专家的表示相似性，引导路由选择更兼容的专家；(2) 瞬态专家引导正则化——在合并过程中选择性保护重要的历史参数。

Result: 在SuperNI基准上实现最先进性能，对未见任务展现出更强的零样本迁移能力；在VQA v2数据集上有效扩展到多模态视觉推理，持续降低遗忘，优于强MoE基线。

Conclusion: CP-MoE通过瞬态专家机制有效解决了MoE持续学习中知识迁移与遗忘抑制的权衡问题，在单模态和多模态持续学习任务上均验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting remains a major obstacle to continual learning in large language models (LLMs) and vision--language models (VLMs). Although Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures offer an efficient path to scaling, existing LoRA-based MoE continual learning methods still face a fundamental trade-off: they either isolate experts too aggressively, limiting knowledge transfer across tasks, or allow task-specific updates to overwrite important existing parameters, leading to severe forgetting. To address this, we propose CP-MoE, a continual learning framework built around a transient expert that captures early task-specific updates and guides their integration into stable experts. CP-MoE introduces a consistency-preserving routing bias, which uses the transient expert to estimate representation similarity with stable experts and steer routing towards more compatible expert selection, and a transient expert-guided regularisation mechanism, which selectively protects important historical parameters during merging. Together, these components reduce parameter interference and forgetting while preserving cross-task knowledge transfer. We validate CP-MoE on both unimodal and multimodal continual learning benchmarks with LLM-based and VLM-based MoE models. On SuperNI benchmark, spanning diverse sequential language tasks, CP-MoE achieves state-of-the-art performance and stronger zero-shot transfer to unseen tasks. On VQA v2 dataset, it scales effectively to multimodal visual reasoning, consistently reduces forgetting, and outperforms strong MoE baselines.

</details>


### [93] [Automated Kernel Discovery Towards Understanding High-dimensional Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20249)
*Taeyoung Yun,Woocheol Shin,Inhyuck Song,Jaewoo Lee,Jinkyoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种LLM驱动的进化框架Kernel Discovery，用于在高维贝叶斯优化中自动搜索更广泛的核函数空间，无需依赖观测数据或预定义组合规则，通过两阶段方法生成可执行代码，并采用LOO-CRPS准则防止过拟合，在5个基准测试中平均排名1.2/17。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯过程核函数设计在高维问题上依赖大量人工，自动化方法受限于核搜索空间（仅基核的加法和乘法）以及LLM方法因上下文长度限制和模式提取困难而难以处理原始观测数据。

Method: 两阶段进化框架：第一阶段LLM生成新颖数学形式，第二阶段将形式转换为已验证的可执行代码；采用留一法连续排序概率得分（LOO-CRPS）作为选择标准以惩罚过拟合核函数。

Result: 在5个高维贝叶斯优化基准测试中，平均排名1.2/17，优于所有对比基线。

Conclusion: 所提出的Kernel Discovery方法能有效发现适用于高维BO的新颖核函数，且进一步分析了哪些核函数能带来性能提升。

Abstract: Gaussian Process (GP) kernels are central to Bayesian optimization (BO), yet designing effective kernels for high-dimensional problems still relies on extensive manual engineering. Existing automated approaches struggle in high dimensions for two bottlenecks: their kernel search space is limited to additions and multiplications of base kernels, and LLM-based approaches require conditioning on raw observations, which becomes infeasible due to context-length limits and the difficulty of extracting meaningful patterns. We introduce \textbf{Kernel Discovery}, a LLM-driven evolutionary framework for high-dimensional BO that searches a broader kernel space beyond predefined composition rules and does not require conditioning on observations. Motivated by the observation that directly prompting an LLM to generate kernel code yields syntactically varied but functionally identical kernels, we adopt a two-stage approach: an LLM first proposes novel mathematical forms, then a second LLM call converts each form into validated, executable code. We also propose a leave-one-out continuous ranked probability score (LOO-CRPS) as a selection criterion that penalizes overfitted kernels. On five high-dimensional BO benchmarks, our method achieves an average rank of \textbf{1.2 out of 17}, outperforming competitive baselines.
  We further analyze the discovered kernels to identify which kernels lead to improvements in high-dimensional BO.

</details>


### [94] [From Circuit Evidence to Mechanistic Theory: An Inductive Logic Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21303)
*Nura Aljaafari,Danilo S. Carvalho,Andre Freitas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了机制可解释性的形式化基础设施，通过因果功能签名（CFS）和归纳逻辑编程（ILP）学习的结构签名，将电路解释视为归纳理论构建，实现跨模型尺度和架构族的结构化比较与迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机制可解释性产生的电路分析往往是孤立的实验产物，缺乏共享的形式化表示来描述电路计算的内容、相互关系，以及不同发现何时提供相同机制的证据。

Method: 每个电路在两个层面进行表征：(1) 因果功能签名（CFS），基于因果归因证据和token角色轮廓来刻画组件行为；(2) 架构签名 τ_arch，通过归纳逻辑编程（ILP）从尺度不变的结构谓词中学习。两者共同构成形式化一致性层，使机制主张显式化、可通过θ-包容进行比较，并可跨模型尺度移植。

Result: CFS揭示了跨任务类型的定性不同的计算策略（如注意力介导的复制与MLP介导的绑定）。ILP签名在结构分离方面显著优于图核和特征向量基线，并支持跨模型尺度和架构族的原则性迁移。

Conclusion: 该工作为累积性机制科学提供了形式化基础设施，使机制解释从孤立的实验产物迈向可比较、可迁移的归纳理论构建。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability produces circuit-level causal analyses of neural network behaviour, but discovered circuits often remain isolated experimental artefacts: there is no shared formal representation for what circuits compute, how they relate, or when two findings provide evidence for the same mechanism. This work provides a formal infrastructure for cumulative mechanistic science by treating circuit interpretation as inductive theory construction. Each circuit is characterised at two levels: a Causal Functional Signature (CFS), which grounds component behaviour in causal attribution evidence and token role profiles, and an architectural signature $τ_{\mathrm{arch}}$, learned by inductive logic programming (ILP) from scale-invariant structural predicates. Together, these constitute a formal coherence layer that makes mechanistic claims explicit, comparable via $θ$-subsumption, and portable across model scales. CFS reveals qualitatively distinct computational strategies across task types, including attention-mediated copying versus MLP-mediated binding. ILP signatures achieve substantially better structural separation than graph kernel and feature-vector baselines, and support principled transfer across model scales and architecture families.

</details>


### [95] [Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Safe Autonomous Driving Under Pedestrian Behavioral Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20255)
*Prakash Aryan,Kaushik Raghupathruni,Timo Kehrer,Sebastiano Panichella*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于多智能体强化学习（MARL）联合训练自动驾驶汽车与行人的仿真框架，相比固定行人策略的基线方法，能生成更真实的交互场景。实验表明，联合训练将碰撞率从33%降至14%，但随意横穿仍占碰撞的62%，且自动驾驶对随意横穿者的速度反应差异表明其未能提前预判此类行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于脚本化或简化行人模型的自动驾驶仿真测试无法捕捉真实行人过街行为的异质性与不确定性，特别是受潜在人格特质影响的随意横穿行为，导致安全评估缺乏真实性。

Method: 构建MARL环境，使用MAPPO算法联合训练1辆自动驾驶汽车与12个行人；行人运动采用Dijkstra寻路脚本控制，RL策略则控制高层"走/等"决策；每个行人的随意横穿概率由随机采样的人格特质决定，且对自动驾驶隐藏。

Result: 联合训练自动驾驶达到78%目标达成率与14%碰撞率（基线为35%与33%）；速度差异指标显示自动驾驶在近距离（0-3米）对随意横穿者比对使用人行道者快2.65m/s，表明未能预判随意横穿；随意横穿仅占13%过街事件却导致62%碰撞；相比单智能体RL，MARL联合训练降低30%碰撞。

Conclusion: MARL联合训练可生成更真实的交互场景并降低碰撞率，但自动驾驶仍难以预判由隐藏人格特质驱动的随意横穿行为，凸显了在仿真中引入不可观测异质性行人模型的重要性。

Abstract: Simulation-based testing of self-driving cars (SDCs) typically relies on scripted or simplified pedestrian models that do not capture the heterogeneity and uncertainty of real human crossing behavior. This limits the realism of safety assessments, especially in scenarios involving jaywalking, which is governed by latent personality traits that the vehicle cannot observe. We hypothesize that jointly training pedestrians and the SDC with multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) produces more realistic interaction scenarios than training the SDC against fixed pedestrian policies, and that the resulting behavior gap between predictable and unpredictable crossings can be measured directly from trajectories. This paper describes a MARL environment in which an SDC and 12 pedestrians are co-trained using Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization (MAPPO). Pedestrian locomotion follows scripted Dijkstra pathfinding, while an RL policy controls high-level go/wait decisions. Jaywalking probability depends on a per-pedestrian personality trait sampled at episode start and hidden from the SDC. In 500-episode evaluations, the co-trained SDC reached 78% of goals with a 14% collision rate, compared to 35% goals and 33% collisions for the best rule-based baseline. A speed differential metric shows that the SDC traveled 2.65 m/s faster near jaywalkers than near crosswalk users at close range (0-3 m), indicating that jaywalking encounters were not anticipated. Jaywalking accounted for 13% of crossing events but was associated with 62% of collisions. Co-training with MARL pedestrians reduced collisions by 30% relative to single-agent RL, as pedestrians learned to wait when the SDC approached at speed.

</details>


### [96] [FBOS-RL: Feedback-Driven Bi-Objective Synergistic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20256)
*Xikai Zhang,Yongzhi Li,Likang Xiao,Yingze Zhang,Yanhua Cheng,Quan Chen,Peng Jiang,Wenjun Wu,Liu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FBOS-RL 提出了一种反馈驱动的双目标协同强化学习框架，通过反馈引导的探索增强和两个互相强化的训练目标（利用导向的策略对齐EPA和探索导向的能力培养ECC），解决了GRPO在复杂任务中因采样质量低导致训练停滞的问题，在相同采样量下实现了更快的训练速度和更高的性能上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO及其变体在强化学习训练中采用简单的采样策略，所有rollout均基于同一原始prompt。当任务超出策略模型当前能力时，这种采样方式难以产生高质量的rollout，导致模型参数更新缺乏有意义的梯度方向，训练陷入停滞。

Method: 提出FBOS-RL框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 反馈引导的探索增强（Feedback-Guided Exploration Enhancement），利用环境反馈指导模型探索；(2) 两个互相强化的训练目标：利用导向的策略对齐（EPA）和探索导向的能力培养（ECC），形成正反馈飞轮效应。

Result: 在相同rollout数量下，FBOS-RL比GRPO和基于反馈的基线方法学习速度更快，最终性能上限更高；训练过程中表现出更高的策略熵和更低的梯度范数。

Conclusion: EPA和ECC两个目标可以相互强化，形成正反馈飞轮效应，显著提升强化学习的训练效率和最终性能天花板。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become a cornerstone for aligning and unlocking the reasoning capabilities of large-scale models. At its core, the training loop of GRPO and its variants alternates between rollout sampling and policy update. Unlike supervised learning, where each gradient step is anchored to an explicit ground-truth target, the optimal gradient direction for updating model parameters in this setting is not known a priori; the high-quality rollouts drawn during the sampling stage therefore act as the implicit "teacher" that guides every parameter update. However, GRPO adopt a simple sampling scheme that conditions all rollouts on the same original prompt. When a task lies beyond the policy model's current capability, this sampling scheme rarely yields a high-quality rollout, leaving the policy model without a meaningful gradient direction when updating its parameters, which causes training to stall. To address this issue, we propose FBOS-RL, a Feedback-Driven Bi-Objective Synergistic reinforcement learning framework. Specifically, we let the model perform Feedback-Guided Exploration Enhancement based on the feedback provided by the environment, and on top of this we design two mutually reinforcing training objectives: Exploitation-oriented Policy Alignment(EPA) and Exploration-oriented Capability Cultivation(ECC). Extensive experiments demonstrate that EPA and ECC can mutually reinforce each other, forming a positive flywheel effect that significantly improves both the training efficiency and the final performance ceiling of reinforcement learning. Specifically, under an identical number of rollouts, FBOS-RL learns substantially faster than GRPO and feedback-based baselines and ultimately attains a higher performance ceiling, while exhibiting higher policy entropy and lower gradient norms throughout training.

</details>


### [97] [Instance Discrimination for Link Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20257)
*Valentin Cuzin-Rambaud,Mathieu Lefort,Rémy Cazabet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对自监督学习中的链接预测任务，提出基于链路表示（而非节点表示）的两个新模型L-GRACE和L-BGRL，并引入基于社区结构的图增广方法，在无属性图上表现优异，达到与监督/自监督方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的实例判别自监督学习在图节点分类任务上已取得显著成果，但在链接预测任务上的研究较少。作者希望填补这一空白，探索如何将自监督方法有效迁移到链接预测场景。

Method: 1) 对现有自监督模型在链接预测任务上进行严格评估，发现性能主要依赖于增广过程；2) 提出基于社区结构的新型结构化图增广方法；3) 提出两个新模型L-GRACE和L-BGRL，核心创新是将学习对象从节点表示改为链路表示。

Result: L-GRACE和L-BGRL在链接预测任务上显著提升现有方法性能，尤其在无属性图上表现突出，并且与有监督和自监督的当前最优方法性能相当。

Conclusion: 基于链路表示的自监督学习能更好地适应链接预测任务，社区结构增广提供了有效的先验信息，该方法在图表示学习中有广泛适用性。
