Two ID-data diagnostics: $η^2$ (class-conditional F-test) and $Δμ$ (log-likelihood shift under synthetic corruptions) -- quantify encoder specialization, while a Tippett minimum $p$-value combination aggregates per-encoder scores into a single, calibration-stable OOD signal. EncMin2L achieves $\geq 0.94$ AUROC across all four shift types simultaneously, outperforming the state-of-the-art representation-space diffusion OOD detectors across overlapping benchmarks.

</details>


### [133] [Reinforcing Human Behavior Simulation via Verbal Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20506)
*Weiwei Sun,Xuhui Zhou,Jiarui Liu,Weihua Du,Haojia Sun,Yiqing Xie,Qianou Ma,Sihao Chen,Mengting Wan,Longqi Yang,Pei Zhou,Sherry Wu,Sean Welleck,Graham Neubig,Yiming Yang,Maarten Sap*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DITTO方法，将自然语言反馈作为强化学习中的一等信号来训练LLM模拟人类行为，在10个SOUL基准任务上平均提升36%，6项超越GPT-5.4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM训练中的反馈信号主要集中于代码和数学等可验证领域，奖励被压缩为标量值。然而，当LLM用于模拟人类行为（如用户、病人、学生等角色）时，需要处理的是主观、多维度、自然语言形式的反馈，现有方法无法有效利用这类信号。

Method: DITTO：每次rollout后接收自然语言反馈，生成反馈条件化的改进rollout；联合优化两个输出与GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization），将语言引导蒸馏到基础策略中，测试时无需反馈。同时构建SOUL基准，涵盖心智理论、角色扮演、社交技能、学习者模拟、用户模拟和人格模拟6类共10个任务。

Result: DITTO在SOUL基准上平均比基础模型提升36%，在10个任务中的6个上超越GPT-5.4，验证了自然语言反馈强化学习训练人类行为模拟的有效性。

Conclusion: 将自然语言反馈作为强化学习的一等信号是训练LLM模拟人类行为的有前景方向；未来可进一步探索更丰富的反馈形式和更复杂的社交场景。

Abstract: Humans learn social norms and behaviors from verbal feedback (e.g., a parent saying "that was rude" or a friend explaining "here's why that hurt"). Yet, learning from feedback for LLMs has largely focused on domains like code and math, where RL rewards are directly verifiable and condensed into scalar values. As LLMs are increasingly used to simulate human behavior, e.g., standing in for users, patients, students, and other personas, there is a pressing need to make them more human-like, which requires embracing a fundamentally different kind of signal: feedback that is verbal, subjective, and multi-faceted. We present DITTO, a model trained by treating verbal feedback as a first-class signal in reinforcement learning. After each rollout, DITTO receives verbal feedback and generates a feedback-conditioned improved rollout; both outputs are jointly optimized with GRPO, distilling verbal guidance into the base policy without requiring feedback at test time. We also introduce SOUL (Simulation gym Of hUman-Like behavior), a unified benchmark and training data suite spanning 10 tasks across six categories: Theory of Mind, character role play, social skill, learner simulation, user simulation, and persona simulation. DITTO achieves an average 36% improvement over the base model and exceeds GPT-5.4 on 6 of 10 SOUL benchmarks, demonstrating that RL with verbal feedback is a promising direction for training LLMs to simulate human behavior.

</details>


### [134] [Online Conformal Prediction with Corrupted Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20515)
*Bowen Wang,Matteo Zecchin,Osvaldo Simeone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在线共形预测在反馈信号被噪声、通信故障或对抗性破坏时的鲁棒性问题，提出了两种鲁棒方案（基于滤波和基于主动补偿），并在理论和实验上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线共形预测方法依赖于完美的覆盖反馈信号，但在实际应用中，反馈信号可能被噪声、通信故障或对抗性攻击破坏，导致校准保证严重退化。现有研究缺乏对此类场景的理论分析和鲁棒方案。

Method: 将反馈损坏建模为任意二进制翻转序列；提出两种鲁棒方案：(1) 基于滤波的鲁棒OCP，利用预测阈值的结构特性过滤损坏反馈；(2) 基于主动补偿的鲁棒OCP，引入主动补偿机制抵消损坏反馈的影响。为两种方法建立了显式的误覆盖保证，并针对独立随机翻转模型和带记忆上界的任意错误模型进行了特化。

Result: 在真实数据集上的实验验证了所提方法在损坏反馈下相比基线OCP方法显著改善了校准效果，并生成了更小的预测集。

Conclusion: 本文系统分析了反馈损坏对在线共形预测的影响，并提供了有效的鲁棒解决方案，拓展了OCP在非理想反馈环境下的应用可行性。

Abstract: Modern artificial intelligence systems require calibrated uncertainty estimates that remain reliable in sequential and non-stationary environments. Online conformal prediction (OCP) addresses this challenge through adaptively updated prediction sets that provide deterministic long-run miscoverage guarantees. These guarantees, however, hinge on the assumption of perfect feedback about the coverage of past prediction sets. In practice, the observed miscoverage indicator may be corrupted by noise, communication failures, or adversarial manipulation, which can severely degrade OCP's calibration guarantees. In this paper, we study OCP under corrupted feedback. We first model feedback corruption as an arbitrary binary flip sequence, and analyze how feedback corruption affects and degrades the miscoverage performance of standard OCP. We then propose two robust schemes: robust OCP via filtering, which leverages the structural properties of the predicted threshold to filter corrupted feedback, and robust OCP via active compensation, which incorporates an active compensation mechanism to mitigate the effect of corrupted feedback. For both methods, we establish explicit miscoverage guarantees, which are further specialized for an independent stochastic flip model and for an arbitrary error model with memory bounds. Experiments on real-world datasets validate the proposed approach, showing markedly improved calibration and significantly smaller prediction sets compared with baseline OCP methods under corrupted feedback.

</details>


### [135] [Ada2MS: A Hybrid Optimization Algorithm Based on Exponential Mixing of Elementwise and Global Second-Moment Estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20533)
*Meng Zhu,Quan Xiao,Weidong Min*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Ada2MS优化算法，通过元素级二阶矩估计与全局二阶矩估计之间的连续指数插值，实现AdamW类行为与动量SGD类行为的平滑过渡，在视觉任务上取得有竞争力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AdamW虽稳定性强但泛化有时不及动量SGD，而动量SGD虽泛化好但对梯度尺度变化和超参数敏感，因此需要一种能平衡两者优缺点的优化算法。

Method: 提出Ada2MS算法，通过连续指数插值在元素级二阶矩估计（类似AdamW）与全局二阶矩估计（类似动量SGD）之间实现平滑过渡。

Result: 在视觉任务上，Ada2MS在统一的优化器比较协议下取得了有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: Ada2MS有效融合了AdamW的稳定性和动量SGD的泛化优势，为优化算法的设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Optimization algorithms are core methods by which machine learning models iteratively minimize loss functions, update parameters, learn from data, and improve performance. Momentum SGD and AdamW represent two important optimization paradigms. AdamW produces stable updates and usually has strong robustness across training scenarios, but its generalization performance is sometimes weaker than that of momentum methods. Momentum SGD can often obtain better generalization after careful tuning, but it is more sensitive to gradient-scale variation and hyperparameter settings. To balance the strengths and weaknesses of the two paradigms, this paper proposes Ada2MS, an optimization algorithm that achieves a smooth transition between AdamW-like behavior and momentum-SGD-like behavior through continuous exponential interpolation between elementwise second-moment estimates and global second-moment estimates. On the visual tasks evaluated in this study, Ada2MS obtains competitive results under a unified optimizer-comparison protocol. The code will be released at https://github.com/mengzhu0308/Ada2MS

</details>


### [136] [OpenSeisML: Open Large-Scale Real Seismic and well-log Dataset for Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20539)
*Ipsita Bhar,Huseyin Tuna Erdinc,Thales Souza,Charles Jones,Felix J. Herrmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了OpenSeisML，一个从英国国家数据仓库公开数据中构建的真实地震数据集集合，旨在支持生成式AI用于地震反演，并提出了一个自动化的数据整理流程以确保可重复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ML方法用于地震反演时受到高质量速度模型稀缺的限制，因为大多数高质量数据由石油天然气公司私有。需要公开可用的真实地震数据集来支持生成式AI在地震反演中的应用。

Method: 从英国国家数据仓库（NDR）中公开的地震调查数据中整理数据集；使用校验炮（checkshot）数据建立时间-深度关系，通过插值构建速度模型进行时深转换；设计了自动化数据整理流程以确保可重复性。

Result: 构建了OpenSeisML数据集集合，实现了自动化的地震数据整理流程，支持训练能够捕捉地下属性统计分布的生成模型。

Conclusion: OpenSeisML数据集能够用于训练生成模型，合成多个统计一致的地下属性实现，为地震反演提供先验信息并支持不确定性量化。

Abstract: The advent of machine learning (ML) and computer vision has significantly accelerated seismic inversion workflows by reducing the computational cost of traditionally expensive iterative methods. However, the development and evaluation of ML methods remain limited by the scarcity of realistic velocity models, as most high-quality data are privately owned by oil and gas companies. To address this gap, we present OpenSeisML, a collection of real seismic datasets designed to support generative AI (Gen-AI) workflows for seismic inversion. The datasets are curated from publicly available surveys in the UK National Data Repository (NDR). When seismic volumes are in the time domain and wells are in depth, a time-to-depth conversion is required. We use checkshot data to establish the time-depth relationship and construct a velocity model through interpolation for accurate conversion of post-stack seismic data. Here, we present an automated data curation pipeline that enables seismic data preparation while ensuring reproducibility. The objective is to train a generative model that captures the statistical distribution of subsurface properties, enabling the synthesis of multiple statistically consistent realizations for uncertainty quantification which can act as a prior for seismic inversion.

</details>


### [137] [Latent Process Generator Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20547)
*Lukas Billera,Hedwig Nora Nordlinder,Ben Murrell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出潜在过程生成匹配（Latent Process Generator Matching），一个统一框架，将生成模型中的观测状态视为可处理马尔可夫过程$Y_t$的确定性投影$X_t=Φ(Y_t)$，并证明可以学习到与投影过程具有相同单时间边际分布的图像空间随机过程生成器。该框架推广了文献中离散潜在过程的结果，并将生成匹配从静态潜在变量扩展到依赖于时间的潜在条件过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多流匹配和扩散生成模型在训练时依赖辅助随机动力学，但辅助状态在生成时要么难以采样，要么不是期望输出的一部分。现有生成匹配理论局限于静态潜在随机变量的条件化，缺乏对时间依赖潜在过程的统一形式化框架。

Method: 将观测生成状态$X_t$定义为可处理马尔可夫过程$Y_t$的确定性投影$Φ(Y_t)$，在此设置下推导出可学习图像空间上具有与投影过程相同单时间边际分布的随机过程生成器。

Result: 该框架推广并包含文献中离散潜在过程的结果，将生成匹配从静态潜在变量扩展到依赖于时间的潜在条件过程家族。

Conclusion: 潜在过程生成匹配为各种依赖辅助随机动力学的生成模型提供了统一的理论基础，填补了现有生成匹配理论在时间依赖潜在过程方面的空白。

Abstract: Many recent flow-matching and diffusion-style generative models rely on auxiliary stochastic dynamics during training: a richer process is simulated to define conditional targets, but the auxiliary state is either intractable to sample at generation time or simply not part of the desired output. Existing Generator Matching theory formalises conditioning on static latent random variables, and several recent papers prove special cases of projection results for particular augmented-state constructions. We introduce latent process generator matching, a general framework that treats the observed generative state as a deterministic image $X_t=Φ(Y_t)$ of a tractable Markov process $Y_t$. We show that in this setting one may learn the generator of a stochastic process on the image space which has the same one-time marginal distributions as the projected process. This generalizes and subsumes the discrete latent process results from the literature, and extends Generator Matching from static latent variables to a rich family of time-dependent latent conditional processes.

</details>


### [138] [Complementing reinforcement learning with SFT through logit averaging in the post training of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20555)
*Xingwei Gan,Ying Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种在GRPO中对冻结参考策略（如SFT）和可训练策略进行logit平均的新方法，替代传统KL正则化，在MATH、cn-k12和MMLU上取得更高或相当的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法依赖KL正则化或critic，计算开销大且可能限制策略优化。作者希望在不使用KL正则化和critic的情况下，同时利用可训练策略的推理能力和SFT的格式化优势。

Method: 在GRPO框架中，对冻结的参考策略（如SFT模型）和可训练策略的logits进行平均，作为策略输出的耦合机制，替代传统的KL正则化项。

Result: 在MATH、cn-k12和MMLU基准上，该方法相比标准KL正则化GRPO取得了更高或至少相当的准确率。

Conclusion: Logit平均耦合结构能有效替代KL正则化，在保持SFT格式优势的同时提升推理能力，是一种更简洁高效的GRPO变体。

Abstract: We introduce a novel method that averages the logits of a frozen reference policy (e.g., SFT) and a trainable policy, and incorporate the method into Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). In contrast to Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) methods, our proposal does not involve a Kullback Leibler (KL) regularization or critic; the trainable policy and the reference anchor are coupled through the logit averaging structure to leverage the reasoning expertise of the trainable policy while maintaining the formatting advantage of SFT. Our method is evaluated on MATH, cn-k12, and MMLU, and the results show a higher accuracy or at least comparable accuracy relative to the canonical KL-regularized GRPO.

</details>


### [139] [TriForces: Augmenting Atomistic GNNs for Transferable Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20581)
*Ali Ramlaoui,Alexandre Duval,Hannah Bull,Victor Schmidt,Hugues Talbot,Fragkiskos D. Malliaros,Joseph Musielewicz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TriForces是一个模型无关的三流框架，通过分离组成和结构信息并结合自监督学习，提升了机器学习原子间势在有限数据下的迁移能力，在MatBench、QM9和OMat24数据集上均取得了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLIPs在跨领域迁移时表现不一致，其表示往往丢失了可访问的组成和结构信息，而实际应用中需要利用小且昂贵的任务特定数据集进行适配。

Method: 提出TriForces模型无关三流框架：1）分离组成和结构信息的表示；2）结合自监督学习以保留可迁移的表示；3）通过学习到的潜空间实现高效相似结构检索。

Result: 在MatBench和QM9上超越基线；在OMat24上，20K样本时能量MAE降低57%，各样本量下力MAE均有提升。

Conclusion: TriForces在无需DFT标签的情况下提升了MLIPs的迁移性能，能通过潜空间高效检索相似结构，并已开源多架构的预训练模型。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) achieve excellent accuracy when trained on large Density Functional Theory (DFT) data. To be useful in practice, they must often be adapted to target chemistries using small and expensive task-specific datasets. However, MLIPs transfer inconsistently across domains, with representations that often loose accessible composition and structure information. To address this, we present TriForces, a model-agnostic three-stream framework that separates composition and structure information, combined with self-supervised learning to preserve transferable representations. TriForces improves performance on MatBench and QM9 over baselines without needing DFT labels and enables efficient similar structure retrieval through its learned latent space. On OMat24, in limited-data training regime, TriForces reduces energy MAE by 57% at 20K samples only and improves force MAE across sample sizes. We release pretrained TriForces variants across multiple MLIP architectures with code at https://github.com/Ramlaoui/triforces.

</details>


### [140] [ReversedQ: Opportunities for Faster Q-Learning in Episodic Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20592)
*Sofia R. Miskala-Dinc,Aviva Prins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限时域马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）中的无模型Q学习，指出现有无模型后验采样方法依赖延迟学习的问题，并提出了三项加速学习的改进策略：值函数更新顺序、更新频率和值函数初始化。基于RandomizedQ改进得到的ReversedQ在BDCL任务中将平均累积奖励从9.53%提升至78.78%，在链式MDP中从21.76%提升至61.81%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出现有无模型后验采样方法依赖延迟学习来保证理论性能的问题，并探索更高效的Q学习策略。

Method: 在RandomizedQ基础上，提出了三项改进：（i）值函数更新顺序、（ii）更新频率、（iii）值函数初始化，并将这些改进组合为ReversedQ方法。

Result: ReversedQ在BDCL任务中平均累积奖励从9.53%提升至78.78%，在链式MDP中从21.76%提升至61.81%。

Conclusion: 通过优化值函数更新顺序、更新频率和初始化策略，可以显著提升无模型Q学习在有限时域MDP中的性能。

Abstract: We study model-free Q-learning in finite-horizon episodic Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) with stationary dynamics across episodes. We identify a central issue in nascent model-free posterior-sampling works: the reliance on delayed learning in order to prove theoretical guarantees. In particular, we identify three opportunities for faster learning - (i) value-function update order, (ii) update frequencies, and (iii) value-function initialization. Using Wang et al.'s RandomizedQ as a basis, we illustrate these changes and their individual (as well as cumulative) impact in multiple empirical studies. We find that our combined modifications, termed ReversedQ, improve scaled mean cumulative reward compared to RandomizedQ, from 9.53% to 78.78% in the Bidirectional Diabolical Combination Lock (BDCL), and from 21.76% to 61.81% in a chain MDP.

</details>


### [141] [Unsupervised clustering and classification of upper limb EMG signals during functional movements: a data-driven](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20599)
*L. F. Salazar Álvarez,D. Escobar-Saltarén,M. B. Salazar Sánchez,S. C. Henao-Aguirre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对上肢表面肌电信号（sEMG）的聚类与分类综合方法，应用于NINAPRO DB4数据集。通过四阶段流水线（预处理、特征提取、手势选择、模型评估），最终确定Extra Trees和ANN为最优分类器，为肌电假肢的自适应低延迟控制提供了可扩展方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现对功能性抓取和伸手运动中上肢sEMG信号的高效聚类与分类，以支持肌电假肢的自适应、低延迟控制策略。

Method: 四阶段流水线：(1) 信号预处理：四阶低通滤波(0.6 Hz)和Hilbert包络变换；(2) 特征提取：26个时频域指标，通过可视化分析、互信息、PCA和决策树重要性评分最终筛选出5个关键特征；(3) 手势选择：基于马氏距离的层次聚类，选出6个代表性动作；(4) 模型评估：PyCaret自动比较筛选出Extra Trees (ET)和ANN，再独立训练验证。

Result: 200 ms时间窗口为最优分段方案。ET和ANN为表现最优的分类器：ANN呈现渐进式学习能力，ET表现稳健且结果一致。两者均具有良好的稳定性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本研究支持在肌电假肢中实现自适应、低延迟的控制策略，提供了一种可扩展的流水线，适用于未来的实时应用场景。

Abstract: This study presents a comprehensive approach for the clustering and classification of upper-limb surface electromyography (sEMG) signals during functional reach and grasp movements. The methodology was applied to the NINAPRO DB4 dataset, which provides multichannel EMG recordings of 52 gestures. A four-stage pipeline was designed, including signal preprocessing, fea-ture extraction, gesture selection via hierarchical clustering, and comparative model evaluation. Preprocessing involved a fourth-order low-pass filter (0.6 Hz) and Hilbert envelope transformation, effectively reducing noise and enhancing signal clarity. Feature extraction yielded 26 temporal and frequency-domain met-rics, which were later refined using visual analysis, mutual information, principal component analysis, and decision tree importance scores. A final subset of five key features was selected for classification tasks. Gesture selection was per-formed through hierarchical clustering using Mahalanobis distance, resulting in six representative movements that balanced biomechanical diversity and compu-tational efficiency. A 200 ms window was identified as optimal for temporal seg-mentation based on stability and physiological plausibility. Classifier models were evaluated in two stages. Automated comparison using PyCaret identified Extra Trees (ET) and Artificial Neural Networks (ANN) as top performers. Sub-sequent independent training confirmed their stability and generalization capac-ity, with ANN showing progressive learning and ET maintaining robust, con-sistent results. The findings support the implementation of adaptive, low-latency control strategies for myoelectric prostheses and provide a scalable pipeline for future real-time applications.

</details>


### [142] [Compositional Transduction with Latent Analogies for Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20609)
*Junseok Kim,Dohyeong Kim,Mineui Hong,Songhwai Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于类比转导的离线目标条件强化学习方法，通过构建任务内生的类比表示来捕捉最优执行下的不变性，实现跨上下文组合泛化，在OGBench环境中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线GCRL方法依赖时间连续片段的轨迹拼接，限制了在不同上下文间的行为组合能力，难以实现组合泛化。

Method: 形式化类比转导为利用任务内生类比与给定上下文合成新计划的过程，提出一种类比表示方法，该表示捕捉最优任务执行下的变化、对上下文变化保持不变且足以支持最优目标达成。在此基础上，解决未见类比-上下文对组合的泛化问题。

Result: 在OGBench操作环境中，该方法显著优于不进行类比转导的先前方法。

Conclusion: 类比转导是提升离线GCRL组合泛化能力的有效途径，所提出的类比表示和泛化策略在实验中得到验证。

Abstract: Compositional generalization is essential for reaching unseen goals under novel contextual variations in offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL), where a generalist goal-reaching agent must be learned from limited data. Most prior approaches pursue this via trajectory stitching over temporally contiguous segments, which limits composing behaviors across varying contexts. To overcome this limitation, we formalize analogy transduction as synthesizing new plans by composing task-endogenous analogies with given contexts and propose a novel analogy representation tailored for it. Grounded in our theory, this analogy representation captures what changes under optimal task execution, remains invariant to contextual variations, and is sufficient for optimal goal reaching. We further contend that generalization to unseen analogy-context pairs is a practical obstacle in analogy transduction, and introduce a new approach for offline GCRL that enables analogy transduction beyond seen pairs to unseen combinations. We empirically demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach on OGBench manipulation environments, substantially outperforming prior methods that do not perform analogy transduction. Project page: https://rllab-snu.github.io/projects/CTA/

</details>


### [143] [Matryoshka Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20612)
*Ziye Chen,Hongbin Lin,Xinyue Xu,Jie Li,Lijie Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Matryoshka Concept Bottleneck Model (MCBM)，通过嵌套层次化概念组织实现自适应概念利用，将概念瓶颈模型的预期干预成本从线性降低到对数级别，同时保证单调性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念瓶颈模型（CBM）在部署时面临测试时干预成本高昂的问题：要么使用单一静态概念集导致专家需穷举标注所有概念，要么训练多个不同概念预算的模型导致大量计算和维护开销。缺乏一种在单一模型内实现自适应概念利用的架构。

Method: 受Matryoshka表示学习启发，将概念按最大相关性和最小冗余度组织成嵌套层次结构，使单一模型无需重新训练即可在多种概念粒度下进行推理，从而实现自适应概念利用。

Result: 理论上证明了预期干预成本从线性降至对数级别 O(log K)，并保证单调性能提升。实验表明MCBM在匹配独立训练模型性能的同时，实现了动态高效的专家交互。

Conclusion: MCBM通过嵌套层次化概念设计，在单一模型中实现了自适应概念粒度选择，显著降低了干预成本，为概念瓶颈模型的实际部署提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) have emerged as a prominent paradigm for interpretable deep learning, learning by grounding predictions in human-understandable concepts. However, their practical deployment is hindered by the high cost of test-time intervention, as correcting model errors typically requires human experts to manually inspect and verify a large set of predicted concepts. Existing approaches suffer from a fundamental structural limitation: they either adopt a single static concept set, forcing experts to exhaustively annotate concepts and incurring prohibitive intervention costs, or train multiple models tailored to different concept budgets, resulting in substantial computational and maintenance overhead. To address this challenge, we propose the Matryoshka Concept Bottleneck Model (MCBM), a unified architecture that enables adaptive concept utilization within a single model. Inspired by Matryoshka Representation Learning, MCBM organizes concepts into a nested hierarchy based on maximum relevance and minimum redundancy, allowing inference at multiple levels of conceptual granularity without retraining. Theoretically, we show that MCBM reduces the expected intervention costs from linear to logarithmic order, $O(\log K)$, while guaranteeing monotonic performance improvement. Empirically, extensive experiments demonstrate that MCBM matches the performance of independently trained models while enabling dynamic and efficient expert interaction.

</details>


### [144] [SURF: Steering the Scalarization Weight to Uniformly Traverse the Pareto Front](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20619)
*Liuyuan Jiang,Chentong Huang,Lisha Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SURF方法，通过几何分析标量化的路径，利用弧长累积分布函数（CDF）的逆映射来指导权重采样，从而在帕累托前沿上实现均匀覆盖。针对结构化问题给出闭式解，通用问题则采用CDF重构与权重采样交替进行。理论上证明了线性收敛到有限采样误差下界，实验在多个多目标优化任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标优化中，均匀采样标量化权重通常会导致帕累托前沿上非均匀覆盖，这与实际应用中对多样用户偏好均匀覆盖的需求存在矛盾。现有方法缺乏对这一不匹配现象的根本性几何解释和系统性的解决方案。

Method: 通过几何分析标量化路径，定义弧长CDF映射，利用其逆映射推导出能产生均匀PF覆盖的权重采样规则。结构化问题（如双目标bandits）给出闭式解；通用问题迭代执行CDF重构和权重采样两个步骤。

Result: 理论上证明在可证明条件下SURF线性收敛到有限采样误差下界。实验在bandits、多目标-gymnasium和多目标LLM对齐任务上，SURF均实现了比基线方法更均匀的PF覆盖。

Conclusion: 揭示了标量化权重与PF覆盖不均匀之间的几何本质，提出了理论驱动的权重采样方法SURF，兼具理论保证和实证有效性，为多目标优化的均匀偏好覆盖提供了新范式。

Abstract: Scalarization is widely used in multi-objective optimization owing to its simplicity and scalability. In many applications, the goal is to generate solutions that represent diverse user preferences, ideally with uniform coverage of the Pareto front (PF). However, uniformly sampling scalarization weights usually induces non-uniform coverage of the PF. We explain this mismatch through a geometric analysis of the scalarization path. As the scalarization weight varies, the corresponding solutions trace the PF with a generally non-uniform traversal speed. This speed induces an arc-length cumulative distribution function (CDF); inverting this CDF map yields a principled rule for selecting weights that produce uniform PF coverage. Building on this insight, we propose SURF (Sampling Uniformly along the PaReto Front). For structured problems, including bi-objective bandits, we derive closed-form expressions for this CDF map and the resulting PF-aware weight sampling rule. For general problems, SURF alternates between CDF reconstruction and weight sampling. Theoretically, we show that under provable conditions, SURF converges linearly to an unavoidable finite-sampling floor. Empirically, experiments on bandits, multi-objective-gymnasium, and multi-objective LLM alignment demonstrate that SURF efficiently achieves more uniform PF coverage than baselines.

</details>


### [145] [Same Target, Different Basins: Hard vs. Soft Labels for Annotator Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20642)
*Mirerfan Gheibi,Gashin Ghazizadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 当标注者之间存在分歧时，本文研究了硬标签交付（multipass和随机标签采样SLS）作为软标签训练的替代方案。实验表明，在标注数量有限时硬标签交付优于软标签训练；当完整标注分布可用时，两者性能相当。SLS与软标签交叉熵优化相同目标，且硬标签交付收敛于更平坦的极值点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标注者之间的分歧反映的是认知不确定性（epistemic uncertainty）而非简单的标签噪声。传统方法要么将投票压缩为单一标签，要么直接在经验软标签分布上训练，但这些方式可能无法充分利用分歧中的信息。本文探索硬标签交付作为替代方案，以更好地处理标注分歧。

Method: 研究了两种硬标签交付方法：1) multipass——在保持数据集大小固定的前提下遍历观测到的投票；2) 随机标签采样（SLS）——每个epoch开始时为每个样本采样一个标签。在CIFAR-10H上进行实验，并使用确定性控制作为multipass的消融实验，打乱顺序的SLS作为破坏样本-分布匹配的对照实验。

Result: 1) 当每个样本只有少量标注时，硬标签交付优于软标签训练，且在稀疏经验目标与完整标注分布差异越大时提升越明显。2) 当完整标注分布可用时，两种硬标签方法与软标签训练性能相当。3) SLS和软标签交叉熵优化相同的期望目标。4) 硬标签交付收敛于更平坦的极值点，在SVHN和CIFAR-100的OOD检测中得到了支持性证据。

Conclusion: multipass是当原始投票计数可用时的强默认选择；SLS作为轻量级替代方案，在仅有少量投票时具有竞争力，且在完整标注分布可用时与软标签训练性能匹配。

Abstract: When annotators disagree, that disagreement can reflect epistemic uncertainty rather than simple label noise. We study hard-label delivery as an alternative to the usual choices of collapsing votes to a single label or training directly on the empirical soft-label distribution. We focus on two primary hard-label methods: multipass, which cycles through observed votes while keeping the dataset size fixed, and stochastic label sampling (SLS), which samples one label per example at the start of each epoch. On CIFAR-10H, we find that when only a small number of annotations per example is available, hard-label delivery improves over soft-label training, with larger improvements where the sparse empirical target is farther from the full annotator distribution. When full annotator distributions are available, both hard-label methods match soft-label training. We use deterministic control as an ablation of multipass and shuffled SLS as a control that breaks the example-to-distribution match. We also show that SLS and soft-label cross-entropy optimize the same expected objective. Hard-label delivery also converges to flatter basins, with supporting descriptive evidence from OOD detection on SVHN and CIFAR-100. Overall, these results suggest that multipass is a strong practical default when raw vote counts are available, while SLS offers a lightweight alternative that remains competitive when only a few votes per example are available and matches soft-label training when full annotator distributions are available.

</details>


### [146] [AVSD: Adaptive-View Self-Distillation by Balancing Consensus and Teacher-Specific Privileged Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20643)
*Duy Nguyen,Hanqi Xiao,Archiki Prasad,Zaid Khan,Anirban Das,Austin Zhang,Sambit Sahu,Hyunji Lee,Elias Stengel-Eskin,Mohit Bansal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AVSD，一种自适应多视角自蒸馏方法，通过在多个特权信息视图中分离跨视图共识与视图特异性残差信号来重构token级监督，在数学竞赛和代码生成基准上显著优于单视图自蒸馏和GRPO基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自蒸馏中教师依赖学生无法访问的特权信息，且最优特权信息类型通常任务相关，难以选择单一教师视角。这两个挑战需要被联合解决。

Method: AVSD将token级监督重构为两部分：跨视图共识信号（提供可靠更新方向）和视图特异性残差信号。当残差信号与共识方向一致且量级适当时，选择性添加以调整更新幅度。

Result: 在AIME24、AIME25、HMMT25数学竞赛基准上，Qwen3-8B和Qwen3-4B的Avg@8分别平均提升3.1%和2.2%；在Codeforces、LiveCodeBench v6代码生成基准上，Qwen3-8B平均提升2.4%。

Conclusion: AVSD通过自适应多视角自蒸馏有效解决了教师-学生信息不对称和视角选择难题，在多个推理任务上取得一致性提升。

Abstract: Self-distillation enables language models to learn on-policy from their own trajectories by using the same model as both student and teacher, with the teacher being conditioned on privileged information unavailable to the student. Such information can come in different types or views, such as solutions, demonstrations, feedback, or final answers. This setup provides dense token-level feedback without relying on a separate external model, but creates a fundamental asymmetry: the teacher may rely on view-specific information that the student cannot access at inference time. Moreover, the best type of privileged information is often task-dependent, making it difficult to choose a single teacher view. In this work, we address both these challenges jointly by introducing AVSD (Adaptive-View Self-Distillation), a novel method of self-distillation with multiple privileged-information views, which reconstructs token-level supervision by separating stable cross-view consensus from view-specific residual signals. AVSD identifies the consensus signal shared across views, which provides a reliable update direction, and then selectively adds the view-specific residual signal to adjust the update magnitude when it both aligns with the consensus direction and remains proportionate to the consensus signal. Experiments on math competition benchmarks (AIME24, AIME25, and HMMT25) show that AVSD consistently outperforms both single-view self-distillation baselines and GRPO, achieving average Avg@8 gains of 3.1% and 2.2% over the strongest baselines on Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-4B, respectively. Moreover, on code-generation benchmarks (Codeforces, LiveCodeBench v6) using Qwen3-8B, AVSD outperforms the single-view self-distillation baseline by 2.4% on average.

</details>


### [147] [LT2: Linear-Time Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20670)
*Chunyuan Deng,Yizhe Zhang,Rui-Jie Zhu,Yuanyuan Xu,Jiarui Liu,T. S. Eugene Ng,Hanjie Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LT2（线性时间循环Transformer）系列架构，用次二次/线性时间注意力替代标准循环Transformer中的二次softmax注意力。研究表明，循环机制与线性/稀疏注意力存在独特协同效应：线性注意力中实现迭代记忆精化，稀疏注意力中渐进扩展有效感受野。混合变体LT2-hybrid (GDN+DSA)在完全线性时间成本下达到标准循环Transformer质量；LT2-hybrid (Full+GDN)在性能和效率上超越标准模型。转换后的1.4B模型仅用约1B tokens训练，性能优于行业1B模型，与4B模型竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer在每层迭代多次带来二次复杂度瓶颈，计算昂贵且速度慢。作者希望在不牺牲质量的前提下，通过引入线性时间注意力机制使循环Transformer更可扩展，推动高效小语言模型的发展。

Method: 提出LT2系列架构，包含三种变体：(1) LT2-linear：线性注意力实现迭代记忆精化；(2) LT2-sparse：稀疏注意力实现渐进有效感受野扩展；(3) LT2-hybrid：在循环设置中混合不同注意力变体（如GDN线性注意力+DSA稀疏注意力，或少量Full注意力+GDN）。并提供将预训练LT转换为LT2-hybrid的方法。

Result: LT2-hybrid (GDN+DSA)在完全线性时间成本下匹配标准循环Transformer质量；LT2-hybrid (Full+GDN)在性能和效率上均超越标准循环Transformer。转换后的Ouro-hybrid-1.4B模型（约1B tokens训练）性能超越行业1B模型，与行业4B模型竞争。

Conclusion: 循环机制与线性/稀疏注意力存在独特协同效应，LT2系列为循环Transformer的可扩展性提供了清晰路径，推动了高效小语言模型的进展。

Abstract: Looped Transformers (LT) have emerged as a powerful architecture by iterating their layers multiple times before decoding the final token. However, pairing them with full attention retains quadratic complexity, making them computationally expensive and slow. We introduce LT2 (Linear-Time Looped Transformers), a family of looped architectures that replace quadratic softmax attention with subquadratic, linear-time attention. We study two variants: LT2-linear with linear attention and LT2-sparse with sparse attention. We find that looping uniquely synergizes with these variants: it enables iterative memory refinement in linear attention and progressively expands the effective receptive field in sparse attention. We formalize these benefits theoretically and demonstrate consistent empirical gains across controlled recall, state-tracking, and language modeling tasks. We then explore LT2-hybrid, which combines different attention variants in a looped setting. Two variants are especially promising: LT2-hybrid (GDN+DSA), which interleaves linear and sparse attention to maximize efficiency and matches the standard looped transformer's quality at fully linear-time cost; and LT2-hybrid (Full+GDN), which interleaves GDN with a small fraction of full attention layers to maximize quality, surpassing the standard looped transformer in both performance and efficiency. We also show how to convert a pre-trained LT into an LT2-hybrid model. With about 1B tokens of training, our converted model, Ouro-hybrid-1.4B, outperforms industry-level 1B models and is competitive with industry-level 4B models while retaining the speed benefits of linear-time attention. Together, these results show a clear path toward making looped transformers more scalable and advancing efficient, capable small language models.

</details>


### [148] [Dynamic TMoE: A Drift-Aware Dynamic Mixture of Experts Framework for Non-Stationary Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20678)
*Jiawen Zhu,Shuhan Liu,Di Weng,Yingcai Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对非平稳时间序列预测中分布漂移难以捕捉的问题，提出Dynamic TMoE框架，通过MMD检测分布漂移动态实例化/剪枝专家，并引入时序记忆路由器实现上下文感知的专家选择，在9个基准上MSE降低10.4%、MAE降低7.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平稳时间序列预测面临分布漂移的挑战，现有静态模型难以适应。虽然MoE架构有望解耦复杂漂移模式，但现有方法受限于固定专家池和无记忆路由，无法适应突发的状态变迁。

Method: 1) 利用最大均值差异(MMD)检测分布漂移；2) 动态实例化异构专家并剪枝冗余专家以优化容量；3) 提出时序记忆路由器，利用循环状态和异常存储库实现稳定、上下文感知的专家选择，无需测试时更新。

Result: 在9个基准数据集上达到最先进性能，MSE降低10.4%，MAE降低7.8%。

Conclusion: Dynamic TMoE通过动态专家管理和时序记忆路由机制，有效应对非平稳时间序列中的分布漂移问题，显著提升了预测精度。

Abstract: Non-stationary time series forecasting is challenged by evolving distribution shifts that static models struggle to capture. While Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures offer a promising paradigm for decoupling complex drift patterns, existing approaches are limited by fixed expert pools and memoryless routing, hampering their ability to adapt to abrupt regime shifts. To address this, we propose Dynamic TMoE, a framework that unifies architectural evolution with temporal continuity during learning phase. By detecting distribution shifts via Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD), we dynamically instantiate heterogeneous experts and prune redundant ones to optimize capacity. Additionally, a temporal memory router leverages recurrent states and an anomaly repository to ensure stable, context-aware expert selection without requiring test-time updates. Experiments on nine benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, reducing MSE by 10.4% and MAE by 7.8%. Code is available at https://github.com/andone-07/Dynamic-TMoE.

</details>


### [149] [Distributed Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20696)
*Zhanhong Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对分布式环境下直接偏好优化（DPO）的收敛性和时间复杂度进行了理论分析，涵盖了联邦学习和去中心化两种场景，并通过实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于偏好的强化学习是使策略与人类判断对齐的关键范式，但其在偏好数据碎片化分布于异构用户群体中的分布式场景下的理论行为尚不明确。DPO虽避免了显式奖励建模，但在联邦和去中心化训练中缺乏收敛保证，通信约束和非独立同分布偏好会根本性地改变优化动态。

Method: 建模个性化离线强化学习与用户特定偏好分布，刻画全局优化景观。对联邦DPO，推导了客户端漂移、通信频率和偏好异质性对收敛速率的影响；对去中心化DPO，推导了在一般通信图上的收敛性，分析谱连通性如何影响优化速度和共识。

Result: 联邦DPO场景下量化了客户端漂移、通信频率和偏好异质性对收敛速率的影响；去中心化DPO场景下证明了收敛性并揭示了谱连通性与优化速度、共识的关系。实验上在标准对齐基准中验证了理论，所提方法兼具强理论保证和实际鲁棒可扩展性能。

Conclusion: 分布式DPO的理论分析填补了该领域的空白，为联邦和去中心化场景下的偏好对齐提供了收敛保证和优化指引，理论结果与实验一致，证明方法的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Preference-based reinforcement learning (RL) is a key paradigm for aligning policies with human judgments, yet its theoretical behavior in distributed settings where preference data are fragmented across heterogeneous users remains poorly understood. Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) avoids explicit reward modeling but lacks convergence guarantees under federated and decentralized training, where communication constraints and non-IID preferences fundamentally alter optimization dynamics. We provide the first convergence and time-complexity analysis of DPO in distributed environments. Modeling personalized offline RL with user-specific preference distributions, we characterize the induced global optimization landscape. For federated DPO, we derive convergence rates that quantify the impact of client drift, communication frequency, and preference heterogeneity; for decentralized DPO, we establish convergence over general communication graphs and show how spectral connectivity governs optimization speed and consensus. Empirically, we corroborate our theoretical insights on standard alignment benchmarks, demonstrating that our proposed methods not only enjoy strong theoretical guarantees but also deliver robust and scalable performance in practice. The code base is available here.

</details>


### [150] [Decision-Path Patterns as Tree Reliability Signals: Path-based Adaptive Weighting for Random Forest Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20716)
*Youngjoon Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于决策路径拓扑模式的类条件比率加权方法，通过重加权随机森林中每棵树的投票权重（而非简单多数投票），在30个二分类基准上显著提升精度（Wilcoxon p=0.018），且不牺牲多数类召回率，仅3/30数据集出现少数类召回率下降，优于传统加权RF和动态集成选择方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机森林采用简单多数投票聚合所有树，但不同树的可靠性存在差异。作者观察到沿决策路径的拓扑模式（主导类标签翻转的位置和频率）包含树可靠性的信号，但直接利用该信号会与预测类产生结构混淆，需要设计无偏的加权方案。

Method: 提出类条件比率加权（class-conditional ratio weighting）：基于每棵树根到叶决策路径上的类标签翻转拓扑模式，计算类条件比率权重，保证零期望类偏差。在共享森林、共享分裂协议的30个二分类基准上，与RF、加权RF、KNORA-Eliminate、KNORA-Union四种方案比较，进行30次重复实验。

Result: 所提方法是唯一在统计上显著优于RF的方案（Wilcoxon p=0.018），其他三种方案均未达到显著性（p>0.5）。唯一没有多数类召回率退化的方案，仅3/30数据集出现少数类召回率下降（动态集成选择方法更易受此影响）。增益在100-1000棵树的规模上保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 基于决策路径拓扑的类条件比率加权能有效提升随机森林分类性能，在保持多数类性能的同时改善整体精度，且比传统加权方法和动态集成选择方法更稳健。

Abstract: Random forests aggregate tree votes by simple majority, treating all trees as equally informative. We observe that the topological pattern along each tree's root-to-leaf decision path -- where and how often the dominant class label flips along it -- carries a signal of tree reliability that is exploitable for per-sample reweighting. The naive use of this signal is structurally confounded with the predicted class, so we propose a class-conditional ratio weighting that guarantees zero expected class bias by construction. On 30 binary classification benchmarks under a shared-forest, shared-split protocol with 30 repeats, the proposed method is the only one among four compared schemes -- RF, weighted RF, KNORA-Eliminate, KNORA-Union -- to yield a statistically significant accuracy improvement over RF (Wilcoxon p = 0.018), while the three alternatives all fail to do so (p > 0.5). It is also the only scheme without majority-recall regressions, with minority-recall regressions limited to 3/30 datasets -- a one-sided loss to which classical dynamic ensemble selection methods are susceptible. The gain is robust across forest sizes from 100 to 1000 trees.

</details>


### [151] [Hack-Verifiable Environments: Towards Evaluating Reward Hacking at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20744)
*Amit Roth,Ankur Samanta,Matan Halevy,Yoav Levine,Yonathan Efroni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的评估范式，通过在环境中嵌入可检测的奖励黑客机会，实现对奖励黑客行为的确定性、自动化测量，并基于TextArena构建了Hack-Verifiable TextArena测试平台，用于分析语言模型在不同环境中的奖励黑客行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要事后分析智能体轨迹来检测奖励黑客行为，缺乏大规模、可靠的测量方法。

Method: 将可检测的奖励黑客机会直接嵌入环境，使得对漏洞的利用可通过设计验证，从而实现确定性和自动化的测量。基于TextArena实例化该方法，构建Hack-Verifiable TextArena测试平台。

Result: 构建了Hack-Verifiable TextArena基准测试平台，能够可靠测量奖励黑客行为，并分析了语言模型在不同环境和设置下的奖励黑客行为模式。

Conclusion: 通过嵌入可验证的奖励黑客机会，实现了对奖励黑客行为的可靠、自动化测量，为评估智能体对齐提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Aligning autonomous agents with human intent remains a central challenge in modern AI. A key manifestation of this challenge is reward hacking, whereby agents appear successful under the evaluation signal while violating the intended objective. Reward hacking has been observed across a wide range of settings, yet methods for reliably measuring it at scale remain lacking. In this work, we introduce a new evaluation paradigm for measuring reward hacking. Whereas prior studies have primarily analyzed it post hoc by inspecting agent trajectories, we instead embed detectable reward hacking opportunities directly into environments. This makes their exploitation verifiable by design, enabling deterministic and automated measurement of whether and how agents exploit such vulnerabilities. We instantiate this approach in $\textit{TextArena}$ and release $\textit{Hack-Verifiable TextArena}$, a testbed in which reward hacking can be measured reliably. Using this benchmark, we analyze reward hacking behavior across language models in diverse environments and settings. We open source the code at https://github.com/MajoRoth/hack-verifiable-environments/.

</details>


### [152] [The Hidden Signal of Verifier Strictness: Controlling and Improving Step-Wise Verification via Selective Latent Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20745)
*Yefan Zhou,Yilun Zhou,Austin Xu,Soroush Vosoughi,Shafiq Joty,Jiang Gui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现生成式验证器存在校准问题（过于宽容或严格），并提出VerifySteer方法，通过隐藏状态干预来控制验证器严格性，在不微调的情况下实现错误检测与正确性认证的平衡，在ProcessBench和Hard2Verify上优于提示优化和激活引导基线，且与自一致性方法竞争力相当但计算量减少4-7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式验证器在逐步验证中校准不良，存在过度宽容（漏检错误步骤）或过度严格（拒绝正确推理）的问题，即"验证器严格性"问题。作者希望探究能否通过隐藏状态干预来控制这种严格性。

Method: 首先发现验证器对步骤接受或拒绝的倾向编码在验证段落的边界附近。基于此提出VerifySteer方法：利用潜在正确性信号进行样本级路由，并在段落边界上选择性干预。

Result: 在ProcessBench和Hard2Verify上，VerifySteer优于提示优化和激活引导基线；与自一致性方法竞争力相当但推理计算量减少4-7倍；并且与验证微调互补，能在微调验证器基础上进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: 验证器严格性可通过隐藏状态干预控制，VerifySteer提供了一种无需微调的有效控制手段，且与现有微调方法兼容。

Abstract: Generative verifiers have emerged as a promising paradigm for step-wise verification, but their verification behavior is often poorly calibrated: they may be under-critical and miss erroneous steps, or over-critical and reject correct reasoning. We refer to this tendency to be overly lenient or overly critical as verifier strictness. In this work, we study whether verifier strictness can be controlled through hidden-state intervention. We uncover a verification-specific hidden-state signal: in step-wise verification, a verifier's tendency to accept or reject a solution step is encoded near the boundary of the corresponding verification paragraph. Exploiting this signal, we show that hidden-state steering can directly modulate verifier strictness without fine-tuning. However, uniform steering induces a trade-off between error detection and correctness certification. To address this, we propose VerifySteer, which exploits latent correctness signals for sample-level routing and selectively intervenes on paragraph boundaries. Experiments on ProcessBench and Hard2Verify show that VerifySteer outperforms prompt optimization and activation steering baselines, and is competitive with self-consistency while requiring 4-7x less inference compute. VerifySteer is also complementary to verification fine-tuning, providing further gains on top of fine-tuned verifiers. The code is available at https://github.com/YefanZhou/VerifySteer.

</details>


### [153] [Correcting Stochastic Update Bias in Preconditioned Language Model Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20756)
*Nikhil Nayak,Julia White,Urchade Zaratiana,Kelton Zhang,Henrijs Princis,Dhruv Atreja,Henry Fawcett,Matthew Thomas,George Hurn-Maloney,Ash Lewis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种单批次偏差校正框架，通过交叉拟合预条件估计和方差校正逆运算，解决了预条件优化器中梯度-预条件子耦合偏差和非线性逆运算偏差两个有限样本偏差问题，在AdamW、Sophia和Shampoo上分别将Qwen2.5-0.5B的预训练困惑度降低了0.15、0.07和0.11 nats。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预条件优化器（如AdamW、Sophia、Shampoo）是语言模型训练的核心，但其随机更新规则通常被视为种群预条件下降的直接近似。然而，这种观点忽略了两个有限样本偏差：一是梯度与预条件子来自同一个小批量导致的耦合偏差；二是即使预条件子估计无偏，其逆或逆根因非线性变换也会引入偏差。这些偏差阻碍了优化器性能的进一步提升。

Method: 提出单批次偏差校正框架，包含两个组件：(1) 交叉拟合预条件估计（Cross-fitted preconditioning），将小批量划分为独立微批次组，分别估计梯度和预条件子以消除耦合偏差；(2) 方差校正逆运算（Variance-corrected inversion），利用微批次变异性减去Delta方法的领先阶偏差项，校正非线性逆运算的偏差。该框架统一适用于对角动量（AdamW）、对角曲率（Sophia）和矩阵预条件（Shampoo）方法。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-0.5B模型上，偏差校正使AdamW、Sophia和Shampoo的预训练损失分别降低0.15、0.07和0.11 nats；在混合质量预训练和下游指令微调上，效果一致为中性到正面。

Conclusion: 偏差校正是减少有限样本更新偏差、提升预条件优化器性能的实用机制，为语言模型训练提供了即插即用的改进方案。

Abstract: Preconditioned optimizers are central to language model training, but their stochastic update rules are usually treated as direct approximations to population preconditioned descent. We show that this view misses two finite-sample biases. First, the gradient and preconditioner are typically estimated from the same minibatch, introducing gradient--preconditioner coupling bias. Second, even when the preconditioner estimate is unbiased, its inverse or inverse-root is generally biased because inversion is nonlinear. We propose a single-batch bias-correction framework that addresses both effects: cross-fitted preconditioning estimates the numerator and preconditioner from independent microbatch groups, while variance-corrected inversion uses microbatch variability to subtract the leading delta-method bias term. The framework applies to diagonal moment, diagonal curvature, and matrix preconditioning methods, instantiated in AdamW, Sophia, and Shampoo. Bias correction reduces held-out pretraining loss on Qwen2.5-0.5B by $0.15$, $0.07$, and $0.11$ nats, respectively; the effects on mixed-quality pretraining and downstream instruction tuning are consistently neutral-to-positive. Together, these results establish bias correction as a practical mechanism for reducing finite-sample update bias and improving the performance of preconditioned optimizers.

</details>


### [154] [Cumulative Meta-Learning from Active Learning Queries for Robustness to Spurious Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20771)
*Kin Whye Chew,Jingxian Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CAML（累积主动元学习）框架，通过主动学习查询的样本来元学习先验（归纳偏置），解决深度学习中虚假相关导致模型依赖无关模式的问题。CAML将每轮主动学习视为元学习任务，利用标注集作为元训练数据、查询批次作为元测试数据，并通过累积归纳偏置捕捉轮次间的序列依赖。理论证明该累积公式引入了交互项，实验表明在多个虚假相关基准上显著提升少数群体准确率（最高提升29.9%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实数据集中的虚假相关导致机器学习模型依赖无关模式，损害可靠性、泛化性和公平性。主动学习虽有潜力通过查询信息样本区分核心特征与虚假特征，但标准方法仅将查询样本追加到标注集（仅更新似然项），在深度学习中这些信息样本的影响会被更大的标注集稀释或被过参数化模型记忆，无法有效引导模型学习正确归纳偏置。

Method: 提出CAML框架：1) 将每轮主动学习建模为元学习任务，当前标注集作为元训练数据指导模型适应，新查询批次作为元测试数据评估泛化；2) 与传统元学习假设任务独立同分布不同，CAML通过维护一个累积归纳偏置（cumulative inductive bias）来利用主动学习轮次之间的序列依赖关系，该偏置被逐步精炼；3) 理论上证明了累积公式引入交互项，耦合早期元学到的归纳偏置与后期查询目标，捕捉标准元学习缺失的依赖关系。

Result: 在多个虚假相关基准上显著提升少数群体准确率：Dominoes提升27.8%，Waterbirds提升29.9%，SpuCo提升14.3%，CivilComments提升24.0%。

Conclusion: CAML通过将主动学习查询的样本用于元学习累积归纳偏置，有效解决了标准主动学习仅更新似然项的局限性，避免了信息样本被稀释或记忆的问题。理论分析和实验验证均表明该方法能显著提升模型在虚假相关数据上的少数群体准确率，增强模型的可靠性和公平性。

Abstract: Spurious correlations in real-world datasets cause machine learning models to rely on irrelevant patterns, undermining reliability, generalization, and fairness. Active learning offers a promising way to address this failure mode by querying informative samples that distinguish core features from spurious ones. However, standard active-learning methods simply append queried examples to the labeled set, effectively updating only the likelihood term. In deep learning regimes, the influence of these informative samples can be diluted by the larger labeled set and memorized by overparameterized models. We propose Cumulative Active Meta-Learning (CAML), an active-learning framework that uses queried examples to meta-learn the prior, or inductive bias, governing how the model adapts. CAML casts each active-learning round as a meta-learning task: the current labeled set serves as meta-train data for adaptation, while the newly queried batch serves as meta-test data for evaluating generalization. Unlike conventional meta-learning, which treats tasks as independent and identically distributed, CAML exploits the sequential dependence between active-learning rounds by maintaining a cumulative inductive bias that is progressively refined. Theoretically, we show that this cumulative formulation introduces interaction terms that couple earlier meta-learned inductive biases with later query-induced objectives, capturing dependencies absent from standard meta-learning. Empirically, CAML improves minority-group accuracy across spurious-correlation benchmarks and acquisition strategies, with gains of up to 27.8% on Dominoes, 29.9% on Waterbirds, 14.3% on SpuCo, and 24.0% on CivilComments.

</details>


### [155] [Learning to Think in Physics: Breaking Shortcut Learning in Scientific Diffusion via Representation Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20780)
*Haozhe Jia,Pengyu Yin,Wenshuo Chen,Shaofeng Liang,Lei Wang,Bowen Tian,Xiucheng Wang,Nanqian Jia,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REPA-P是一种无教师、架构无关的框架，通过将扩散模型的中间特征与物理状态对齐（使用一阶原理残差），在零额外推理开销下，加速收敛最高2倍，降低物理残差最高66.4%，提升分布外鲁棒性最高49.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息扩散模型通常仅在最终输出上施加PDE约束，导致中间表示不受约束，在边界条件变化时容易产生捷径学习（shortcut learning）问题。

Method: 在选定层附加轻量级1×1投影头，将隐藏激活解码为物理量，并在训练过程中施加PDE残差损失；推理时丢弃这些投影头，实现零额外开销。该方法无需教师模型，且架构无关（支持U-Net和Diffusion Transformer）。

Result: 在Darcy流、拓扑优化、静电势、湍流通道流四个PDE任务上，收敛加速最高2倍，物理残差降低最高66.4%，分布外鲁棒性提升最高49.3%。消融实验表明仅监督少量中间层即可获得大部分收益，且与输出级物理损失互补。

Conclusion: REPA-P通过在训练阶段对中间特征施加物理约束，有效提升了物理信息扩散模型的收敛速度、预测精度和泛化能力，且不增加推理成本。

Abstract: Physics-informed diffusion models typically enforce PDE constraints only on final outputs, leaving intermediate representations unconstrained and prone to shortcut learning under shifted boundary conditions. We introduce **REPA-P**, a teacher-free, architecture-agnostic framework that aligns intermediate features with physical states using first-principles residuals. REPA-P attaches lightweight $1{\times}1$ projection heads to selected layers, decodes hidden activations into physical quantities, and applies PDE residual losses during training. These heads are discarded at inference, introducing **zero overhead**. Across four PDE tasks, including Darcy flow, topology optimization, electrostatic potential, and turbulent channel flow, REPA-P accelerates convergence by up to $2{\times}$, reduces physics residuals by up to $66.4\%$, and improves out-of-distribution robustness by up to $49.3\%$, with consistent gains on both U-Net and Diffusion Transformer backbones. Ablations show that supervising a small set of intermediate layers captures most benefits and complements output-level physics losses. Code is available at [https://github.com/Hxxxz0/REPA-P](https://github.com/Hxxxz0/REPA-P).

</details>


### [156] [Causal Machine Learning Is Not a Panacea: A Roadmap for Observational Causal Inference in Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20782)
*Donna Tjandra,Trenton Chang,Sonali Parbhoo,Rajesh Ranganath,Andre Kurepa Waschka,William Mitchell,Maggie Makar,Shalmali Joshi,Finale Doshi-Velez,Leo Anthony Celi,Jenna Wiens*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套将因果机器学习应用于观察性临床数据的路线图，强调需严格评估因果假设的有效性，否则可能导致有偏误或误导性的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型观察性临床数据集日益丰富，而随机对照试验实施困难，促使研究者希望使用因果机器学习从观察性数据中进行因果推断，但该方法的局限性尚未被充分认识。

Method: 提出一个应用路线图，强调评估数据中因果假设（如无混淆性、 positivity、一致性）的重要性，并针对临床专家和机器学习从业者分别提供负责任的实践指导。

Result: 观察到因果机器学习在不同学科中的局限性普遍未被充分认识，这种知识共享的不足可能影响研究发现的有效性。

Conclusion: 因果机器学习是生成因果假设的有力工具，但必须满足因果假设并证明建模选择的合理性；作者提供了一个模板来增强因果分析的严谨性和可解释性。

Abstract: Objective: The growing availability of large-scale observational clinical datasets and challenges in conducting randomized controlled trials have spurred enthusiasm in using causal machine learning (ML) for causal inference in observational data. We present a roadmap for applying causal ML to observational data. Materials and methods: We outline the importance of assessing validity assumptions within available data and applying causal ML responsibly for clinical experts using causal ML and ML practitioners with limited clinical expertise. Observations: Despite advances in causal ML, its limitations remain largely under-appreciated across disciplines. This gap in shared knowledge may impact the validity of findings. Discussion: Causal assumptions must be satisfied and modeling choices justified. Otherwise, these approaches risk producing biased or misleading results, with consequences for clinical research and patient care. Conclusion: Causal ML can be a powerful tool for generating causal hypotheses. We provide a template to strengthen the rigor and interpretability of causal analyses.

</details>


### [157] [Beyond Numerical Features: CNN-Driven Algorithm Selection via Contour Plots for Continuous Black-Box Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20797)
*Yiliang Yuan,Xiang Shi,Mustafa Misir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于等高线图视觉表征的算法选择方法，利用CNN回归器预测优化器性能，在BBOB 2009基准上超越单一最优求解器，且无需手工特征工程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续优化中的算法选择方法主要依赖数值描述符（如ELA特征或Deep-ELA嵌入），缺乏对景观空间结构的视觉表征利用。本文旨在探索一种互补的表征方式——等高线图可视化，以简化特征工程并提升算法选择效果。

Method: 将探测到的优化景观转换为等高线图，使用CNN回归器对每个实例的多个等高线视图（堆叠或逐视图编码后聚合）进行建模，预测每个求解器的性能，从而选择预测最佳值对应的求解器。

Result: 在BBOB 2009单目标协议上，所提选择器显著优于单一最优求解器（SBS），并与基于特征的方法相当。在DeepELA设置下的双目标评估中，使用窗口化等高线视图的图像方法同样具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 简单的视觉模型能够利用探测景观中的空间结构进行算法选择，无需手工设计的ELA特征，为算法选择提供了一种新的有效表征途径。

Abstract: The present paper introduces a new representation-driven approach to per-instance algorithm selection, applied to black-box optimization, for automatically choosing the most promising solver from a fixed portfolio. Prior work in continuous optimization largely relies on numerical descriptors, including Exploratory Landscape Analysis features and learned embeddings such as Deep-ELA. This work studies a complementary representation: contour-map visualizations of probed landscapes. A CNN regressor takes multiple instance-specific contour views (stacked or encoded per view and aggregated) and predicts per-solver performance, enabling selection by the predicted best value. On the standard BBOB 2009 single-objective protocol, the resulting selectors significantly outperform the single best solver (SBS) and are competitive with feature-based baselines. A subsequent bi-objective evaluation under the DeepELA setting further indicates that the same image-based principle can be competitive when using windowed contour views. Overall, the results suggest that simple vision models can exploit spatial structure in probed landscapes for algorithm selection without handcrafted ELA features.

</details>


### [158] [Most Transformer Modifications Still Do Not Transfer at 1-3B: A 2020-2026 Update to Narang et al. (2021) with Downstream Evaluation and a Noise Floor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20798)
*Yang Zhao,Jiahao Lu,Bin Huang,Guhua Zhang,Jie Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在1.2B和3B参数规模下，对20种2021年后的Transformer改进进行严格受控实验，发现大多数改进无法迁移；仅两种在1.2B时通过Bonferroni校正，其中一种在3B时训练不稳定；注意力输出改进的损失-下游性能差距显著扩大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Narang等人（2021）的结论——大多数Transformer改进无法迁移——是否在更大的参数规模（1-3B）和下游评估标准下仍然成立，并量化损失与下游性能之间的差距。

Method: 在严格的iso-data、iso-compute、iso-recipe控制条件下，对20种2021年后的Transformer改进在1.2B和3B参数规模下进行多种子基线噪声测试，并以CLIMB-12下游评估为主要指标。

Result: 20种改进中仅两种在1.2B时通过Bonferroni校正；其中一种在3B时训练不稳定；注意力输出改进的损失-下游性能差距显著（2-3%损失差异对应6-16 CLIMB得分下降）。

Conclusion: 在1-3B参数规模下，噪声基线报告、下游评估和跨规模稳定性测试应成为架构比较的必备条件。

Abstract: Narang et al. (2021) evaluated 40+ Transformer modifications at T5-base scale and concluded that most did not transfer. Five years later, the typical working regime has moved to 1-3B parameters, downstream evaluation has replaced pretraining perplexity, and a substantially different catalogue of modifications has emerged. We revisit their question by testing 20 post-2021 Transformer modifications at 1.2B and 3B under strict iso-data, iso-compute, iso-recipe control, with a multi-seed baseline noise floor and CLIMB-12 downstream evaluation as the primary metric. The central finding reproduces theirs at this curated set: most modifications do not transfer. Of the 20 modifications, only two clear Bonferroni correction at 1.2B; one of those two further fails to train stably at 3B under the shared recipe. We also find that the loss-downstream gap reported by Tay et al. (2023) enlarges several-fold for attention-output modifications: two significant failures converge to within 2-3% of baseline validation loss yet drop 6-16 CLIMB-points. We conclude that noise-floor reporting, downstream evaluation, and cross-scale stability testing are now prerequisites for architecture comparisons at 1-3B.

</details>


### [159] [Tunable MAGMAX: Preference-Aware Model Merging for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20803)
*Kei Hiroshima,Kento Uchida,Shinichi Shirakawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Tunable MAGMAX框架，通过在模型合并中引入偏好向量控制每个任务向量的元素选择数量，实现对持续学习中任务性能的可调节控制，并能自动构建偏好向量以适应不同部署环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法主要关注所有任务的平均性能，无法根据不同部署环境或用户偏好来灵活调整各任务的具体表现。

Method: 提出Tunable MAGMAX模型合并框架，引入偏好向量控制各任务向量在合并时被选取的元素数量；并提出利用少量目标环境数据和训练任务数据自动构建适当偏好向量的方法。

Result: 在持续学习基准任务上，Tunable MAGMAX能有效控制各任务性能，并成功使合并模型适应不同目标环境，性能达到或超过基线方法。

Conclusion: Tunable MAGMAX通过偏好感知的模型合并，为将持续学习模型部署到不同任务偏好各异的环境中提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) aims to train models sequentially on multiple tasks while mitigating catastrophic forgetting of previously learned knowledge. Recent advances in large pre-trained models (LPMs) and model merging techniques, such as MAGMAX, have demonstrated effective CL performance by combining task-specific parameters. However, existing methods primarily focus on average performance across all tasks and do not adequately address how to construct models accommodating different deployment environments or varying user preferences. This paper proposes a model merging framework, termed Tunable MAGMAX, which enables preference-aware control of task-specific performance in CL. Our method introduces a preference vector that controls the number of elements selected from each task vector during model merging, allowing us to adjust the merged model performance according to their deployment needs. We further propose a method for automatically constructing appropriate preference vectors by leveraging small amounts of target environment data and datasets from model training tasks, thereby eliminating the need for manual specification. The experimental result on CL benchmark tasks demonstrates that Tunable MAGMAX effectively controls task-wise performance and successfully adapts merged models to various target environments. The proposed Tunable MAGMAX achieves superior or comparable performance to baseline methods, making it a practical solution for deploying CL models to various environments where the preferences of each task performance differ.

</details>


### [160] [Markovian Circuit Tracing for Transformer State Dynamic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20824)
*Abdullah X*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出马尔可夫电路追踪（MCT）诊断管线，在合成隐马尔可夫模型（HMM）任务上检验Transformer激活是否包含粗粒度状态转移结构。实验发现小型因果Transformer能学习接近贝叶斯最优的预测器，残差激活包含部分贝叶斯信念信息，状态强制干预能有效恢复HMM反事实目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多序列计算更适合通过内部状态转移来研究，而非孤立的局部电路。当前缺少受控基准来检验Transformer激活中是否存在可解释的粗粒度状态转移结构。

Method: 提出MCT诊断管线，使用6类HMM合成任务（已知隐状态、转移矩阵、贝叶斯信念向量等），训练小型因果Transformer，从残差激活中提取状态抽象，并通过状态强制干预（patching recovered-state centroid）验证结构。

Result: 1) Transformer平均超额损失0.0138；2) 残差激活包含部分贝叶斯信念信息；3) 状态抽象在持久态和低状态数设置下恢复转移动态最强，在模糊发射和六状态设置下较弱；4) 状态强制干预将KL散度从0.1957降至0.0532，优于多种对照方法。

Conclusion: 本文贡献了一个受控基准和评估框架用于Transformer状态动态可解释性研究，MCT可作为简单的参考管线。

Abstract: Many sequence computations are easier to study as movement through internal states than as isolated local circuits. We introduce Markovian Circuit Tracing (MCT), a diagnostic pipeline for testing whether transformer activations contain coarse state-transition structure. The benchmark uses synthetic Hidden Markov Model (HMM) tasks where latent states, transition matrices, Bayesian belief vectors, Bayes-optimal predictions, and forced-state counterfactual targets are known exactly. Across six HMM families and three seeds per family, tiny causal transformers learn near-Bayes next-token predictors, with mean excess loss over Bayes of 0.0138. Residual activations contain partial Bayesian belief information in this controlled synthetic benchmark. State abstractions extracted from these activations recover coarse transition signal, strongest in persistent and lower-state regimes, and weaker in ambiguous-emission and six-state regimes. The clearest result comes from state forcing. Patching a recovered-state centroid reduces KL to the exact HMM counterfactual target from 0.1957 in the unpatched model to 0.0532 on average, beating wrong-state, mean-activation, random-activation, and shuffled-label controls. The contribution is a controlled benchmark and evaluation framework for transformer state-dynamics interpretability, with MCT as a simple reference pipeline

</details>


### [161] [Finite-Time Regret Analysis of Retry-Aware Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20854)
*Bingkui Tong,Junpei Komiyama,Soichiro Nishimori,Paavo Parmas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了重试感知目标下的随机bandit算法ReMax，该算法通过最大化M次虚拟采样中的期望最大奖励来选择采样分布。针对高斯奖励和M=2的情况，刻画了最优ReMax分布并通过期望改进平衡条件证明了首个次线性遗憾界。分析揭示了次优臂的饱和行为与ReMax特有的低估效应（最优臂因不利估计被过度少采），解释了ReMax比汤普森采样更具剥削性及其遗憾分析的技术难点。实验表明：在适度低估下ReMax优于KL-UCB和TS，后验方差缩放可缓解严重低估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索重试感知目标（如pass@k和max@k）下的随机bandit算法ReMax的遗憾性质，该目标在强化学习中作为不确定性下的探索机制被提出，但其在bandit问题中的遗憾性质尚不明确。

Method: 针对高斯奖励分布和M=2的情形，通过期望改进平衡条件刻画了最优ReMax分布；理论分析分离了次优臂的饱和行为与ReMax特有的低估效应；实验验证了理论发现。

Result: 证明了ReMax的首个次线性遗憾界；揭示了低估效应导致ReMax比TS更具剥削性；实验表明ReMax在适度低估下优于KL-UCB和TS，后验方差缩放可缓解严重低估。

Conclusion: ReMax在重试感知目标下具有理论保证和实际竞争力，但其低估效应需要谨慎处理，后验方差缩放是一种有效的缓解策略。

Abstract: We study a stochastic bandit algorithm motivated by retry-aware objectives that value the best outcome among multiple attempts, such as pass@$k$ and max@$k$. Given a posterior over arm values, ReMax chooses a sampling distribution that maximizes the posterior expected maximum reward over $M$ virtual draws. Although this objective was introduced in reinforcement learning as an exploration mechanism under uncertainty, its regret properties in bandit problems have remained unclear. For Gaussian rewards and the first nontrivial case $M=2$, we characterize the optimal ReMax distribution through an expected-improvement balance condition and prove the first sublinear regret bound for ReMax. Our analysis separates the usual saturation behavior of suboptimal arms from a ReMax-specific underestimation effect, in which the optimal arm may be sampled too rarely after an unfavorable estimate. This explains why ReMax can be more exploitative than Thompson sampling (TS) and why its regret analysis is technically delicate. Experiments support this picture: ReMax often outperforms KL-UCB and Thompson sampling under mild underestimation, while posterior-variance scaling empirically mitigates severe underestimation.

</details>


### [162] [Multi-Step Likelihood-Ratio Correction for Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20865)
*Deokgyu Yoon,Hyungkyu Kang,Joongkyu Lee,Byeongchan Kim,Gyungin Shin,Sungrae Park,Min-hwan Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出N步前向迹策略优化（NFPO），通过在PPO替代目标中引入累积似然比来桥接PPO与精确策略梯度目标，实现偏差-方差权衡的灵活控制，理论证明其策略改进界更紧，实验验证其在推理基准上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPO替代目标是局部的，依赖于精确策略梯度的局部近似，虽然通过重要性采样降低了方差从而提升稳定性，但也引入了结构偏差，需要通过信任区域机制来控制。现有方法在偏差-方差权衡上缺乏灵活且 principled 的控制机制。

Method: 提出N步前向迹（N-step forward trace），利用后续N-1个token的累积似然比来增强PPO替代目标；在此基础上设计NFPO算法，将其集成到掩码策略梯度框架中，实现从PPO替代目标到精确策略梯度目标的连续桥接。

Result: 理论分析表明，选择适当的N时，所提目标函数比标准PPO替代目标具有更紧的策略改进界。在综合推理基准上的实验一致性地提升了性能，支持了理论发现。

Conclusion: NFPO提供了一种 principled 的机制来控制偏差-方差权衡，通过N步前向迹实现了PPO与精确策略梯度之间的连续过渡，在推理任务上取得了有效改进。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) plays a pivotal role in improving the reasoning ability of large language models. However, widely used PPO surrogate objectives are fundamentally local, as they rely on a local approximation of the exact policy gradient objective. While this approximation improves stability by reducing the variance induced by importance sampling, it also introduces structural bias into the surrogate objective, which must be controlled through trust region mechanisms. In this work, we introduce the $N$-step forward trace, which augments the PPO surrogate objective using the cumulative likelihood ratio of the next $N-1$ tokens. Building on this idea, we propose $N$-Step Forward-Trace Policy Optimization (NFPO), a practical RLVR algorithm that integrates the $N$-step forward trace into the masked policy gradient framework. NFPO provides a continuous bridge between the PPO surrogate objective and the exact policy gradient objective, offering a principled mechanism for controlling the bias-variance trade-off. Our theoretical analysis shows that, with an appropriate choice of $N$, the proposed objective yields a tighter policy-improvement bound than the standard PPO surrogate. Experiments on comprehensive reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that NFPO consistently improves performance, supporting our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [163] [LOSCAR-SGD: Local SGD with Communication-Computation Overlap and Delay-Corrected Sparse Model Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20866)
*Yassine Maziane,Ammar Mahran,Artavazd Maranjyan,Peter Richtárik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LOSCAR-SGD，一种结合通信压缩、本地训练和通信-计算重叠的分布式学习方法，并首次为该组合提供了收敛理论保证，实验验证了其在非凸优化中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式学习中通信是主要瓶颈，现有方法分别采用通信压缩、本地训练和通信-计算重叠来降低开销，但缺乏同时结合这三种技术的理论分析。

Method: 提出LOSCAR-SGD，支持异构计算环境下不同工作节点执行不同数量的本地步骤，仅通信模型坐标的稀疏子集，并在通信过程中继续优化；核心是延迟校正合并规则，在合并延迟同步信息时保留重叠阶段取得的进展。

Result: 给出了光滑非凸目标下的收敛保证，揭示了稀疏度、重叠和worker异构性对收敛速率的影响；实验表明通信-计算重叠减少了训练时间，延迟校正合并优于朴素覆盖策略。

Conclusion: 这是首次为通信压缩、本地训练和通信-计算重叠三者的组合提供理论分析，填补了该领域的理论空白。

Abstract: Communication is a major bottleneck in distributed learning, especially in large-scale settings and in federated learning environments with slow links. Three standard ways to reduce this cost are communication compression, local training, and communication-computation overlap. Methods that combine these ingredients are used in practice and have been found to be effective for large-scale training, but there is little theory for methods that combine all three. We study a heterogeneous-compute setting in which different workers may take different numbers of local steps, and we propose LOSCAR-SGD, a Local SGD method that communicates only a sparse subset of model coordinates and continues optimizing while communication is in flight. A key ingredient is a delay-corrected merge rule that incorporates delayed synchronized information without discarding the progress made during the overlap phase. We give convergence guarantees for smooth non-convex objectives and show how sparsity, overlap, and worker heterogeneity affect the rate. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first theory for this combination of ingredients. Experiments further show that communication-computation overlap reduces training time and that the delay-corrected merge outperforms naive overwriting.

</details>


### [164] [CIG: Exploration via Conditional Information Gain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20878)
*Tim Joseph,Marcus Fechner,Philipp Stegmaier,Karam Daaboul,J. Marius Zöllner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出条件信息增益（CIG）奖励，通过集成分歧核的对数行列式目标及其Cholesky分解，在深度模型中同时融合生命周期经验与轨迹内前缀条件，实现可扩展的因果逐步探索奖励，在离散和连续控制任务中优于或匹配现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有探索方法中，生命周期奖励忽视轨迹内冗余，轨迹内奖励丢弃生命周期进展，混合方法依赖启发式权重或不可扩展的高斯过程，而轨迹级信息增益在深度模型中难以处理。

Method: 推导CIG奖励作为轨迹级信息增益的可处理替代：定义集成分歧核的对数行列式目标，利用Cholesky分解得到因果逐步奖励，同时保留回放缓冲区（生命周期）和 rollout 前缀（轨迹内）两个条件集，且可扩展至高维状态空间。在基于模型的设置中实例化。

Result: 在MiniGrid（离散）和OGBench（连续控制）共12个任务上，包括干净环境和随机干扰物环境，CIG优于或匹配现有探索方法，且对随机干扰物具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CIG通过可扩展的因果逐步奖励有效融合了生命周期与轨迹内两种条件信息，是一种实用的深度探索方法。

Abstract: Intrinsic rewards for exploration in reinforcement learning condition on different contexts: lifelong rewards score each transition against accumulated experience but ignore within-rollout redundancy; episodic rewards penalize intra-trajectory repetition but discard lifetime progress. Hybrid methods combine both signals through heuristic weights or require Gaussian-process dynamics that do not scale beyond low-dimensional state spaces. Trajectory-level information gain decomposes into per-step terms that condition on the replay buffer and rollout prefix simultaneously, but remains intractable for deep models. We derive the Conditional Information Gain (CIG) reward as a tractable surrogate: a log-determinant objective over an ensemble disagreement kernel whose Cholesky factorization yields causal per-step rewards that retain both conditioning sets while scaling to high-dimensional state spaces. We instantiate CIG in a model-based setting, where rollouts are short and within-rollout corrections remain largely unexplored. Across twelve tasks spanning discrete (MiniGrid) and continuous control (OGBench), in both clean and stochastic-distractor settings, CIG outperforms or matches prior exploration methods while remaining robust to stochastic distractors.

</details>


### [165] [NeighborDiv: Training-free Zero-shot Generalist Graph Anomaly Detection via Neighbor Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20879)
*Kaifeng Wei,Teng Liu,Liang Dong,Xiubo Liang,Yuke Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NeighborDiv，一种无需训练的通用图异常检测框架。该框架跳出传统的节点-邻居一致性范式，转向邻居-邻居多样性范式，通过衡量节点邻居集内部结构的分散性作为异常检测信号，在多个标准评估范式下取得了最优性能，且消除了训练集依赖性和性能波动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用图异常检测方法过度依赖节点-邻居一致性范式，存在训练流程复杂、依赖大量训练数据、计算成本高以及跨域泛化不稳定的问题。

Method: 提出Neighbor-to-Neighbor Diversity Paradigm（邻居到邻居多样性范式），通过计算邻居间特征相似度的方差来量化邻居多样性，以此作为异常检测的独立判别信号，无需模型训练。

Result: 在SDIT范式下，平均AUC提升10.25%，平均AP提升17.78%；在UMDT范式下，AUC提升6.89%，AP提升9.58%。所有数据集上性能零波动。

Conclusion: NeighborDiv通过转向邻居多样性范式，实现了一种轻量级、无训练依赖、高泛化能力的通用图异常检测框架，消除了传统方法的训练集依赖性和性能不稳定性。

Abstract: Graph Anomaly Detection (GAD) is increasingly shifting to Generalist GAD (GGAD) for cross-domain "one-for-all" detection, but existing GGAD methods predominantly rely on the neighbor consistency principle, falling into the \textbf{Node-to-Neighbor Consistency Paradigm} for anomaly quantification. These methods suffer from complex training pipelines, heavy training data dependency, high computational costs, and unstable cross-domain generalization. To address these limitations, we propose NeighborDiv, a training-free generalist graph anomaly detection framework based on neighbor diversity. Departing from the dominant Node-to-Neighbor Consistency Paradigm, we shift the focus to the \textbf{Neighbor-to-Neighbor Diversity Paradigm}, and uncover that the internal structural dispersion of a node's neighbor set is a powerful, independently discriminative anomaly signal. We quantify neighbor diversity via the variance of inter-neighbor feature similarities, which captures how a node organizes its local graph environment, and operates independently of conventional node-to-neighbor consistency frameworks. Extensive experiments under two standard GGAD evaluation paradigms show NeighborDiv achieves state-of-the-art performance, with relative gains of 10.25% in average AUC and 17.78% in average AP over the second-best baseline under Single-Domain Independent Training (SDIT), and 6.89%/9.58% in AUC/AP under Unified Multi-Domain Training (UMDT), respectively. Notably, NeighborDiv yields zero performance volatility across all datasets, eliminating training-set dependency and establishing a lightweight and highly practical GGAD framework.

</details>


### [166] [Training distribution determines the ceiling of drug-blind cancer sensitivity prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20885)
*Taekyung Heo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 药物敏感性预测的停滞源于评估指标（global Pearson r）被药物间效力差异主导，而单药Pearson r显示药物表征并无优势；机制分层训练和响应匹配可恢复预测增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准肿瘤学需从分子谱预测药物敏感性，但药物盲敏感度预测尽管药物表征日趋复杂却陷入停滞，需探究其根本原因。

Method: 分析global Pearson r与per-drug Pearson r的差异；对比药物特征与仅细胞特征的效果；将作用机制（MoA）作为特征或训练分布约束进行对照实验。

Result: 全局指标被药物间效力差异主导；单药指标下药物编码无提升；MoA作为特征无效，但作为分层训练（机制分层）显著提升靶向激酶抑制剂的单药r值。

Conclusion: 药物盲预测停滞是指标假象而非表征瓶颈；机制分层训练与响应匹配可恢复主要预测增益来源。

Abstract: Precision oncology requires predicting which drugs will suppress a specific tumor from its molecular profile, but drug-blind sensitivity prediction has plateaued despite increasingly complex drug representations. Here we show that this stagnation reflects a metric artifact rather than a representational bottleneck. The standard benchmark, global Pearson r, is dominated by between-drug potency differences that a trivial drug-mean predictor captures without any cell-specific learning. Per-drug Pearson r, which isolates within-drug cell ranking, reveals that no drug encoding improves over cell-only features across four independent datasets. A controlled experiment channeling mechanism-of-action identity as either a drug feature or a training-distribution constraint identifies the cause. Supplying MoA as a feature yields negligible benefit, whereas using it to stratify training raises per-drug r substantially for targeted kinase inhibitors, because pan-cancer co-training suppresses pathway-specific sensitivity signals. Mechanism-stratified training and response matching from pilot observations provide two deployable strategies that together recover the principal sources of predictive gain in drug-blind sensitivity prediction.

</details>


### [167] [A Deployment Audit of Release-Side Risk in Conformal Triage under Prevalence Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20956)
*Chengze Li,Xiao Liu,Hanrong Zhang,Haiyang Peng,Yanghao Ruan,Huanhuan Ma,Chunyu Miao,Qichao Zhou,Xiangrong Qi,Philip Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种泄漏感知部署审计方法，用于评估分布偏移下合规分诊系统中事件阳性患者被错误释放（未经审查）的安全风险，并揭示低审查率可能掩盖的安全隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的合规分诊系统在患病率偏移下，通常仅关注边际覆盖率和人工审查率等汇总指标，但这些指标无法捕捉到真正经历目标事件的患者是否未经审查就被释放这一安全关键问题，存在安全隐患。

Method: 提出一种泄漏感知部署审计方法，将目标受试者划分为三个非重叠角色：患病率校正、合规校准和保留的释放安全性评估；通过这种分离直接评估释放安全性，包括被清除的事件阳性患者数量、校准所需事件标签是否充足以及安全-审查权衡的转移情况。

Result: 在回顾性NSCLC试点中应用该方法发现：经过患病率校正后，池化合规分支通过释放更多患者来降低审查率，其中部分为事件阳性患者；类别合规分支作为稀缺性诊断工具，显示试点中可用的事件标签数量不足以认证安全的低审查释放。

Conclusion: 低审查率可能具有误导性，仅仅降低审查率而不考虑事件阳性患者的泄漏风险是不安全的；所提出的审计方法能够有效诊断合规分诊系统在患病率偏移下的实际安全性。

Abstract: Conformal triage converts predictive scores into deployment actions that either release a case, flag it for urgent attention, or defer it to human review. Under prevalence shift, however, the usual summaries of marginal coverage and human-review rate can miss the safety-critical question of whether patients who truly experience the target event are released without review. To address this gap, we introduce a leakage-aware deployment audit for release-side conformal triage. It first assigns target subjects to three non-overlapping roles: prevalence correction, conformal calibration, and held-out release-safety evaluation. This separation then lets the audit evaluate release directly: how many event-positive patients are cleared without review, whether the pilot has enough event labels for calibration, and how the safety-review trade-off shifts. Applying this audit to a retrospective NSCLC pilot shows why lower review can be misleading: after prevalence correction, the pooled conformal branch lowers review by releasing more patients, some of whom are event-positive. Within the audit, the classwise branch acts as a scarcity diagnostic: the pilot has too few event labels to certify safe low-review release.

</details>


### [168] [A Sharper Picture of Generalization in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20988)
*Paul Lintilhac,Sair Shaikh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从傅里叶谱角度研究Transformer在布尔域上的泛化行为，通过PAC-Bayes理论而非Rademacher复杂度推导泛化界，证明稀疏低阶谱可实现低锐度构造并产生非平凡泛化界，最后通过实验和 mechanistic interpretability 验证理论构造的现实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作（Edelman et al., 2022; Trauger and Tewari, 2024）使用Rademacher复杂度推导Transformer在布尔域上的泛化界，作者希望探索是否可以通过PAC-Bayes理论获得更好的泛化界，并理解傅里叶谱结构对泛化的影响。

Method: 1) 证明对于任意稀疏度不超过上下文长度的布尔函数，存在平坦最小值（flat minima）实现该函数；2) 对理想化的低锐度学习器应用PAC-Bayes界，推导非平凡泛化界；3) 进行实证评估和mechanistic interpretability研究以验证理论构造在真实Transformer中的现实性。

Result: 1) 稀疏且集中在低阶成分上的傅里叶谱能够实现具有良好泛化性质的低锐度构造；2) 获得了非平凡（non-vacuous）的泛化界；3) 实验和mechanistic interpretability分析支持了理论构造在真实Transformer中的可行性。

Conclusion: PAC-Bayes理论是分析Transformer泛化的有效工具，傅里叶谱的稀疏性和低阶性是与泛化能力紧密相关的关键结构特征。

Abstract: We study transformers' generalization behavior on boolean domains from the perspective of the Fourier Spectra of their target functions. In contrast to prior work (Edelman et al., 2022; Trauger and Tewari, 2024), which derived generalization bounds from Rademacher complexity, we investigate the feasibility of obtaining generalization bounds via PAC-Bayes theory. We show that sparse spectra concentrated on low-degree components enable low-sharpness constructions with good generalization properties. Our idea is to show the existence of flat minima implementing any boolean function of sparsity no greater than the context length, and then apply a PAC-Bayes bound to an idealized low-sharpness learner, resulting in a non-vacuous generalization bound. We evaluate predictions empirically and conduct a mechanistic interpretability study to support the realism of our theoretical construction in real transformers.

</details>


### [169] [Modeling Temporal scRNA-seq Data with Latent Gaussian Process and Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20989)
*Mehmet Yigit Balik,Harri Lähdesmäki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合希尔伯特空间近似的高斯过程和最优传输目标的生成式框架，用于从单细胞RNA测序快照数据中推断时序过程，在插值、外推和扰动轨迹推断任务上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞RNA测序提供了单细胞分辨率的基因表达信息，但从静态快照数据中推断时序过程仍是一个基本挑战；现有基于神经微分方程和流的方法对过拟合敏感，且缺乏对生物变异性的仔细考量。

Method: 提出一个生成式框架，使用希尔伯特空间方法近似的潜在异方差高斯过程建模群体趋势；利用最优传输目标对齐生成和观察到的群体分布；通过细胞特异性潜在时间和细胞类型条件显式捕获生物异质性，以解耦时间异步性和不同细胞类型的轨迹。

Result: 在复杂的插值和外推基准测试中展示了最先进的性能，并引入了一种基于梯度的新策略用于推断扰动轨迹。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了单细胞时序推断中的过拟合和生物变异性问题，在插值、外推和扰动轨迹推断任务上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Single-cell RNA sequencing provides insights into gene expression at single-cell resolution, yet inferring temporal processes from these static snapshot measurements remains a fundamental challenge. Current approaches utilizing neural differential equations and flows are sensitive to overfitting and lack careful considerations of biological variability. In this work, we propose a generative framework that models population trends using a latent heteroscedastic Gaussian process (GP) approximated by Hilbert space methods. To address the absence of genuine cell trajectories, we leverage an optimal transport (OT) objective that aligns generated and observed population distributions. Our method explicitly captures biological heterogeneity by incorporating cell-specific latent time and cell type conditioning to disentangle temporal asynchrony and trajectories to different cell types. We demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on complex interpolation and extrapolation benchmarks and introduce a novel gradient-based strategy for inferring perturbation trajectories.

</details>


### [170] [Beyond the Bellman Recursion: A Pontryagin-Guided Framework for Non-Exponential Discounting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20996)
*Hojin Ko,Jeonggyu Huh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对非指数折扣下Bellman递归失效的问题，提出Pontryagin-Guided Direct Policy Optimization (PG-DPO)框架，通过Pontryagin最大值原理与蒙特卡洛 rollout 结合，在超几何和生存折扣基准中提升了精度和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于值函数和 Actor-Critic 的强化学习方法依赖Bellman递归，但该递归在非指数折扣（如人类偏好和生存过程中的超几何折扣）下会结构性崩溃，需要新的优化框架。

Method: 提出PG-DPO：一种变分框架，放弃递归，将Pontryagin最大值原理与蒙特卡洛rollout结合，通过Adjoint-MC投影强制实现逐点哈密顿量最大化。

Result: 在多维超几何和生存折扣基准上，PG-DPO在方程驱动求解器和基于评论家的基线方法发散的情况下，提高了准确性和稳定性。

Conclusion: 指数折扣处于乘性与时间齐次性的脆弱交集中，违反任一性质都会破坏标准动态规划；PG-DPO提供了一种避免递归崩溃的有效替代方案。

Abstract: Most value-based and actor--critic reinforcement learning methods rely on Bellman-style recursions, yet these recursions collapse under non-exponential discounting common in human preferences and survival processes. We show the breakdown is structural: exponential discounting sits at a fragile intersection of multiplicativity and time homogeneity, and violating either property breaks standard dynamic programming. To overcome this, we propose Pontryagin-Guided Direct Policy Optimization (PG-DPO), a variational framework that abandons recursion and couples the Pontryagin Maximum Principle with Monte Carlo rollouts via an Adjoint-MC projection enforcing pointwise Hamiltonian maximization. Across multi-dimensional hyperbolic and survival-discount benchmarks, PG-DPO improves accuracy and stability where equation-driven solvers and critic-based baselines diverge.

</details>


### [171] [Efficient Banzhaf-Based Data Valuation for $k$-Nearest Neighbors Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21033)
*Guangyi Zhang,Lutz Oettershagen,Lixu Wang,Aristides Gionis*
