Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对k近邻分类器中的Banzhaf值计算问题，证明了其#P-hard复杂度，并提出了动态规划框架：加权kNN的伪多项式算法O(Wkn²)和未加权kNN的线性算法O(nk²)，同时提供蒙特卡洛估计方法，实验验证了实际效率与有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据估值是机器学习中的基础挑战，基于博弈论的方法（如Banzhaf值）提供了公平的估值框架，但这些方法存在指数级计算复杂度的痛点，亟需高效算法来解决实际问题。

Method: 首先理论证明了kNN中Banzhaf值计算是#P-hard问题；然后利用kNN分类器的局部性，提出了动态规划框架：加权kNN的伪多项式算法（O(Wkn²)，W为top-k权重最大和）和未加权kNN的线性算法（O(nk²)）；同时提供蒙特卡洛估计方法。

Result: 理论层面证明了问题为#P-hard；算法层面实现了加权kNN的O(Wkn²)伪多项式复杂度和未加权kNN的O(nk²)线性复杂度（相对数据点数线性）；实验层面在真实数据集上验证了实际效率和有效性。

Conclusion: 通过利用kNN的局部性，尽管问题理论上不可解，但本文提出的动态规划算法在实际中实现了高效的数据估值，为博弈论方法在机器学习中的应用提供了可行的计算方案。

Abstract: Data valuation, the task of quantifying the contribution of individual data points to model performance, has emerged as a fundamental challenge in machine learning. Game-theoretic approaches, such as the Banzhaf value, offer principled frameworks for fair data valuation; however, they suffer from exponential computational complexity. We address this challenge by developing efficient algorithms specifically tailored for computing Banzhaf values in $k$-nearest neighbor ($k$NN) classifiers. We first establish the theoretical hardness of the problem by proving that it is \#P-hard. Despite this intractability, we exploit the locality properties of $k$NN classifiers to develop practical exact algorithms. Our main contribution is a dynamic programming framework that achieves significant computational improvements: we present a pseudo-polynomial algorithm with $O(Wkn^2)$ time complexity for weighted $k$NN classifiers, where $W$ is the maximum sum of top-$k$ weights, and a specialized algorithm for unweighted $k$NN that achieves $O(nk^2)$ time complexity, that is, linear in the number of data points. We also offer efficient Monte Carlo estimation methods. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate the practical efficiency of our approach and its effectiveness in data valuation applications.

</details>


### [172] [A Dialogue between Causal and Traditional Representation Learning: Toward Mutual Benefits in a Unified Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21058)
*Yan Li,Yuewen Sun,Shaoan Xie,Gongxu Luo,Yunlong Deng,Kun Zhang,Guangyi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一框架，将因果表示学习（CRL）与传统表示学习整合在一起，通过"任务组件"和"约束组件"两个维度来表征表示学习，并通过实验证明因果约束的有效性强烈依赖于与其配对的特定任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果表示学习与传统表示学习在术语、问题设定和评估方式上存在显著差异，导致两个领域之间缺乏有效沟通，甚至出现重复劳动。作者认为应将两者视为互补而非对立的范式，弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 提出了一个统一形式化框架，将表示学习分解为两个组成部分：任务组件（规定所学表示需要保留哪些信息）和约束组件（规定在潜空间上施加何种结构）。在此基础上，通过CausalVerse数据集进行实验，研究不同任务组件如何影响CRL方法在不同结构约束下的行为。

Result: 在CausalVerse上的实验结果表明，因果约束的有效性强烈依赖于与之配对的任务类型，不同任务组件会显著改变CRL方法的表现。

Conclusion: CRL与传统表示学习应当进行对话而非被视为分离的范式。CRL为理解结构化潜约束的有用性提供了理论工具，传统表示学习则为任务设计和目标选择提供了实践见解，两者双向受益。

Abstract: Causal representation learning (CRL) and traditional representation learning have largely developed along different trajectories. Traditional representation learning has been driven mainly by applications and empirical objectives, whereas CRL has focused more on theoretical questions, particularly identifiability. This difference in emphasis has created a gap between the two fields in terminology, problem formulation, and evaluation, limiting communication and sometimes leading to disconnected or redundant efforts. In this paper, we argue that these two fields should be brought into dialogue rather than treated as separate paradigms. To this end, we introduce a unified formulation in which the representation learning is characterized by two components: a task component, which specifies what information the learned representation is required to preserve, and a constraint component, which specifies what structure is imposed on the latent space. Under this formulation, the benefits run in both directions. CRL provides theoretical tools for understanding when structured latent constraints are useful or necessary, while traditional representation learning offers practical insights on task design and objective choice that can improve the development of CRL methods. To illustrate this interaction, we experimentally study how different task components affect the behavior of CRL methods under different structured constraints. Results on CausalVerse show that the effectiveness of causal constraints depends strongly on the tasks with which they are paired.

</details>


### [173] [Divide et Calibra: Multiclass Local Calibration via Vector Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21060)
*Cesare Barbera,Lorenzo Perini,Giovanni De Toni,Andrea Passerini,Andrea Pugnana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于向量量化的组合式多类校准方法，通过共享码字依赖因子构建区域特定校准图，解决了全局校准假设误差同质和局部校准信息丢失的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险场景中，机器学习模型需要准确且良好校准，但现有方法存在不足：全局校准假设校准误差在潜在空间同质分布，局部校准依赖降维导致信息丢失。

Method: 采用组合式方法，通过向量量化对表示空间进行结构化分区，并利用索引化的狄利克雷浓度参数化实现跨区域参数共享，从而构建区域特定的校准映射。

Result: 在基准数据集上，该方法在局部校准方面取得显著改进，同时保持具有竞争力的全局校准和预测性能。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够学习异构校准图，即使在潜在空间的稀疏区域也能很好地泛化，有效兼顾了局部与全局校准质量。

Abstract: Accurate and well-calibrated Machine Learning (ML) models are mandatory in high-stakes settings, yet effective multiclass calibration remains challenging: global approaches assume calibration errors are homogeneous across the latent space, while local methods often rely on latent-space dimensionality reduction, which leads to information loss. To address these issues, we propose a compositional approach to multiclass calibration, where region-specific calibration maps are constructed from shared codeword-dependent factors. We instantiate this idea via Vector Quantization (VQ), which induces a structured partition of the representation space, and an indexed parameterization of Dirichlet concentrations that enables parameter sharing across regions. Our approach learns heterogeneous calibration maps that generalize well even to sparse regions of the latent space. Experiments on benchmark datasets show significant improvements in local calibration while maintaining competitive global calibration and predictive performance.

</details>


### [174] [Robust Personalized Recommendation under Hidden Confounding in MNAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21066)
*Zongyu Li,Wanting Su,Tianyu Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PUID框架，通过估计用户-物品级别的个性化灵敏度界限来克服隐藏混杂因素下的选择偏差问题，并进一步提出BPUID变体引入预训练模型作为稳定参考，在无需RCT数据的情况下显著优于全局方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逆倾向加权和双重鲁棒估计器在隐藏混杂因素下不可靠，而依赖RCT或全局灵敏度界限的方法存在成本高或忽略用户-物品交互异质性的局限。

Method: 提出PUID框架估计用户-物品级别的个性化灵敏度界限，放松全局灵敏度界限的同质性假设；采用对抗优化策略确保鲁棒性和预测精度；提出BPUID变体引入预训练模型作为稳定参考。

Result: 在三个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，该方法在隐藏混杂因素下显著优于全局方法，且无需RCT数据。

Conclusion: 通过个性化灵敏度界限和对抗优化，有效解决了隐藏混杂因素下的选择偏差问题，克服了全局方法的局限性。

Abstract: Recommender systems often rely on observational user--item interaction data, which is prone to selection bias due to users' selective interactions with items. Inverse propensity weighting and doubly robust estimators effectively mitigate selection bias under observed confounding, but are unreliable in the presence of hidden confounders. Existing approaches relying on randomized controlled trials (RCTs) or global sensitivity bounds are constrained in practice: RCTs demand costly experimental data, while global sensitivity bounds presume a uniformly bounded effect of unmeasured confounders on propensities through sensitivity analysis, thereby neglecting heterogeneity across user--item interactions. To overcome this limitation, we propose a novel framework, which estimates user--item level sensitivity bounds, thereby substantially relaxing the homogeneity assumption inherent in global sensitivity bounds named Personalized Unobserved-Confounding-aware Interaction Deconfounder (PUID). To ensure both robustness and predictive accuracy, we further develop an adversarial optimization strategy and propose a benchmark-guided variant (BPUID) that incorporates pre-trained models as stabilizing references. Extensive experiments on three real-world datasets demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms global methods under hidden confounding, without requiring RCT data.

</details>


### [175] [Towards Understanding Self-Pretraining for Sequence Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21070)
*Omar Coser,Loredana Zollo,Paolo Soda,Antonio Orvieto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过复现和消融实验，揭示了自预训练（SPT）提升Transformer序列分类性能的核心机制：SPT帮助模型学习邻近交互（将绝对位置编码转化为邻近偏置的注意力分数），而标准监督训练在随机初始化下难以学到有用的查询-键注意力模式。在简化理论框架中，监督学习对某些注意力分数方向存在局部盲点，而这些方向可通过掩码重建检测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解SPT如何驱动优化找到更优解，以及为什么标准监督训练在Transformer中可能失败。

Method: 对Amos et al. (2024)的SPT方法进行系统性复现和消融研究，并通过简化理论设置分析监督学习的局限性。

Result: SPT的核心改进在于帮助模型学习邻近交互；监督训练在随机初始化下难以学习有用的查询-键注意力模式；监督学习对某些注意力分数方向存在局部盲点。

Conclusion: 瓶颈不在于深度或泛化能力，而在于监督信号本身无法有效引导注意力模式的学习；掩码重建目标可弥补监督学习的这一盲区。

Abstract: Amos et al. (2024) showed that the accuracy of Transformer models in sequence classification can be significantly improved by first pretraining with a masked token prediction objective without external data or augmentation, a procedure referred to as self-pretraining (SPT). While the primary objective of Amos et al. (2024) was to showcase that Transformers can achieve strong performance on the Long-Range Arena (LRA), their pipeline raises more fundamental questions: How does SPT drive optimization to better solutions? Why can standard supervised training fail in Transformers? To better understand this, we replicate and systematically ablate the findings of Amos et al. (2024). Our ablations suggest that a central bottleneck in the studied settings is not depth or generalization alone, but the ability of label supervision to learn useful query-key Attention patterns from random initialization. With a minimal setup, we identify learning proximity interactions - turning absolute positional encodings into proximity-biased Attention scores - as a key source of the improvements brought by SPT. Finally, in a simplified theoretical setup, we show that label supervision can be locally blind to certain Attention-score directions that are instead detectable through masked reconstruction.

</details>


### [176] [UOTIP: Unbalanced Optimal Transport Map for Unpaired Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21094)
*Donggyu Lee,Taekyung Lee,Jaewoong Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于非平衡最优传输（UOT）的无配对图像逆问题求解方法UOTIP，通过松弛精确边际约束并结合似然代价函数，实现从含噪测量到干净信号分布的非平衡映射，在无配对训练场景下达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无配对图像逆问题求解方法难以同时处理多级观测噪声、类别不平衡及不同噪声类型场景，缺乏鲁棒且泛化的解决方案。

Method: 将逆问题重构任务建模为从含噪测量分布到干净信号分布的非平衡最优传输映射学习，引入似然代价函数，并采用二次代价项保证传输映射的存在唯一性（满足扭曲条件）。

Result: 在线性和非线性逆问题的无配对基准测试中，UOTIP取得了最先进的性能，展现出对多级噪声、类别不平衡和多样噪声类型的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 非平衡最优传输框架为无配对逆问题提供了有效的解决途径，理论上的扭曲条件保证了病态逆问题中映射的唯一性。

Abstract: We investigate unpaired image inverse problems, a challenging setting where only independent, non-paired sets of noisy measurements and clean target signals are available for training. We propose a novel inverse problem solver based on Unbalanced Optimal Transport, called Unbalanced Optimal Transport Map for Inverse Problems (UOTIP). Our method formulates the reconstruction task, predicting clean target signals from noisy measurements, as learning a UOT Map from noisy measurement distribution to clean signal distribution by incorporating a likelihood-based cost function. By relaxing the exact marginal constraint, the UOT framework provides key advantages to our model: robustness to multi-level observation noise, adaptability to class imbalance between noisy and clean datasets, and generalizability to diverse noise-type scenarios. Furthermore, we theoretically demonstrate that incorporating a quadratic cost term ensures the existence and uniqueness of the transport map by satisfying the twist condition, even for ill-posed inverse problems. Our experiments demonstrate that UOTIP achieves state-of-the-art performance on unpaired image inverse problem benchmarks, across linear and nonlinear inverse problems.

</details>


### [177] [A Typed Tensor Language for Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21103)
*Theofilos Mailis,Kalliopi-Christina Despotidou,Konstantinos Filippopolitis,Yannis Foufoulas,Thanasis-Michail Karampatsis,Andreas Ktenidis,Evdokia Mailli,Theodore Papamarkou,Yannis Ioannidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种类型化张量语言，用于形式化联邦学习与联邦分析中通用的客户端本地张量计算、可合并聚合与仅共享后处理的结构。通过区分联邦张量与共享张量，证明了单轮程序可通过与客户端数量无关的固定维度共享状态分解，并建立了可微分片段用于学习，涵盖了基于共享状态的联邦学习计算类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习与联邦分析常被描述为一系列独立的协议，尽管它们共享相同的数学形式（客户端本地张量计算、可合并聚合到共享状态、仅共享后处理）。缺乏一个统一的形式化框架来描述和推理这些计算的结构。

Method: 引入一种类型化张量语言，区分联邦张量（记录按客户端划分）与共享张量（全局可用）。通过虚拟全局张量定义语义，建立共享状态分解理论，证明类型化单轮程序可通过固定维度共享状态分解，并证明其可表示性的逆定理。进一步开发可微分片段，从客户端本地损失和梯度表达推导全局梯度。

Result: 证明了类型化单轮程序可分解为与客户端和记录数量无关的固定维度共享状态；证明了可表示性的逆定理，即分解的编码器和解码器可用该语言表达；建立了可微分片段，支持服务器端梯度下降和共享线性代数二阶更新的类型化迭代程序。

Conclusion: 该框架形式化刻画了一类广泛的联邦学习计算，其通信通过固定维度的共享状态进行，为理解和设计联邦学习协议提供了统一的数学基础。

Abstract: Federated learning and analytics are often described as collections of separate protocols, even when they share the same mathematical form: client-local tensor computation, mergeable aggregation into shared state, and shared-only post-processing. We introduce a typed tensor language that formalizes this structure. The language distinguishes federated tensors, whose records are partitioned across clients along a tracked record axis, from shared tensors, which are available globally. Its semantics are defined by comparison with a virtual global tensor, used only as a reference object. The main result is a shared-state factorization theory. We show that typed one-round programs factor through fixed-dimensional shared state whose size is independent of the number of clients and records, computed from client-local tensor expressions and merged across clients. We also prove a converse representability result; factorizations whose encoders and decoders are expressible in the language are realized by typed one-round programs, and the correspondence extends to iterative programs whose cross-round state is shared. This gives a formal account of the computations in the language that can be expressed as encode, merge, and decode procedures. We then develop a differentiable fragment for learning. If a per-record loss and its per-record gradient are represented by client-local tensor expressions, the global gradient is represented by record-axis summation of the federated gradient tensor. This yields typed iterative programs for server-side gradient descent and shared-linear-algebra second-order updates. The framework characterizes a broad class of federated learning computations whose communication passes through fixed-dimensional shared state.

</details>


### [178] [HORST: Composing Optimizer Geometries for Sparse Transformer Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21104)
*Tom Jacobs,Rohan Jain,Rebekka Burkholz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HORST优化器，通过双曲镜像映射组合自适应优化器（L∞偏置）和L1稀疏性偏置，在保持稳定性的同时实现Transformer稀疏训练，在视觉和语言任务上显著优于AdamW。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准优化器无法同时兼顾稀疏性（需要L1偏置）和训练稳定性（自适应优化器隐含L∞偏置），两者存在根本性冲突。

Method: 将优化器步骤视为非交换算子，通过双曲镜像映射（hyperbolic mirror map）组合自适应方法的L∞稳定偏置和L1稀疏偏置，得到模块化优化器HORST。

Result: 在视觉和语言任务的Transformer稀疏训练中，HORST在所有稀疏度水平上一致且显著优于AdamW基线，在高稀疏度时优势尤为明显。

Conclusion: 通过算子组合视角可以原则性地融合不同优化几何特性，HORST有效解决了稀疏训练中稳定性和稀疏性的矛盾。

Abstract: Sparsifying transformers remains a fundamental challenge, as standard optimizers fail to simultaneously encourage sparsity and maintain training stability. Effective adaptive optimizers exhibit an implicit $L_{\infty}$ bias favoring stability, yet, sparsity requires an $L_1$ bias. To integrate sparsity, we propose a composition of optimizer steps, which we cast as non-commutative operators to analyze and combine their optimization geometry in a principled way. This yields HORST (Hyperbolic Operator for Robust Sparse Training), a modular optimizer that inherits stability from adaptive methods while inducing $L_1$ sparsity bias through a hyperbolic mirror map. Our experiments demonstrate its utility for sparse training of transformers on both vision and language tasks. HORST consistently and significantly outperforms AdamW baselines across all sparsity levels, with large gains at higher sparsity.

</details>


### [179] [Improved Guarantees for Constrained Online Convex Optimization via Self-Contraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21107)
*Dhruv Sarkar,Abhishek Sinha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对具有对抗性约束的在线凸优化问题，提出了一种基于投影的简单算法，在强凸损失下实现了$O(\log T)$的遗憾和$O(\log T)$的累积约束违反（CCV），在凸损失下实现了$O(\sqrt{T})$的遗憾和$O(\sqrt{T})$的CCV，显著改进了现有结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法在强凸损失下虽能达到$O(\log T)$的遗憾，但CCV为$O(\sqrt{T \log T})$；在凸损失下遗憾为$O(\sqrt{T})$，CCV为$O(\sqrt{T} \log T)$。本文旨在同时优化遗憾和CCV，实现指数级或对数级别的改进。

Method: 提出一种基于投影的简单算法，利用自缩曲线（self-contracted curves）的几何结果来约束轨迹长度，从而在保证低遗憾的同时显著降低累积约束违反。

Result: 强凸损失：遗憾$O(\log T)$，CCV $O(\log T)$（CCV指数级改进）；凸损失：遗憾$O(\sqrt{T})$，CCV $O(\sqrt{T})$（CCV改进$\log T$因子）。

Conclusion: 该工作通过几何工具大幅提升了约束在线凸优化中累积约束违反的界，所采用的几何结果（自缩曲线）可能具有独立的研究价值。

Abstract: We consider Constrained Online Convex Optimization (COCO) with adversarially chosen constraints. At each round, the learner chooses an action before observing the loss and constraint function for that round. The goal is to achieve small static regret against the best point satisfying all constraints while also controlling cumulative constraint violation ($\mathsf{CCV}$). For strongly convex losses, state-of-the-art algorithms achieve $O(\log T)$ regret and $O(\sqrt{T \log T})$ $\mathsf{CCV}.$ The corresponding best-known bounds for convex losses is $O(\sqrt{T})$ regret and $O(\sqrt{T} \log T)$ $\mathsf{CCV}$. In this paper, we give a simple projection-based algorithm that simultaneously achieves $O(\log T)$ regret and $O(\log T)$ $\mathsf{CCV}$ for strongly-convex losses, yielding an exponential improvement in the $\mathsf{CCV}$. For the convex losses, our algorithm improves the $\mathsf{CCV}$ to $O(\sqrt{T})$ while maintaining the optimal $O(\sqrt{T})$ regret. The key to our improvement is a recent geometric result for self-contracted curves, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [180] [Advantage Collapse in Group Relative Policy Optimization: Diagnosis and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21125)
*Xixiang He,Qiyao Sun,Ao Cheng,Xingming Li,Xuanyu Ji,Hailun Lu,Runke Huang,Qingyong Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出优势坍塌率（ACR）作为诊断指标，用于量化GRPO算法中因组内奖励同质化导致的梯度失效问题，并在此基础上提出自适应虚拟样本策略优化（AVSPO），通过注入虚拟奖励样本来缓解优势坍塌，在0.5B至14B参数模型上实现4-6个百分点的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO算法在RLVR框架下提升LLM推理能力时，存在优势坍塌问题：组内奖励同质化（全对或全错）导致优势趋近于零，梯度消失，训练停滞。现有方法缺乏对这一失效模式的系统性诊断与缓解手段。

Method: 1) 提出ACR指标，量化训练批次中梯度无效的比例；2) 提出AVSPO算法，作为GRPO的轻量扩展，通过ACR实时监测，在检测到优势坍塌时向同质化组中注入虚拟奖励样本，在不增加模型 rollout 的情况下恢复有效梯度。

Result: 在0.5B至14B参数的多个模型上，ACR能强预测训练停滞与最终性能；AVSPO相比GRPO减少58-63%的优势坍塌，在所有模型规模上一致提升4-6个百分点的准确率，且在域外任务上保持泛化能力。

Conclusion: ACR是诊断RLVR训练中优势坍塌的有效指标，AVSPO是一种轻量且有效的缓解方案，能够在不增加推理开销的前提下显著提升GRPO的训练效果与最终性能。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), a prominent algorithm within the Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) framework, has achieved strong results in improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). However, GRPO is prone to advantage collapse, a failure mode where homogeneous rewards within a group (e.g., all correct or all incorrect answers) yield near-zero advantages and vanishing gradients. To address this, we introduce the Advantage Collapse Rate (ACR), the first diagnostic metric quantifying the proportion of training batches with ineffective gradients. Across models from 0.5B to 14B parameters on mathematical reasoning benchmarks, we show that ACR strongly predicts training stagnation and final performance. We then propose Adaptive Virtual Sample Policy Optimization (AVSPO), a lightweight extension of GRPO that injects virtual reward samples, guided by real-time ACR monitoring, to enable learning from homogeneous groups without additional model rollouts. AVSPO reduces advantage collapse by 58-63% relative to GRPO and yields consistent accuracy gains of 4-6 percentage points across all model scales, while maintaining generalization on the evaluated out-of-domain task. Code and datasets are available at https://qingyonghu.github.io/AVSPO.

</details>


### [181] [Reasoning-Trace Collapse: Evaluating the Loss of Explicit Reasoning During Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21127)
*Lukas Twist,Helen Yannakoudakis,Jie M. Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 标准微调（使用无推理链的指令-回复数据）会导致推理模型产生“推理轨迹坍缩”：模型保留正确答案但失去有效的结构化推理过程。本文提出结构评估框架，发现简单损失掩码策略可缓解此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型通过产生中间推理步骤获得性能提升，但下游微调使用的普通指令数据不含推理链。这种不匹配导致推理轨迹坍缩，而仅看答案准确率的评估会掩盖这一失败模式。

Method: 提出结构评估框架，从有效推理、空推理、缺失推理、截断推理四个维度评估推理质量，并测量推理条件下的任务性能。在四个开源推理模型上进行标准监督微调实验，并测试损失掩码策略。

Result: 标准SFT会快速抑制有效推理轨迹；仅看答案指标会严重掩盖推理失败；在有效推理条件下任务性能仍高，但有效推理率急剧下降；简单损失掩码策略可显著缓解坍缩。

Conclusion: 对微调后的推理模型评估应报告结构推理可靠性指标，而非仅报告最终答案性能，尤其在适应数据不含推理链时更为重要。

Abstract: Explicit reasoning models are trained to produce intermediate reasoning traces before final answers, but downstream fine-tuning is often performed on ordinary instruction-response data that contains no such traces. We show that this mismatch can induce reasoning-trace collapse: a fine-tuned model continues to produce plausible final answers while losing the structurally valid explicit reasoning traces that made it a reasoning model in the first place. We introduce a structural evaluation framework that separates answer correctness from reasoning-trace validity, measuring valid, empty, missing, and truncated reasoning alongside reasoning-conditioned task performance. Using this framework, we study four open-weight reasoning models and find that standard supervised fine-tuning can rapidly suppress valid reasoning traces, and that answer-only metrics can substantially obscure this failure: in several settings, performance conditional on valid reasoning remains high while the rate of valid reasoning falls sharply. We further show that simple loss-masking strategies can substantially mitigate collapse without requiring teacher-generated reasoning traces. These results suggest that evaluations of fine-tuned reasoning models should report structural reasoning reliability metrics in addition to final-answer performance, especially when adaptation data does not contain explicit reasoning traces.

</details>


### [182] [Learning First Integrals via Backward-Generated Data and Guided Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21160)
*Jingfeng Zhong,Zhengxiang Liu,Zhijie Wang,Shuai Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FISolver，一种基于LLM的一阶积分发现求解器。通过"反向生成"算法构建大规模(微分方程,一阶积分)配对数据集，结合监督微调与基于Levenshtein距离的强化学习奖励，以较低计算成本显著超越Mathematica等商业求解器及更大规模的数学LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一阶积分（首次积分）的发现对理解动力系统中的守恒律具有基础性科学意义，但现有符号计算工具和LLM在该任务上表现有限，主要受限于高质量训练数据稀缺以及成功求解高度依赖数学直觉。

Method: 1) 提出"反向生成"算法：从采样的积分反推微分方程，系统构建大规模(微分方程,一阶积分)配对数据集；2) 对紧凑型数学模型进行监督微调；3) 引入基于Levenshtein距离的整形奖励进行强化学习进一步优化；4) 设计数据合成与混合策略，从稀疏样本实现向困难问题族的有效迁移。

Result: 在挑战性基准测试上，FISolver以显著更低的计算成本，大幅优于更大规模数学LLM及Mathematica等商业求解器。

Conclusion: 这项工作展示了一条数据驱动的自动化发现一阶积分的新路线，表明通过合理的数据生成与训练策略，小型模型也能在符号数学推理任务上超越大型模型和商业软件。

Abstract: The discovery of first integrals is of fundamental scientific importance for understanding conservation laws in dynamical systems. However, existing symbolic computation tools and Large Language Models (LLMs) remain limited on this task because high-quality training data are scarce and successful solutions often depend on mathematical intuition. This paper presents FISolver, an LLM-based solver developed to address this challenge. First, we introduce a "Backward Generation" algorithm that systematically builds large-scale datasets of (differential equation, first integral) pairs by deriving differential equations from sampled integrals, thereby alleviating the data scarcity bottleneck. Second, we apply supervised fine-tuning to a compact mathematical model and further improve its performance through reinforcement learning with a Levenshtein Distance-based shaped reward. In addition, we design data synthesis and blending strategies that support effective adaptation to difficult problem families from sparse examples. Experiments show that FISolver, while requiring substantially lower computational cost, significantly outperforms larger mathematical LLMs and commercial solvers such as Mathematica on challenging benchmarks, indicating a new data-driven route for automated discovery of first integrals.

</details>


### [183] [PREFINE: Preference-Based Implicit Reward and Cost Fine-Tuning for Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21225)
*Richa Verma,Bavish Kulur,Sanjay Chawla,Balaraman Ravindran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PREFINE方法，通过偏好反馈将预训练强化学习策略调整为安全感知策略，无需从头训练。该方法将DPO扩展到连续控制领域的轨迹级偏好对齐，通过构造反事实轨迹实现偏好对比，联合优化奖励保持与安全对齐。实验表明，PREFINE在保持原始奖励性能的同时，将约束违反和灾难性故障减少60%以上，且数据与计算效率显著优于传统离线RL或模仿学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习策略在部署时可能产生不安全行为，传统的安全对齐方法需要重新训练或使用数值成本约束，但实际场景中成本常以偏好形式提供。因此，如何在无需从头训练的情况下，利用偏好数据将预训练的奖励优化策略调整为安全感知策略成为一个关键问题。

Method: PREFINE方法将广泛用于大语言模型微调的DPO扩展到序列决策场景。具体包括：(1) 构造策略采样的反事实轨迹以建立有意义的偏好对比；(2) 联合优化奖励保持与安全对齐目标；(3) 在连续控制环境中实现轨迹级别的偏好对齐。

Result: 在连续控制环境中，PREFINE将约束违反和灾难性故障减少60%以上，同时保持原始奖励性能。与全量离线RL或模仿学习相比，具有显著更高的数据和计算效率。

Conclusion: PREFINE成功地将偏好对齐方法引入连续域的安全策略适应问题，在偏好反馈设置下实现了低成本、高回报的策略微调，为预训练RL策略的安全对齐提供了一种高效实用的解决方案。

Abstract: We address the problem of making a pre-trained reinforcement learning (RL) policy safety-aware by incorporating cost constraints without retraining it from scratch. While costs could be numerically encoded, we assume a more general setting is when costs are provided as preferences. Given a reward-optimized policy and a small dataset of preferred (low-cost) and dispreferred (high-cost) trajectories, our goal is to fine-tune the policy to generate low-cost behaviors while retaining high rewards. Unlike standard RLHF in language models, where preferences are defined over responses to the same prompt, our setting involves trajectory-level preferences in continuous control environments. We introduce PREFINE: Preference-based Implicit Reward and Cost Fine-Tuning for Safety Alignment which is a preference-based fine-tuning method that adapts Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), which is now widely used for LLM fine-tuning, to the sequential decision making setting. PREFINE constructs policy-sampled counterfactual trajectories to establish meaningful preference contrasts and jointly optimizes for reward retention and safety alignment. Empirically, PREFINE reduces constraint violations and catastrophic failures by over 60% while maintaining original reward behavior. PREFINE produces policies that achieve low-cost, high-reward performance with significantly improved data and computational efficiency compared to full offline RL or imitation learning, bridging preference alignment and safe policy adaptation in continuous domains.

</details>


### [184] [OCTOPUS: Optimized KV Cache for Transformers via Octahedral Parametrization Under optimal Squared error quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21226)
*Mark Boss,Vikram Voleti,Simon Donné,Shimon Vainer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OCTOPUS提出了一种基于八面体参数化的联合旋转坐标三元组量化方法，通过非均匀比特分配和Lloyd-Max量化器，在长上下文自回归推理的KV缓存压缩中超越了先前的旋转预条件编解码器，且无需解压缩即可在解码时重构key。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文自回归推理中，KV缓存占据了大量内存带宽和存储空间，需要高效的压缩方法来降低开销。

Method: 对旋转后的坐标三元组进行联合量化：通过八面体参数化将三元组方向映射到正方形，对得到的两个坐标和三元组范数分别使用Lloyd-Max量化器，并依据实现匹配的边缘分布进行优化；采用非均匀比特分配，仅依赖于key的总维度。

Result: 在文本、视频、音频等任务中，OCTOPUS在所有比特宽度和评估指标上匹配或超越所有先前的旋转编解码器，在极低比特率下优势更为显著；同时，融合的Triton实现可在不物化解压缩key的情况下动态重构，不增加解码时的带宽或延迟。

Conclusion: OCTOPUS通过八面体参数化联合量化三元组，实现了数据无关、在线、确定性的KV缓存压缩，在多种模态下均达到最优性能，且解码效率高。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache dominates memory bandwidth and footprint in long-context autoregressive inference. Recent rotation-preconditioned codecs (TurboQuant, PolarQuant) show that a structured random rotation followed by a per-coordinate scalar quantizer matched to an analytically tractable marginal is a near-optimal recipe for KV compression. OCTOPUS advances this paradigm through joint quantization of rotated coordinate triplets. Each triplet's direction is mapped to a square via an octahedral parameterization, and the two resulting coordinates and the triplet norm are Lloyd-Max quantized against implementation-matched marginals. Optimizing the per-triplet squared error gives a strictly non-uniform bit allocation depending only on the total dimensionality of the keys. We find the finite-dimensional quality optimum with sweeps to be constant on every real decoder we test. The codec is data-oblivious, online, and deterministic given a seed. Across text, video, and audio, OCTOPUS matches or beats every prior rotation codec at every reported bit width and metric, with a lead that grows as bits drop for extreme compression. Furthermore, a fused Triton implementation reconstructs keys on the fly without materializing the uncompressed key, so the codec adds no decode-time bandwidth or latency over the existing dequantization. Project Page: https://octopus-quant.github.io/

</details>


### [185] [APEX: Autonomous Policy Exploration for Self-Evolving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21240)
*Yibo Li,Jiashuo Yang,Zhi Zheng,Zhiyuan Hu,Yuan Sui,Shizun Wang,Yufei He,Bryan Hooi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出APEX方法，通过构建和维护策略有向无环图来解决自进化智能体在测试时的探索崩溃问题，在文本冒险和网页交互任务上超越所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化智能体在测试时通过累积记忆和反思来学习，但会遭遇探索崩溃——随着记忆增长，行为集中在熟悉的高奖励路径上，降低了发现更优策略的可能性。

Method: APEX通过策略图（里程碑的有向无环图，含先决依赖边）来构建并维护显式策略空间。Fork Discovery基于证据扩展未探索方向，Policy Selection在规划时平衡探索与利用。

Result: 在Jericho九个文本冒险游戏和WebArena真实网页交互基准上，APEX超越所有基线。消融实验验证了各组件的贡献及其在不同设置下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: APEX有效解决了自进化智能体的探索崩溃问题，实现了持续探索，在复杂交互任务中表现优异。

Abstract: LLM agents have shown strong performance across a wide range of complex tasks, including interactive environments that require long-horizon decision making. But these agents cannot learn on the fly at test time. Self-evolving agents address this by accumulating memory and reflection across episodes rather than requiring model-weight updates. However, these agents often suffer from exploration collapse: as memory grows, behavior concentrates around familiar high-reward routines, reducing the chance of discovering better alternatives. To address this problem, we propose Autonomous Policy EXploration (APEX), which builds and maintains an explicit strategy space through a strategy map-a directed acyclic graph of milestones with prerequisite dependency edges. In APEX, Fork Discovery expands the map with evidence-grounded unexplored directions, while Policy Selection balances exploration and exploitation during planning. Evaluated on nine Jericho text-adventure games and WebArena, a realistic web interaction benchmark, APEX outperforms all baselines. Extensive ablations validate each component's contribution and demonstrate robustness across diverse settings, demonstrating APEX's effectiveness for sustained exploration in self-evolving agents.

</details>


### [186] [Divide and Contrast: Learning Robust Temporal Features without Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21241)
*Abdul-Kazeem Shamba,Kerstin Bach,Gavin Taylor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Divide and Contrast (Di-COT)，一种无监督时间序列表示学习框架，通过随机划分窗口为重叠子块进行对比学习，避免数据增强和多编码器前向传播，在六个大规模真实数据集及UCR/UEA基准上达到SOTA性能，同时显著降低训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督时间序列表示学习方法要么计算成本高，要么依赖的假设在多样化的时序动态中不成立，需要一种既高效又无需强假设的无监督框架。

Method: Di-COT随机将每个时间窗口划分为少量重叠子块，在子块层面而非时间步层面进行对比学习；采用与批次大小和子块数量相关的对比目标函数，使损失计算与序列长度无关。

Result: 在六个大规模真实数据集及UCR/UEA基准上，Di-COT在分类、聚类、kNN和跨数据集迁移任务中达到SOTA性能，同时大幅减少训练时间。

Conclusion: Di-COT通过学习语义结构化且可迁移的表示，证明无需数据增强和多编码器前向传播的对比框架在时间序列表示学习中既高效又有效。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning for time-series representation aims to reduce reliance on labeled data while maintaining strong downstream performance, yet many existing approaches incur high computational costs or rely on assumptions that do not hold across diverse temporal dynamics. In this work, we introduce Divide and Contrast (Di-COT), an unsupervised framework that avoids data augmentation and multiple encoder passes by contrasting informative substructures within a window rather than individual timesteps. Di-COT stochastically partitions each window into a small number of overlapping sub-blocks per iteration, enabling efficient and meaningful contrast while mitigating false positives during temporal transitions. To further improve scalability, we adopt a contrastive objective whose computation depends on the batch size and the number of sub-blocks, making loss computation independent of sequence length. Extensive experiments on six large-scale real-world datasets, as well as the UCR and UEA benchmarks, demonstrate that Di-COT learns semantically structured and transferable representations, achieving state-of-the-art performance on classification, clustering, $k$NN, and cross-dataset transfer, while substantially reducing training time. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/sfi-norwai/Di-COT.

</details>


### [187] [On the Cost and Benefit of Chain of Thought: A Learning-Theoretic Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21260)
*Yue Zhang,Zhiyi Dong,Tommaso Cesari,Yongyi Mao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个学习理论框架来分析思维链（CoT），将其分解为两个关键风险项： oracle-轨迹风险（OTR）和轨迹不匹配风险（TMR），并证明了在稳定性条件下，TMR存在由精确放大因子决定的紧上界，从而精确刻画了CoT何时有益、何时有害及其转换条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作缺乏一个严谨的理论框架来理解CoT何时有效、何时无效以及影响其效果的关键因素。

Method: 将CoT建模为答案映射与链式规则之间的交互，定义了推理风险及其规范分解为OTR和TMR两项；通过稳定性分析推导TMR的紧上界及误差增长模式。

Result: 1) 推理风险可规范分解为OTR（CoT的收益）和TMR（CoT的误差累积代价）；2) 缺乏稳定性时，即使OTR为零，TMR也可能任意大；3) 在稳定性条件下，TMR存在由精确放大因子决定的紧上界，包括有界、线性和指数三种误差增长机制。

Conclusion: 该理论精确刻画了CoT有帮助、有害以及二者转换的条件，为理解和改进CoT提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We develop a learning-theoretic framework for understanding Chain of Thought (CoT). We model CoT as the interaction between an answer map and a chain rule that generates intermediate questions autoregressively, and define the reasoning risk of a hypothesis under this interaction. Our first result is a tight canonical decomposition of this risk into two terms with opposing roles: an oracle-trajectory risk (OTR), which captures the benefit of CoT and reduces to a target-domain risk in a domain adaptation problem, and a trajectory-mismatch risk (TMR), which captures the cost of CoT through error accumulation along mismatched reasoning trajectories. We then show that this cost is unavoidable without structure: if any one of the loss, the hypothesis answer map, or the chain rule lacks stability, the TMR can be arbitrarily large even when the OTR is zero and the hypothesis is uniformly close to the ground truth. Conversely, under stability, we prove a tight upper bound on the TMR governed by an exact amplification factor that identifies bounded, linear, and exponential error-growth regimes. Together, these results give a precise theory of when CoT helps, when it hurts, and what controls the transition between the two.

</details>


### [188] [Nonparametric Learning and Earning with One-Point Feedback under Nonstationarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21263)
*Xiangyu Yang,Feng Xu,Jian-Qiang Hu,Jiaqiao Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在仅能观察单一定价产生的收入、且市场环境可能渐变或突变的情况下，卖方如何通过基于收入的梯度近似和重启机制进行非参数动态定价，并提供了理论保障和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决实际动态定价中两个交织的挑战：从有限反馈（每周期仅一个价格对应的收入）中学习收入-需求关系，以及适应可能渐变或突变的非平稳市场环境，且不假设需求的具体参数形式。

Method: 提出一个学习框架：使用每周期单一观测数据构建基于收入的梯度近似来更新价格；引入重启机制定期刷新学习过程以折扣过时信息；针对未知非平稳程度，加入元学习层自适应地在多个重启调度间进行对冲。

Result: 给出了方法的性能保证，展示了累积收入损失如何依赖于时间跨度和市场变化幅度；模拟实验（含合成数据和真实数据）验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架能在非参数设定下有效应对非平稳环境中的动态定价问题，兼顾学习与收益，且对未知变化程度具有自适应性。

Abstract: Firms increasingly rely on dynamic pricing to respond to evolving customer demand, yet in many applications they observe only the revenue generated by a single posted price in each period. At the same time, market conditions may shift gradually or abruptly due to changes in customer preferences, competition, or external shocks. These features create two intertwined challenges: learning the revenue--demand relationship from limited feedback and adapting pricing decisions to a changing environment. We study how a seller can learn and earn effectively under these constraints, without assuming a specific parametric form for demand. We develop a learning framework that updates prices using revenue-based gradient approximations constructed from one observation per period. To address environmental changes, we incorporate a restarting mechanism that periodically refreshes the learning process so that outdated information is discounted. When the degree of nonstationarity is unknown, we further introduce a meta-learning layer to adaptively hedge across multiple restarting schedules. We provide performance guarantees for our approach, showing how cumulative revenue loss relative to a fully informed benchmark depends on both the time horizon and the magnitude of market variation. Simulation experiments using synthetic and real-world data illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed procedures.

</details>


### [189] [How Much Online RL is Enough? Informative Rollouts for Offline Preference Optimization in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21266)
*Richa Verma,Balaraman Ravindran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出G2D（GRPO到DPO）三阶段流水线：先进行少量GRPO在线预热，构建静态偏好数据集，再使用DPO进行离线微调。实验表明，适度预热后的离线DPO在数学推理任务上以更低计算成本匹配或超越纯在线GRPO，并将离线-在线差距重新定义为数据信息性问题而非算法本质差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO虽在语言模型推理中表现优异，但需要持续在线 rollout 生成，计算成本高且难以扩展。DPO作为稳定高效的离线替代方案，在冷启动的SFT策略上通常被认为不如在线RL方法。作者希望弥合这一离线-在线差距，实现计算效率与性能的平衡。

Method: 提出G2D三阶段流水线：(1) 短时GRPO在线预热；(2) 构建静态偏好数据集；(3) 使用DPO离线微调。在Qwen2.5-7B和Llama-3.1-8B上变化在线步数K进行实验分析。

Result: Qwen2.5-7B上，G2D在K=150时MATH-500准确率达62.4%，超过GRPO的51.6%（提升10.8%），计算成本降低约4倍。Llama-3.1-8B上，G2D在K=500时达49.4%，超越GRPO。性能的关键不在于偏好对数量，而在于数据的信息性（informativeness）。适度预热产生具有校准不确定性的rollout，提供更强对比信号；过度预热导致过度自信策略和数据信息性下降。

Conclusion: 离线-在线差距本质上是数据信息性问题，而非算法根本差异。短时在线RL预热配合适当难度校准的微调数据集，可作为在线RL的计算高效替代方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for reasoning in language models, with GRPO as its primary example. However, GRPO requires continuous online rollout generation, making it computationally expensive and difficult to scale. While Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) offers a stable and efficient offline alternative, it is typically expected to underperform w.r.t. online RL methods such as GRPO when trained on rollouts from a cold supervised fine-tuned (SFT) policy. We introduce G2D (GRPO to DPO)}, a three-stage pipeline that performs a short GRPO warm-up, constructs a static preference dataset, and fine-tunes a model offline with DPO. Across a set of values of the number of online steps (K) in GRPO on Qwen2.5-7B and Llama-3.1-8B, we find that offline DPO with moderate warm-up matches or outperforms GRPO at substantially lower compute cost in our setting. On Qwen2.5-7B, G2D at K=150 achieves 62.4% on MATH-500, outperforming GRPO (51.6%) by 10.8% at ~4x lower compute. On Llama-3.1-8B, G2D at K=500 achieves 49.4%, surpassing GRPO in our experimental setting. We show that performance is not governed by the number of preference pairs, which does not vary much w.r.t. K, but by their informativeness. Moderate warm-up produces rollouts with calibrated uncertainty, yielding stronger contrastive signal, while excessive warm-up leads to overconfident policies and less informative data. Our results recast the offline-online gap in RLVR as primarily a data informativeness problem, and identify short online RL warm-up with appropriate difficulty calibration of the fine-tuning dataset as a compute-efficient alternative to online RL.

</details>


### [190] [A Mechanistic Study of Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21288)
*Marin Biloš,James T. Wilson,Anderson Schneider,Yuriy Nevmyvaka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统刻画了表格基础模型在分类与回归任务中的工作机制：不同架构的模型虽在精度上趋同，但实现了性质不同的基于相似性的读取机制（注意力加权投票 vs. 类条件均值读取）；验证了置换不变性源于特定位置参数，移除后可使近似不变性变为精确不变性；并针对每种读取机制设计了特定扰动（hub攻击与排名攻击），揭示了其特征性失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格基础模型在精度上趋于一致，但排行榜无法回答三个关键问题：(i) 模型是否执行相同的上下文算法；(ii) 行、列、类别置换不变性源于何处；(iii) 针对推理机制设计的扰动下模型的鲁棒性如何。

Method: 通过因果干预实验确认不同模型家族实现性质不同的基于相似性的读取机制（注意力加权投票与类条件均值读取）；分析位置参数与置换不变性的关系，移除参数使近似不变性变为精确不变性；针对每种读取机制设计特定扰动（hub攻击与排名攻击），并与重拟合基线对比。

Result: 不同模型家族实现了性质不同的相似性读取机制；先前提到的表示坍塌在实践中并非问题；置换不变性可追溯到特定位置参数，移除后精度保持且不变性变为精确；针对读取机制设计的扰动成功复现了预期失败模式，hub攻击与排名攻击将其与重拟合基线区分开来。

Conclusion: 研究为当代表格基础模型提供了机制层面的解释，并识别出哪些归纳偏置同时决定了模型的精度与特征性失败模式。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models with different architectures converge in accuracy across a range of classification and regression tasks. This raises questions a leaderboard cannot answer: (i) whether the models execute the same in-context algorithm, (ii) where row, column, and class-permutation invariances originate, and (iii) how robust they are under perturbations engineered against the inferred mechanism. We characterize all three. The model families realize qualitatively distinct similarity-based readouts: from an attention-weighted vote over context labels to a class-conditional mean readout, each confirmed by causal intervention. We find that the representation collapse highlighted in prior work is not a practical concern for them. Each model's permutation invariances trace to specific positional parameters whose removal preserves accuracy and makes approximate invariance exact. Perturbations engineered against each readout reproduce predicted failure modes; hub and rank attacks isolate them from refit baselines. Together these results give a mechanistic account of contemporary tabular foundation models and identify which inductive biases govern both their accuracy and characteristic failures.

</details>


### [191] [Automatic Discovery of Disease Subgroups by Contrasting with Healthy Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21301)
*Robin Louiset,Edouard Duchesnay,Benoit Dufumier,Antoine Grigis,Pietro Gori*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种对比子群发现方法Deep UCSL，通过对比患者与健康对照者，识别仅由病理因素驱动的亚组，排除与健康人群共有的变异，在多个医疗影像数据集上取得更优效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生物医学子群发现中，患者子群常受到与健康人群共有的无关因素干扰，导致发现的子群混杂了非病理变异。作者希望分离仅由病理因素驱动的子群，提升子群发现的解释性和临床价值。

Method: 采用深度特征提取器学习判别性表示空间；推导基于潜聚类和患者/对照标签的条件联合似然损失函数，通过期望最大化策略交替进行子群推断和特征编码器更新；加入正则化项鼓励表示捕获疾病特异性变异，忽略与对照共享的变异。

Result: 在MNIST示例和四个真实医学影像数据集上，Deep UCSL在子群质量上定量优于先前相关工作。

Conclusion: 对比学习范式能有效分离病理因素驱动的子群，为生物医学子群发现提供了一种更具解释性和鲁棒性的方法。

Abstract: In biomedical Subgroup Discovery, practitioners are interested in discovering interpretable and homogeneous subgroups within a group of patients. In this paper, assuming that healthy subjects (i.e., controls) share common but irrelevant factors of variation with the patients, we motivate and develop a Contrastive Subgroup Discovery method, entitled Deep UCSL. By contrasting patients with controls, Deep UCSL identifies subgroups driven solely by pathological factors, ignoring common variability shared with healthy subjects. Our framework employs a deep feature extractor to learn a discriminative representation space. Mathematically, we derive a novel loss based on the conditional joint likelihood of latent clusters and patient/control labels, optimized via an Expectation-Maximization strategy alternating between subgroup inference and feature encoder updates. A regularization term further encourages representations to capture disease-specific variability while ignoring variability shared with controls. Compared to previous related works, our approach quantitatively improves the quality of the estimated subgroups, as demonstrated on a MNIST example and four distinct real medical imaging datasets. Code and datasets are available at: https://github.com/rlouiset/deep_ucsl.

</details>


### [192] [CRAFT: Conflict-Resolved Aggregation for Federated Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21317)
*Ziqi Wang,Qiang Liu,Nils Thuerey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRAFT提出了一种将联邦学习中的全局更新聚合视为几何校正问题的新框架，通过闭式解进行冲突消解，并采用逐层自适应处理不同粒度特征冲突，在提升全局模型精度的同时减少了客户端间的性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异质数据分布下，联邦学习中对冲突客户端更新的简单平均会产生全局更新，该更新虽能改进全局目标但与特定客户端冲突，导致这些客户端性能下降。

Method: 将聚合问题建模为在满足无冲突对齐约束条件下寻找最接近参考方向的更新，推导了约束优化问题的闭式解，并采用逐层自适应机制处理不同特征粒度上的冲突。

Result: 在异质基准测试上的广泛实验表明，与最先进基线相比，CRAFT提升了全局模型精度并减少了客户端间的性能差异。

Conclusion: CRAFT通过其投影几何结构促进了共同下降结构并缓解了冲突，是一种有效的联邦学习聚合框架。

Abstract: The aggregation of conflicting client updates remains a fundamental bottleneck in federated learning (FL) under heterogeneous data distributions. Naive averaging can produce a global update that improves the global objective while conflicting with specific clients, causing degradation for those clients. In this work, we propose CRAFT (Conflict-Resolved Aggregation for Federated Training), a new aggregation framework that treats the global update as a geometric correction problem. We formulate aggregation as finding the update closest to a reference direction while satisfying conflict-free alignment constraints. We derive a closed-form expression for the constrained optimization problem, avoiding the computational overhead of iterative solvers. Furthermore, we use a layer-wise adaptation to address conflicts at varying feature granularities. We provide a theoretical analysis showing that CRAFT promotes a common-descent structure and mitigates conflicts through its projection geometry. Extensive experiments on heterogeneous benchmarks demonstrate that CRAFT improves the accuracy of the global model while reducing performance disparity across clients compared with state-of-the-art baselines. The source code for CRAFT is available at https://github.com/tum-pbs/CRAFT.

</details>


### [193] [Optimized Federated Knowledge Distillation with Distributed Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21322)
*Chaimaa Medjadji,Sylvain Kubler,Yves Le Traon,Guilain Leduc,Sadi Alawadi,Feras M. Awaysheh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedKDNAS框架，结合客户端神经架构搜索与服务端知识蒸馏，在非独立同分布条件下实现高精度、低计算与通信开销的联邦学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FL方法假设固定客户端架构，无法适应数据异质性和硬件约束，导致精度与效率间次优权衡。

Method: 每个客户端在精度-资源约束下自主选择轻量模型，结合监督学习与知识蒸馏进行本地训练，仅向服务端共享公共参考集上的预测；服务端聚合、平滑这些预测，可选结合教师模型生成稳定蒸馏目标用于下一轮。

Result: 在6个数据集上对比6种基线，FedKDNAS在非独立同分布条件下准确率提升高达15%，客户端CPU使用减少约28%，通信开销降低最多44倍。

Conclusion: FedKDNAS通过客户端架构自适应与蒸馏驱动的知识传递，有效实现了联邦学习中精度与效率的帕累托最优。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative model training without centralizing data. However, real-world deployments must simultaneously address statistical heterogeneity across client data (non-IID), system heterogeneity in device capabilities, and communication efficiency. Existing FL approaches mitigate these challenges through improved aggregation, personalization, or knowledge distillation, but they almost universally assume a fixed client architecture, limiting adaptability to heterogeneous data complexity and hardware constraints. This architectural constraint often leads to suboptimal trade-offs between accuracy and efficiency in real-world FL systems. This work introduces FedKDNAS, a distillation-driven FL framework that combines client-side neural architecture selection with distillation of server-coordinated knowledge. Each client autonomously selects a lightweight model under accuracy-resource constraints. It then trains it locally using a hybrid objective combining supervised learning and knowledge distillation and shares only predictions on a public reference set. The server then aggregates and smooths these predictions, optionally combining them with a teacher model, to produce stable distillation targets for the next round. Extensive evaluation on six datasets against six representative FL baselines (FedAvg, Ditto, FedMD, FedDF, FedDistill, Local-KD) demonstrates that FedKDNAS consistently achieves superior Pareto efficiency, improving accuracy by up to 15\% under non-IID conditions, reducing client CPU usage by approximately 28\%, and decreasing communication overhead by up to 44 times while maintaining lightweight logit-based communication.

</details>


### [194] [Fast and Stable Triangular Inversion for Delta-Rule Linear Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21325)
*Aleksandros Sobczyk,Gioele Gottardo,Christos K. Matzoros,Mirko De Vita,Filip Skogh,Anastasios Zouzias,Jiawei Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了线性注意力中三角矩阵求逆的直推法和迭代法，重点关注数值稳定性、计算复杂度和硬件效率。在NPU上实现最高4.3倍加速，同时保持端到端模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力（如Delta-Rule）中三角矩阵求逆是关键子例程，但它是性能瓶颈且对数值误差高度敏感，不恰当实现会严重影响模型精度。现有实现缺乏系统性分析。

Method: 系统分析直推法和迭代法两类三角矩阵求逆算法，聚焦于富含矩阵乘法（便于利用现代硬件）的方法，从数值稳定性、计算复杂度、硬件效率和实际部署角度进行全面评估。

Result: 在NPU上相比SGLang的最优实现取得最高4.3倍加速，在层级别实现显著性能提升，同时完全保持端到端模型精度。

Conclusion: 通过系统分析三角矩阵求逆算法并优化硬件实现，可以有效解决线性注意力中的性能瓶颈问题，在实现大幅加速的同时不影响模型精度。

Abstract: Linear attention has emerged as a cornerstone for efficient long-context architectures, as evidenced by its integration into state-of-the-art open-source models including Qwen3.5/3.6, Kimi Linear, and RWKV-7. Models that incorporate linear attention layers with the so-called Delta-Rule involve the inversion of triangular matrices as a core sub-routine. This operation often forms a performance bottleneck, and, due to its high-sensitivity to numerical errors, it can significantly deteriorate end-to-end model accuracy if it is not carefully implemented. This work provides a systematic analysis of both direct and iterative triangular inversion algorithms, targeting methods that are rich in matrix products, and, therefore, have the potential to efficiently utilize modern hardware. To that end, our analysis covers a broad spectrum of mathematical and practical aspects, with a heavy focus on numerical stability, computational complexity, and, ultimately, hardware efficiency and practical considerations. We provide a rigorous experimental evaluation to verify these properties in practical scenarios, and in low-precision floating-point representations, highlighting the strengths and limitations of each method. Performance benchmarks on NPUs reveal up to $4.3\times$ speed-up against the state-of-the-art implementations of SGLang for triangular matrix inversion, leading to significant performance improvements on the entire layer level, while maintaining full end-to-end model accuracy.

</details>


### [195] [Data-Efficient Neural Operator Training via Physics-Based Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21348)
*Alicja Polanska,Lorenzo Zanisi,Vignesh Gopakumar,Stanislas Pamela*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理信息的主动学习算法（physics-based acquisition），通过利用PDE残差指导数据选择，在神经算子求解偏微分方程时显著降低训练数据需求，实验表明该方法在数据效率上达到或超越现有最优水平，并具有注入物理先验偏置的独特优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子求解PDE虽能降低计算成本，但受限于大量训练数据需求，亟需减少数据采集成本的高效主动学习方法。

Method: 提出physics-based acquisition算法，利用PDE残差作为物理信息指导选择性采样，在迭代过程中获取最具有信息量的样本。

Result: 在1D Burgers方程和2D可压缩Navier-Stokes方程的数值实验中，该方法在数据效率上一致优于随机采样，达到或超过现有最优水平。

Conclusion: 该方法通过注入物理归纳偏置，确保计算资源专注于模型物理理解最薄弱的区域，在提升数据效率的同时保留了物理可解释性。

Abstract: Solving partial differential equations with neural operators significantly reduces computational costs but remains bottlenecked by high training data requirements. Active learning offers a natural framework to mitigate this by selectively acquiring the most informative samples in an iterative manner. We introduce physics-based acquisition - a novel physics-informed active learning algorithm that leverages the partial differential equation residual to guide data selection. We validate the method by presenting numerical experiments for the 1D Burgers equation and the 2D compressible Navier-Stokes equations. We show that, in our experiments, physics-based acquisition consistently outperforms random acquisition and matches the state of the art in data efficiency. At the same time, it has the unique advantage of injecting a physics inductive bias into the training process, ensuring that simulation cost is spent where the model's physical understanding is weakest.

</details>


### [196] [On the Regularity and Generalization of One-Step Wasserstein-guided Generative Models for PDE-Induced Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21388)
*Likun Lin,Zhongjian Wang,Jack Xin,Zhiwen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为PDE诱导的概率测度生成模型建立了理论基础，证明最优传输映射的Hölder连续性，并给出DeepParticle方法的泛化误差界和鲁棒性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成模型在经验上取得了显著成功，但关于其在科学计算中统计精度的现有理论大多较为悲观。本文旨在为正演概率测度的一步生成模型提供理论基础，弥合理论与实际表现之间的差距。

Method: 考虑线性椭圆/抛物方程、扩散方程和Fokker-Planck方程诱导的概率测度，证明目标测度满足加倍条件；结合最优传输的加倍测度正则性理论，证明均匀源测度到目标测度的最优传输映射具有Hölder连续性。以DeepParticle为代表实例，推导过量风险界，并建立目标漂移下的鲁棒性估计。

Result: 证明了PDE诱导的目标测度满足加倍条件；最优传输映射是Hölder连续的；给出了DeepParticle方法的过量风险界；建立了目标漂移下的鲁棒性估计；实验支持了理论导出的收敛速率。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上证明了一步生成模型在PDE诱导概率分布学习中的合理性，为Wasserstein引导生成模型提供了严格的近似理论依据，包括泛化误差和鲁棒性保证。

Abstract: Despite the remarkable empirical success of generative models, the available theory on their statistical accuracy in scientific computing remains largely pessimistic. This paper develops a theoretical framework for understanding the regularity of transport maps and the generalization properties of one-step Wasserstein-guided generative models for PDE-induced probability measures. We consider normalized target densities associated with linear elliptic and parabolic equations on bounded domains, as well as diffusion and Fokker--Planck equations on the torus. Under standard structural assumptions, we prove that these target measures satisfy doubling conditions. By combining this fact with regularity theory for optimal transport between doubling measures, we show that the optimal transport map from a uniform source measure to the target measure is Hölder continuous. This regularity yields an approximation-theoretic justification for one-step generative models that learn PDE-induced distributions via a single pushforward map. As a representative instance, we study DeepParticle and derive excess-risk bounds characterizing the discrepancy between the learned map and the population-optimal map. We also establish a robustness estimate under target shift and illustrate the theory with experiments which support the derived rates.

</details>


### [197] [HiRes: Inspectable Precedent Memory for Reaction Condition Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21420)
*Shreyas Vinaya Sathyanarayana,Raja Sekhar Pappala,Deepak Warrier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HiRes提出了一种层次化反应表示方法，结合图编码器、跨注意力机制、多流融合和k-NN检索，实现基于检索增强的反应条件推荐，在USPTO-Condition数据集上达到SOTA性能，同时提供了可解释的化学先例证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学家在逆合成断开选择后需要准确的反应条件预测以及支撑该预测的先例证据，现有方法难以同时满足预测准确性和化学可解释性。

Method: HiRes包含图编码器、转换感知的交叉注意力机制、多流反应融合以及k-NN检索层，将学习到的反应空间同时作为分类特征和可检查的先例记忆。

Result: 在USPTO-Condition数据集上，Catalyst、Solvent和Reagent的top-1准确率分别达到0.929、0.534和0.530，Catalyst与最佳基线持平，Solvent和Reagent优于REACON等模型。配对bootstrap分析证明检索增强比纯参数化方法在溶剂和试剂选择上有统计显著的提升。

Conclusion: HiRes弥合了预测准确性与化学可解释性之间的差距，提供统一的表示同时输出竞争性的条件推荐和具体的化学先例，为实际合成规划提供支持。

Abstract: Reaction condition recommendation sits immediately after retrosynthetic disconnection selection, and in practice, chemists require both accurate predictions and the precedents that justify them. We present HiRes (Hierarchical Reaction Representations), a retrieval-augmented condition recommendation system whose learned reaction space serves as both a classifier feature and an inspectable precedent memory. The model combines a graph encoder, transformation-aware cross-attention, multi-stream reaction fusion, and a k-NN retrieval layer. HiRes achieves state-of-the-art performance among primary-slot USPTO-Condition models, reaching Catalyst, Solvent, and Reagent top-1 accuracies (Acc@1) of 0.929, 0.534, and 0.530 respectively. It ties the best reported baseline on Catalyst while outperforming models such as REACON on Solvent and Reagent. Furthermore, paired bootstrap analysis demonstrates that integrating retrieval with learned condition heads provides statistically significant gains for solvent and reagent selection over purely parametric approaches. Ultimately, HiRes bridges the gap between predictive accuracy and chemical interpretability, offering a single representation that supplies both competitive recommendations and the concrete chemical precedents necessary for practical synthesis planning.

</details>


### [198] [Adaptive Signal Resuscitation: Channel-wise Post-Pruning Repair for Sparse Vision Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21426)
*Qishi Zhan,Ziheng Chen,Minxuan Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对高稀疏度下全局幅度剪枝导致的精度崩塌问题，提出无训练的自适应信号复苏（ASR）方法，通过通道级别的方差匹配校正与数据驱动的收缩规则，在剪枝后修复中显著优于层级修复方法，ResNet-50在90%稀疏度下CIFAR-10 top-1准确率提升至55.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高稀疏度下一次性幅度剪枝会导致精度严重下降，即使保留最大权重也无法避免。现有层级激活修复方法（如BatchNorm重校准）采用单一校正作用于整个层，无法处理同一层内不同通道受损程度不一致的问题——近乎坍缩的通道与保留信息方差的有效通道并存时，层级校正会过度放大受损通道，反而破坏修复效果。

Method: 提出Adaptive Signal Resuscitation (ASR)，一种无训练（training-free）的通道级剪枝后修复方法：(1) 对每个输出通道估计方差匹配校正量；(2) 引入数据驱动的收缩规则（shrinkage rule），对后剪枝信号弱的通道抑制不可靠校正，对健康通道保留校正；(3) 在BatchNorm重校准前应用，仅需少量校准集的前向传播，无需重训练。

Result: 在三个数据集、四种卷积架构、非结构化和结构化稀疏设置下，ASR均优于层级修复，在高稀疏度下增益最显著。ResNet-50在90%稀疏度下CIFAR-10 top-1准确率：ASR 55.6%，层级修复41.0%，仅BN重校准28.0%。消融实验表明：朴素的通道级方差匹配不足，引入收缩规则对稳定剪枝后修复至关重要。

Conclusion: 剪枝后修复的粒度应与剪枝造成的损害粒度相匹配；通道级修复搭配数据驱动的收缩机制，能以无训练方式有效恢复高稀疏度剪枝模型的精度，为一次性高稀疏度剪枝后的模型修复提供了实用且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: One-shot magnitude pruning can cause severe accuracy collapse in the high-sparsity regime, even when the pruning mask preserves the largest weights. We argue that this failure reflects a granularity mismatch in post-pruning repair. Under global magnitude pruning, nearly collapsed channels can coexist with channels that retain informative activation variance within the same layer. Existing layer-wise activation repair methods apply a single correction to the whole layer, and can therefore over-amplify damaged channels while trying to restore the layer-level signal. We propose Adaptive Signal Resuscitation (ASR), a training-free channel-wise repair method that matches the granularity of repair to the granularity of damage. ASR estimates a variance-matching correction for each output channel and stabilizes it with a data-driven shrinkage rule, suppressing unreliable corrections for channels with weak post-pruning signal while preserving corrections for healthier channels. Applied before BatchNorm recalibration, ASR requires only forward passes on a small calibration set and no retraining. Across three datasets, four convolutional architectures, and both unstructured and structured sparsity settings, ASR generally improves over layer-wise repair, with the clearest gains in high-sparsity regimes. On ResNet-50 at 90% sparsity, ASR recovers 55.6% top-1 accuracy on CIFAR-10, compared with 41.0% for layer-wise repair and 28.0% for BatchNorm-only recalibration. Ablations show that naive channel-wise variance matching is insufficient, and that shrinkage stabilizes post-pruning repair.

</details>


### [199] [Polynomial-Time Robust Multiclass Linear Classification under Gaussian Marginals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21428)
*Ilias Diakonikolas,Giannis Iakovidis,Mingchen Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究高斯分布下多类线性分类器的不可知学习。标准多类感知机算法在k≥3时存在根本性障碍，需要超多项式样本；本文提出配对不当学习框架实现全多项式时间鲁棒学习器，误差为Õ(k^{3/2}√opt)+ε，并针对k=3给出更优的O(opt)+ε结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二元情况(k=2)下线性分类器的不可知学习已有成熟算法理论，但k≥3时进展甚少。即使k=3，现有鲁棒算法在复杂度与表示规模上均对精度倒数呈指数依赖。本文旨在填补这一空白，发展多类线性分类器的结构性理论并设计高效算法。

Method: (1) 证明标准多类感知机算法在干净标签与高斯边际下仍需超多项式样本，揭示二元与多类之间的根本差异。(2) 提出配对不当学习框架(pairwise improper-learning)，通过成对比较与不当假设类实现高效学习。(3) 针对k=3及几何正则的多类线性分类器，提出基于局部化(localization)的更精细框架。

Result: (1) 多类感知机算法存在超多项式下界。(2) 配对不当学习框架实现误差Õ(k^{3/2}√opt)+ε，全多项式时间，维度无关。(3) k=3时误差达到O(opt)+ε；对几何正则的多类线性分类器达到poly(k)opt+ε。

Conclusion: 本文系统性地发展了多类线性分类器不可知学习的理论，揭示了二元与多类间的本质差异，并首次给出全多项式时间、维度无关的鲁棒学习算法，为后续研究开辟了方向。

Abstract: We study the task of agnostic learning of multiclass linear classifiers under the Gaussian distribution. Given labeled examples $(x, y)$ from a distribution over $\mathbb{R}^d \times [k]$, with Gaussian $x$-marginal, the goal is to output a hypothesis whose error is comparable to that of the best $k$-class linear classifier. While the binary case $k=2$ has a well-developed algorithmic theory, much less is known for $k \ge 3$. Even for $k=3$, prior robust algorithms incur exponential dependence on the inverse of the desired accuracy in both complexity and representation size. In this work, we develop new structural results for multiclass linear classifiers and use them to design fully polynomial-time robust learners with dimension-independent error guarantees. Our first result shows that the standard multiclass perceptron algorithm requires super-polynomially many samples and updates, even with clean labels and Gaussian marginals, revealing a basic obstruction absent in the binary case. Our main positive result is a pairwise improper-learning framework which yields an efficient learner with error $\widetilde O(k^{3/2}\sqrt{\mathrm{opt}})+ε$ for general $k$. Additionally, we develop a sharper localization-based framework which leads to error $O(\mathrm{opt})+ε$ for $k=3$, and error $\mathrm{poly}(k)\mathrm{opt}+ε$ for geometrically regular $k$-class linear classifiers.

</details>


### [200] [torchtune: PyTorch native post-training library](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21442)
*Mark Obozov,Maxime Griot,Joseph Cummings,Evan Smothers,Felipe Mello,Rafi Ayub,Philip John Bontrager,Salman Mohammadi,Ariel Kwiatkowski,Nathan Azrak,Mircea Mironenco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: torchtune 是一个 PyTorch 原生库，专注于 LLM 后训练阶段，强调模块化、可扩展性和透明性，在性能和内存效率上与 Axolotl、Unsloth 等框架相当，同时更利于研究迭代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微调框架（如 Axolotl、Unsloth）虽然在易用性或硬件效率上做了优化，但往往牺牲了透明性和可扩展性。torchtune 旨在提供一个既高效又透明的后训练工具，便于研究者深入理解和定制。

Method: 基于 PyTorch 原生组件构建，设计上强调模块化的模型构建器、训练配方和分布式训练栈，通过直接暴露底层 PyTorch 组件来保证可 hack 性。

Result: 在多个代表性后训练设置下，torchtune 在性能和内存效率上与 Axolotl、Unsloth 等主流框架表现相当，同时保持了更高的灵活性和透明度。

Conclusion: torchtune 可作为 LLM 后训练研究中可复现、可迭代的实用基础框架。

Abstract: Modern LLMs typically require multistage training pipelines to achieve strong downstream performance, with post-training serving as the main interface for adapting open-weight models. We introduce torchtune, a PyTorch-native library designed to streamline the post-training lifecycle of LLMs, enabling efficient fine-tuning, experimentation, and deployment-oriented workflows. Unlike many existing fine-tuning frameworks, which often optimize for ease of use, specialized recipes, or hardware efficiency at the cost of transparency and extensibility, torchtune emphasizes modularity, hackability, and direct access to the underlying PyTorch components. In this paper, we present the design principles behind torchtune, describe how they are reflected in its model builders, training recipes, and distributed training stack, and evaluate the library across representative post-training settings. We compare against popular fine-tuning frameworks, including Axolotl and Unsloth, and show that torchtune provides strong performance and memory efficiency across many settings while remaining flexible enough for rapid research iteration. These results position torchtune as a practical foundation for reproducible LLMs post-training research.

</details>


### [201] [A Machine Learning Framework for Weighted Least Squares GNSS Positioning based on Activation Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21461)
*Pin-Hsun Lee,Harry Leib*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对城市峡谷中GNSS信号质量退化导致的定位误差问题，本文提出了一种基于机器学习与激活函数的加权最小二乘定位框架，利用信号质量指标作为训练特征，通过集成学习预测质量分数并经由激活函数转换为权重，在香港和东京的实测数据中验证了该方法能显著提升定位精度，且具有良好的地理可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市峡谷中高楼和狭窄街道导致的信号遮挡、非视距接收和多径效应会引入GNSS伪距测量误差，尽管多星座GNSS增加了可用卫星数量，但劣质信号的混入反而可能导致严重的定位误差，因此需要一种有效识别和降低劣质信号影响的方法。

Method: 提出一种基于机器学习的加权最小二乘（WLS）定位框架：首先利用多个信号质量指标作为训练特征，通过集成学习算法（如随机森林等）预测信号质量分数；然后采用激活函数（如sigmoid）将预测分数转换为适用于WLS的权重，从而降低劣质信号对定位解算的影响。

Result: 在香港和东京城市区域的真实数据集实验中，sigmoid激活函数在不同机器学习算法和GNSS星座配置下均取得最佳改进效果；算法在单星座和多星座场景下均显著降低了定位误差，且在不同城市间（香港与东京）具有强地理可迁移性，在相似城市化程度的区域数据上训练后性能保持稳定。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于机器学习与激活函数的WLS定位框架能有效提升城市峡谷环境下的GNSS定位精度，具有良好的泛化能力和跨区域适用性。

Abstract: Global Navigation Satellite Systems (GNSS) are widely used to provide position, velocity, and timing (PVT) information for various applications, including transportation, location-based communication services, and intelligent agriculture. In urban canyons, high-rise buildings and narrow streets can cause signal obstruction, non-line-of-sight (NLOS) reception, and multipath effects that introduce errors in GNSS pseudorange measurements. Although multi-constellations GNSS effectively increase the number of available satellites, the inclusion of degraded signals can lead to severe positioning errors. This study proposes a machine learning framework for the weighted least squares (WLS) algorithm incorporating activation functions to enhance positioning accuracy. Several signal quality indicators are employed as training features for ensemble learning algorithms to identify poor quality signals by providing quality scores. Then, activation functions are employed to transform the machine learning predicted scores to appropriate weights for WLS positioning. To evaluate the performance of our approach, experiments are conducted using real-world datasets from Hong Kong and Tokyo urban areas. Comparative analysis of activation functions reveals that sigmoid functions consistently yield the greatest improvements with different machine learning algorithms and GNSS constellation configurations. The proposed algorithm demonstrates substantial reductions in positioning errors for both single- and multiconstellation scenarios. Furthermore, our results indicate that the proposed algorithm exhibits strong geographical transferability. The proposed algorithm maintains comparable level of performance when trained on data from other regions with similar levels of urbanization.

</details>


### [202] [You Only Need Minimal RLVR Training: Extrapolating LLMs via Rank-1 Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21468)
*Zhepei Wei,Xinyu Zhu,Wei-Lin Chen,Chengsong Huang,Jiaxin Huang,Yu Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现RLVR（基于可验证奖励的强化学习）中权重的参数轨迹具有极低秩和高度可预测的特性，并提出RELEX方法——通过观察短窗口内的秩1子空间并利用线性回归外推未来检查点，仅需15%的训练步数即可匹配或超越完整RLVR性能，且能外推至10-20倍于观察窗口的长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管RLVR已成为提升LLM推理能力的主流范式，但其参数轨迹的底层几何结构尚未被充分探索。理解这种结构有助于揭示RLVR的内在工作机制，并可能为更高效的计算方法提供指导。

Method: RELEX方法：从短观察窗口估计参数增量的秩1子空间，然后通过线性回归外推未来检查点。该方法无需学习模型，仅需对参数轨迹进行低秩近似和线性预测。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B、Qwen3-4B-Base和Qwen3-8B-Base三个模型上，RELEX仅需15%的RLVR训练步数即可匹配或超越完整训练性能；能外推至观察窗口10-20倍的长度且性能持续提升；消融实验表明增加秩或采用非线性建模均无额外收益。

Conclusion: RELEX的成功源于其"去噪"效应：通过将参数更新投影到秩1子空间，模型丢弃了随机优化噪声，从而在外推时避免性能退化。这表明RLVR的参数轨迹本质上由单一主导方向驱动，具有惊人的可预测性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a dominant paradigm for improving reasoning in large language models (LLMs), yet the underlying geometry of the resulting parameter trajectories remains underexplored. In this work, we demonstrate that RLVR weight trajectories are extremely low-rank and highly predictable. Specifically, we find that the majority of downstream performance gains are captured by a rank-1 approximation of the parameter deltas, where the magnitude of this projection evolves near-linearly with training steps. Motivated by this, we propose a simple and compute-efficient method RELEX (REinforcement Learning EXtrapolation), which estimates the rank-1 subspace from a short observation window and extrapolates future checkpoints via linear regression, with no learned model required. Across three models (i.e., Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B, Qwen3-4B-Base, and Qwen3-8B-Base), RELEX produces checkpoints that match or exceed RLVR performance on both in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks, requiring as few as 15% steps of full RLVR training. Remarkably, RELEX is able to extrapolate far beyond the observation window at no training cost, predicting checkpoints up to 10-20$\times$ beyond the observed prefix with continued improvement (e.g., observe only the first 50 steps and extrapolate to 1000 steps). Our ablation analysis confirms the minimalist sufficiency of RELEX: neither increasing the subspace rank nor employing non-linear modeling yields further gains in extrapolation. Finally, we show that RELEX's success stems from a "denoising" effect: by projecting updates onto the rank-1 subspace, the model discards stochastic optimization noise that would otherwise degrade performance during extrapolation. Our code is available at https://github.com/weizhepei/RELEX.

</details>


### [203] [Agent JIT Compilation for Latency-Optimizing Web Agent Planning and Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21470)
*Caleb Winston,Ron Yifeng Wang,Azalia Mirhoseini,Christos Kozyrakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种"即时编译"方法，将自然语言任务描述直接编译为可执行代码，通过计划生成、并行调度和不变性协议三大组件，在Web自动化任务中实现10.4倍加速和28%的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CUA系统采用顺序的抓取-截图-执行循环，每轮迭代都需要LLM调用，导致高延迟和频繁的工具使用错误，亟需更高效的替代方案。

Method: 提出Agent JIT编译方法，包含三个组件：(1) JIT-Planner：生成多个代码计划并验证工具规范后选择最低成本候选；(2) JIT-Scheduler：通过蒙特卡洛成本估计探索并行化策略；(3) 不变性工具协议：通过前置/后置条件约束减少工具使用错误的计划生成。

Result: 在5个Web应用中，JIT-Planner相比Browser-Use实现10.4倍加速和+28%准确率提升；JIT-Scheduler相比OpenAI CUA实现2.4倍加速和+9%准确率提升。

Conclusion: Agent JIT编译通过将任务编译为包含LLM调用、工具调用和并行化的可执行代码，显著降低了延迟并提升了准确率，为CUA系统提供了高效替代方案。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUA) automate tasks specified with natural language such as "order the cheapest item from Taco Bell" by generating sequences of calls to tools such as click, type, and scroll on a browser. Current implementations follow a sequential fetch-screenshot-execute loop where each iteration requires an LLM call, resulting in high latency and frequent errors from incorrect tool use. We present agent just-in-time (JIT) compilation, an alternative that compiles task descriptions directly into executable code that is free to include LLM calls, tool calls, and parallelization. Our approach comprises three components: (1) JIT-Planner, which generates multiple code plans, validates each against tool specifications, and selects the minimum-cost candidate; (2) JIT-Scheduler, which explores parallelization strategies via Monte Carlo cost estimation from learned latency distributions; and (3) an invariant-enforcing tool protocol specifying precondition and postcondition state requirements that reduce the rate of generating plans with incorrect tool use. Across 5 web applications, JIT-Planner achieves $10.4\times$ speedup and $+28\%$ accuracy over Browser-Use, while JIT-Scheduler achieves $2.4\times$ speedup and $+9\%$ accuracy over OpenAI CUA.

</details>


### [204] [Is Fixing Schema Graphs Necessary? Full-Resolution Graph Structure Learning for Relational Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21475)
*Yi Huang,Qingyun Sun,Jia Li,Xingcheng Fu,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FROG框架，将关系数据库中的表格角色建模为可学习的节点/边角色，通过角色驱动的消息传递机制联合优化图结构与GNN表示，并引入函数依赖约束保证语义一致性，在关系预测任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关系深度学习（RDL）方法在将关系数据库构建为图时，普遍采用全分辨率属性作为设计原则，导致图结构固定不可优化，无法灵活捕捉表与表之间的复杂语义关系。

Method: 提出FROG框架，将关系结构学习建模为可学习的表角色建模问题，使表格可以作为节点或边参与消息传递；设计角色驱动的消息传递机制捕捉关系语义；引入函数依赖约束，在表和实体层面正则化表示，实现图结构与GNN表示的联合优化。

Result: 大量实验表明FROG优于现有方法，并揭示了表角色对下游任务的影响，为RDL中的图构建提供了新见解。

Conclusion: 图结构是可学习的而非固定的，通过可微的表角色建模和联合优化能更好地捕捉关系数据库中的语义信息，提升关系预测性能。

Abstract: Relational prediction tasks are fundamental in many real-world applications, where data are naturally stored in relational databases (RDBs). Relational Deep Learning (RDL) addresses this problem by modeling RDBs as graphs and applying graph neural networks (GNNs) for end-to-end learning. However, the full-resolution property is commonly adopted as a design principle in graph construction for RDBs to preserve relational semantics, which leads most existing methods to rely on fixed graph structures. In this paper, we propose FROG, a Full-Resolution and Optimizable Graph Structure Learning} framework for RDL that formulates relational structure learning as a learnable table role modeling problem, allowing tables to contribute as nodes and edges in message passing. We further design role-driven message passing mechanisms to capture relational semantics, enabling joint optimization of graph structure and GNN representations. To ensure semantic consistency, we introduce functional dependency constraints that regularize representations across table and entity levels. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms existing approaches and reveal how table roles impact downstream tasks, offering new insights into graph construction for RDL

</details>


### [205] [EvoStruct: Bridging Evolutionary and Structural Priors for Antibody CDR Design via Protein Language Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21485)
*Mansoor Ahmed,Sujin Lee,Umar Khayaz,Murray Patterson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对抗体CDR设计中GNN方法存在的词汇坍塌问题，提出EvoStruct模型，通过冻结的蛋白质语言模型与E(3)-等变GNN的交叉注意力适配器结合，并采用渐进式PLM解冻和R-Drop一致性正则化，在CHIMERA-Bench上实现最高序列恢复率和最低困惑度，同时显著提升氨基酸多样性和结合对相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前用于抗体CDR设计的最佳等变GNN方法虽然序列恢复率最高，但存在严重的词汇坍塌问题，即过度预测极少数氨基酸（如酪氨酸和甘氨酸），而忽略了功能重要的残基。这一失败源于GNN编码器从有限的结构数据中从头学习氨基酸分布，丢弃了进化数据库中编码的替换模式。

Method: EvoStruct模型通过交叉注意力适配器将冻结的蛋白质语言模型（PLM）与E(3)-等变GNN的3D结构上下文桥接起来。针对CDR设计特有的词汇坍塌问题，采用渐进式PLM解冻和R-Drop一致性正则化策略，区别于通用的PLM-结构适配器方法。

Result: 在CHIMERA-Bench数据集上，EvoStruct相比最佳GNN基线：序列恢复率提升16%，困惑度降低43%，氨基酸多样性提升2.3倍，并实现了与真实标签最高的结合对相关性。

Conclusion: EvoStruct通过融合进化信息（PLM）和结构信息（等变GNN），有效解决了抗体CDR设计中的词汇坍塌问题，在多项指标上超越现有方法。

Abstract: Equivariant graph neural network (GNN) methods for antibody complementarity-determining region (CDR) design achieve the highest sequence recovery but suffer from severe vocabulary collapse. The current best GNN methods over-predict very few amino acids, such as tyrosine and glycine, while ignoring functionally important residues. We trace this failure to GNN encoders learning amino acid distributions de novo from limited structural data, discarding substitution patterns encoded in evolutionary databases. To resolve this, we propose EvoStruct, which bridges a frozen protein language model (PLM) with 3D structural context from an E(3)-equivariant GNN via a cross-attention adapter. Unlike prior PLM-structure adapters for general protein design, EvoStruct targets the vocabulary collapse problem specific to CDR design through progressive PLM unfreezing and R-Drop consistency regularization. On the CHIMERA-Bench dataset, EvoStruct achieves the highest amino acid recovery and lowest perplexity among several antibody design methods, improving sequence recovery by 16% and reducing perplexity by 43% relative to the best GNN baselines, while recovering 2.3x greater amino acid diversity and the highest binding-pair correlation with ground truth.

</details>


### [206] [Quantifying Hyperparameter Transfer and the Importance of Embedding Layer Learning Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21486)
*Dayal Singh Kalra,Maissam Barkeshli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个量化超参数迁移的框架，并通过大量消融实验发现，μP相比SP在AdamW训练中的优势主要源于它最大化了嵌入层的学习率，而SP中嵌入层学习率是瓶颈。同时研究了权重衰减对迁移的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超参数迁移对于训练大规模语言模型至关重要，现有方法包括拟合缩放律或选择特定参数化（如μP）。但μP相对于标准参数化(SP)为何能提供高质量的学习率迁移，现有理论尚不完善，需要深入理解其根本原因。

Method: 首先构建了一个量化超参数迁移的框架，包含三个指标：(1)缩放律拟合质量、(2)外推鲁棒性、(3)参数化选择导致的渐近损失惩罚。然后通过一系列全面的消融实验，研究μP相对于SP的优势来源。

Result: μP相对于SP的优势主要源于最大化嵌入层学习率；SP中嵌入层学习率是瓶颈，导致训练不稳定；将SP的嵌入层学习率按宽度因子放大以匹配μP，可显著平滑训练并改善超参数迁移。权重衰减改善缩放律拟合，但在固定token-per-parameter设置下损害外推鲁棒性。

Conclusion: μP的高质量超参数迁移效果主要归因于嵌入层学习率的正确处理，而非复杂的参数化策略本身。

Abstract: Hyperparameter transfer allows extrapolating optimal optimization hyperparameters from small to large scales, making it critical for training large language models (LLMs). This is done either by fitting a scaling law to the hyperparameters or by a judicious choice of parameterization, such as Maximal Update ($μ$P), that renders optimal hyperparameters approximately scale invariant. In this paper, we first develop a framework to quantify hyperparameter transfer through three metrics: (1) the quality of the scaling law fit, (2) the robustness to extrapolation errors, and (3) the asymptotic loss penalty due to choice of parameterization. Next, we investigate through a comprehensive series of ablations why $μ$P appears to offer high-quality learning rate transfer relative to standard parameterization (SP), as existing theory is inadequate. We find that the overwhelming benefit of $μ$P relative to SP when training with AdamW arises simply from maximizing the learning rate of the embedding layer. In SP, the embedding layer learning rate acts as a bottleneck that induces training instabilities; increasing it by a factor of width to match $μ$P dramatically smooths out training while improving hyperparameter transfer. We also find that weight decay improves the scaling law fits, while, in the fixed token-per-parameter setting, it hurts the robustness of the extrapolation.

</details>


### [207] [Variance Reduction for Expectations with Diffusion Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21489)
*Jesse Bettencourt,Xindi Wu,Matan Atzmon,James Lucas,Jonathan Lorraine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CARV框架，通过分层蒙特卡洛估计、噪声重采样、时间步重要性采样和分层逆CDF构建，在不改变目标函数的情况下，在文本到3D蒸馏和归因实验中实现2-3倍有效计算倍数提升，单步蒸馏中梯度方差降低一个数量级但下游FID未改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练扩散模型作为冻结教师模型用于文本到3D、单步蒸馏和数据归因等下游任务时，教师梯度的蒙特卡洛估计方差主导了计算成本，因为每次采样都需要昂贵的上游计算（渲染、模拟、编码），亟需降低方差以提高计算效率。

Method: 提出CARV（计算感知方差核算框架），核心是分层蒙特卡洛估计器：将昂贵的上游计算摊销到廉价的扩散噪声重采样上，结合时间步重要性采样和分层逆CDF（累积分布函数）构造来进一步优化。

Result: 文本到3D蒸馏和归因实验中实现2-3倍有效计算倍数提升（主要来自摊销重采样，约25%来自重要性采样+分层）；单步蒸馏中梯度方差降低一个数量级，但下游FID未改善。

Conclusion: CARV能有效降低梯度估计方差并提升计算效率，但存在适用边界：当MC方差不再是瓶颈时（如单步蒸馏中），方差降低不转化为下游性能提升，这为未来工作指明了方向。

Abstract: Pretrained diffusion models serve as frozen teachers feeding downstream pipelines such as text-to-3D, single-step distillation, and data attribution. The teacher gradients these pipelines consume are Monte Carlo (MC) expectations over noise levels and Gaussian noise samples; their estimator variance dominates compute cost because each draw requires expensive upstream work (rendering, simulation, encoding). We introduce CARV, a compute-aware variance-accounting framework that motivates a hierarchical MC estimator: amortize the expensive upstream computation over cheap diffusion-noise resamples, sharpened by timestep importance sampling and a stratified-inverse-CDF construction. In our text-to-3D distillation and attribution experiments, CARV delivers 2-3x effective compute multipliers (most from amortized reuse; ~25% additional from IS+stratification) without changing the objective; in single-step distillation, the same techniques cut gradient variance by an order of magnitude but do not improve downstream FID, marking the regime where MC variance is no longer the bottleneck.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [208] [E-ReCON: An Energy- and Resource-Efficient Precision-Configurable Sparse nvCIM Macro for Conventional and Spiking Neural Edge Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20717)
*Ankit Kumar Tenwar,Mukul Lokhande,Santosh Kumar Vishvakarma*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出E-ReCON，一款基于紧凑3T1R ReRAM位单元（0.85 µm²）的16 Kb数字存内计算宏，支持CNN和SNN推理。通过交错10T/28T加法树减少面积和功耗，在65 nm CMOS下实现最高419 TOPS/W能效、0.48 ns延迟，并在多个数据集上保持高精度。相比传统ADC型ReRAM-CIM方案，延迟和能效提升约30-40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘AI推理对能效和延迟要求严苛，传统冯·诺依曼架构存在存储墙瓶颈，而现有ReRAM存内计算设计依赖ADC转换，开销大、能效低。需要一种紧凑、可靠且同时支持CNN和SNN的纯数字存内计算方案。

Method: 采用3T1R ReRAM位单元（0.85 µm²）实现AND型存内乘法；提出交错式10T/28T加法树结构替代传统28T RCA，减少37%晶体管数和28%功耗；支持2A2W配置的SNN推理；采用40%剪枝策略减少MAC运算。

Result: 65 nm CMOS @1.2V：最小延迟0.48 ns，吞吐量2.31-3.1 TOPS，能效高达419 TOPS/W。LeNet-5/AlexNet/CNN-8在MNIST-AZ/CIFAR10/SVHN上分别达97.81%/93.23%/96.51%准确率。40%剪枝保留99.8%原精度。SNN任务中2A2W配置接近FP32基线。相较于ADC型方案，延迟和能效提升约30-40%。

Conclusion: E-ReCON作为一种可扩展、低延迟、高能效的非易失性存内计算平台，适用于下一代边缘AI、物联网、生物医学感知和神经形态应用。
