Abstract: This work presents E-ReCON, a 16 Kb energy and resource-efficient digital compute-in-memory (DCIM) macro based on a compact 3T1R ReRAM bitcell for edge-AI inference. The proposed bitcell occupies only 0.85 um^2 and supports reliable AND-based in-memory multiplication for both conventional convolutional neural network (CNN) and spiking neural network (SNN) workloads. To reduce accumulation overhead, a novel interleaved 10T/28T adder tree is introduced, reducing transistor count and power consumption by 37% and 28%, respectively, compared to a conventional 28T RCA-based design. Implemented in 65 nm CMOS at 1.2 V, the proposed macro achieves a minimum latency of 0.48 ns, throughput of 2.31-3.1 TOPS, and energy efficiency of up to 419 TOPS/W. When evaluated on LeNet-5, AlexNet, and CNN-8 models, the macro achieves 97.81%, 93.23%, and 96.51% accuracy on MNIST/A-Z, CIFAR10, and SVHN datasets, respectively. In addition, 40% pruning preserves nearly 99.8% of the original accuracy while reducing MAC operations and computation cycles. For SNN-oriented workloads, the proposed AND-type bitcell efficiently supports spike-weight multiplication with low switching activity, where the 2A2W configuration achieves accuracy close to the FP32 baseline across VGG-8, VGG-16, and ResNet-18 networks on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and ImageNet-1K datasets. Compared to prior ADC-based ReRAM-CIM designs, the proposed architecture improves latency and energy efficiency by nearly 30-40% while maintaining robust operation under full PVT and ReRAM variability. Overall, E-ReCON provides a scalable, low-latency, and energy-efficient nvCIM platform for next-generation edge-AI, IoT, biomedical sensing, and neuromorphic applications.

</details>


### [209] [Privacy-Preserving Distributed Optimization Under Time Constraints Using Secure Multi-Party Computation and Evolutionary Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20944)
*Sebastian Gruber,Tobias Harzfeld,Christoph G. Schuetz,Florian Wohner,Thomas Lorünser*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合进化算法与安全多方计算（MPC）的隐私保护分布式优化方法，适用于时间敏感场景，通过混淆评估结果在保护隐私与解质量之间取得平衡，并在多种优化问题上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私保护分布式优化方法（如MPC）在时间敏感场景下因运行时开销过大，可能导致优化无法在规定时限内完成，亟需一种能降低计算开销、满足截止时间要求且保护隐私的优化方法。

Method: 将进化算法用于解空间搜索，MPC用于解的评估；通过混淆评估结果来防止诚实但好奇的平台提供者推断隐私输入，并在此过程中权衡保护强度与解质量。

Result: 在单目标分配问题、旅行商问题和多目标分配问题（使用NSGA-II）上的实验表明，该方法能在截止时间内返回可行解，且隐私保护与解质量之间存在可量化的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效降低了隐私保护计算对运行时的负面影响，能在时间约束下完成优化任务；混淆评估结果提供了额外的隐私保护，但需根据具体场景权衡保护力度与解质量。

Abstract: In distributed optimization, multiple parties collaborate to find an optimal solution to a problem. Privacy-preserving distributed optimization uses techniques, such as secure multi-party computation (MPC), to protect the private inputs of each party. In time-critical settings, the runtime overhead introduced by privacy-preserving computations may prevent the optimization from finishing within the deadline. This paper presents an approach for privacy-preserving distributed optimization in time-critical settings that combines evolutionary algorithms for solution search and MPC for the evaluation of solutions. The approach reduces the impact of privacy-preserving computations on runtime and allows to return solution within the deadline. Obfuscation of evaluation results provides additional protection for private inputs from an honest-but-curious platform provider, but introduces a potential trade-off between protection and solution quality. This trade-off is investigated in experiments using a genetic algorithm for both the single-objective assignment problem and the traveling salesperson problem, as well as NSGA-II for the multi-objective assignment problem.

</details>


### [210] [Convergence Analysis of Evolution Strategies for Mixed-Integer Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21000)
*Ryoki Hamano,Kento Uchida,Shinichi Shirakawa*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文针对混合整数进化策略（ES）中整数变量处理导致的收敛问题，首次提供了理论收敛性分析。通过对比两种（1+1）-ES变体——(1+1)-LB-ES（对整数变量标准差设下界）和(1+1)-LUB-ES（同时设上下界），证明了LB-ES在大整数变量数时会出现早熟收敛，而LUB-ES在适当参数设置下可实现线性收敛，为混合整数ES的设计提供了理论指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合整数ES中，对整数坐标离散化处理时通常会对整数变量的标准差设置下界以防止早熟收敛，但这种方法会拖慢连续变量的收敛速度，且缺乏清晰的理论解释。现有研究主要依赖经验结果，缺少理论分析来阐明这种整数处理方式对收敛性能的实质影响。

Method: 受连续域(1+1)-ES漂移分析启发，提出两种混合整数(1+1)-ES变体：(1+1)-LB-ES（仅对整数变量标准差设下界）和(1+1)-LUB-ES（同时设上下界）。聚焦整数变量优化后的连续变量优化阶段，在混合整数基准函数上严格分析其收敛行为。

Result: (1+1)-LB-ES当整数变量数量较大时会出现早熟收敛；(1+1)-LUB-ES在合适的参数设置下能够实现线性收敛。

Conclusion: 该研究首次为混合整数ES的收敛性提供了理论分析，揭示了整数变量处理方式对收敛性能的影响机制，为设计更高效的混合整数ES算法提供了理论依据和参数设置指导。

Abstract: Mixed-integer extensions of evolution strategies (ES) that discretize selected coordinates of sampled continuous vectors often impose a lower bound on the standard deviation of integer variables to prevent premature convergence. While these methods show promising empirical results, this handling can slow the convergence of continuous variables, and its impact has lacked a clear theoretical account. In this paper, we provide a convergence analysis of evolution strategies for mixed-integer optimization, inspired by the drift analysis of the (1+1)-ES in the continuous domain. Specifically, we consider two (1+1)-ES variants for mixed-integer domains: (1+1)-LB-ES, which introduces a lower bound on the standard deviation for integer variables, and (1+1)-LUB-ES, which combines both lower and upper bounds to enhance the convergence of the continuous variables. Focusing on the optimization phase after the integer variables have been optimized, we rigorously analyze their convergence behavior on a benchmark function designed for mixed-integer domains. Our results show that (1+1)-LB-ES can suffer from premature convergence when the number of integer variables is large, while (1+1)-LUB-ES achieves linear convergence under suitable parameter settings. These findings provide theoretical insights into the impact of integer handling on convergence performance and guidance for the design of mixed-integer ES.

</details>


### [211] [Genetic Programming with Transformer-Based Mutation for Approximate Circuit Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21055)
*Ondrej Galeta,Lukas Sekanina*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Transformer的新型变异算子用于笛卡尔遗传规划（CGP），通过混合变异策略和专用训练方案，在近似算术电路自动设计领域取得了超越现有EvoApproxLib库的优化效果，实现了更好的accuracy-area trade-offs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用机器学习模型改进进化设计与优化过程，解决CGP中标准变异算子在近似电路设计中的停滞问题，产生更优的近似算术电路。

Method: 提出基于Transformer的变异算子用于CGP；设计混合方案，在Transformer变异算子和标准变异算子之间切换以防止停滞；开发新的Transformer训练方案，使用数千个CGP染色体组成的训练向量（代表各种近似乘法器）。

Result: 在多个目标误差约束下，使用Transformer变异算子的CGP进化的近似乘法器在trade-offs上优于EvoApproxLib库中高度优化的设计。

Conclusion: 尽管训练和进化过程计算成本高，但它们是改进现有近似电路和生成新的、潜在可专利化电路设计的必要步骤。

Abstract: A recent trend is to leverage machine learning models to improve the evolutionary design and optimization process. We propose a novel transformer-based mutation operator for Cartesian genetic programming (CGP) for the automated design of approximate arithmetic circuits. We introduce a hybrid scheme for CGP in which the proposed mutation operator is switched with the standard mutation operator to prevent stagnation of the circuit approximation process. We also develop a new training scheme for the underlying transformer that utilizes training vectors composed of thousands of CGP chromosomes representing various approximate multipliers. For several target error constraints, the approximate multipliers evolved with CGP utilizing the transformer-based mutation achieve better trade-offs than the highly optimized designs available in the state-of-the-art EvoApproxLib library of approximate circuits. Although both training and evolutionary processes are computationally demanding, they appear to be necessary steps for improving existing approximate circuits and producing new, potentially patentable circuit designs.

</details>


### [212] [How to Build Marcus's Algebraic Mind: Algebro-Deterministic Substrate over Galois Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21379)
*Hiroyuki Chuma,Kanji Otsuk,Yoichi Sato*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出PyVaCoAl/VaCoAl架构——一种基于XOR-and-shift原语的超维计算系统，实现了Marcus提出的认知架构三大支柱（变量操作、递归结构表示、个体/种类区分），并证明其与齿状回-CA3回路存在生物同源性，同时扩展至Pearl反事实推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二十五年前Marcus指出标准多层感知机无法支撑认知架构的三大核心要素（变量操作、递归结构、个体/种类区分），并提出利用发育程序而非梯度下降构建寄存器与treelets的神经实现方案。本文旨在证明这种所需的计算基质现已可用，并展示其生物学对应物。

Method: 构建PyVaCoAl/VaCoAl架构，核心采用单一代数原语：GF(2)域上的XOR-and-shift操作，通过本原多项式线性反馈移位寄存器实现。具体包括：可逆变量绑定（Bind(R,F)=R XOR shift(F)）、不可交换组合捆绑（区分"狗咬人"与"人咬狗"）、地址空间个体/种类分离。同时论证齿状回-CA3回路是该架构的生物同源物，发育指定的苔藓纤维投射提供了Marcus预期的先天微电路。

Result: 该架构实现了Marcus要求的全部三大支柱，且相比2001年可用的张量积、循环卷积或时间同步方法更接近Marcus的规范。将treelet重新解释为由本原生成多项式索引的代数寄存器集，并展示该架构自然扩展至Pearl第三层级（反事实推理）的能力——这是原始treelet程序未直接设计的目标。

Conclusion: PyVaCoAl/VaCoAl提供了满足Marcus认知架构规范的功能性神经基质，其代数原语的自然扩展能够支持反事实推理，标志着从程序性猜想走向可实现计算架构的关键进展。

Abstract: In The Algebraic Mind, Gary Marcus identified three components essential for any adequate cognitive architecture: operations over variables, recursively structured representations, and a distinction between mental representations of individuals and kinds. He argued that standard multilayer perceptrons supported none of these, acknowledging that a neural implementation using registers and treelets, constructed via developmental programs rather than gradient descent, remained a programmatic conjecture. Twenty-five years later, the required substrate is now available. Our newly developed PyVaCoAl/VaCoAl is a hyperdimensional computing architecture organized end-to-end around a single algebraic primitive: XOR-and-shift over GF(2), implemented by primitive-polynomial linear-feedback shift registers. The architecture supports reversible variable binding via Bind(R,F) = R XOR shift(F), non-commutative compositional bundling that distinguishes "the dog bites the man" from "the man bites the dog," and address-space individual/kind separation under the same algebra. A companion perspective argues that the dentate gyrus-CA3 circuit is a biological homologue of this same engine, with developmentally specified mossy-fiber targeting supplying the innate microcircuitry Marcus anticipated. In this paper, we map the correspondence between Marcus's three pillars and the operational commitments of PyVaCoAl/VaCoAl. We reinterpret the treelet as an algebraic register set indexed by a primitive generator polynomial, arguing that this architecture provides a functional neural substrate meeting Marcus's specifications far more closely than the tensor products, circular convolution, or temporal synchrony available in 2001. We also demonstrate how this substrate naturally extends to Pearl's rung-3 counterfactual reasoning, a capability the original treelet program did not directly target.

</details>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [213] [Fifty Years of Transaction Processing Research (extended)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20466)
*Philip A. Bernstein*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文回顾了事务研究的早期历史，解释了事务研究至今仍在继续的原因（尽管看似是一个已解决的问题），并对未来进行了展望。本文是SIGMOD-Companion '25上发表论文的扩展版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回顾事务研究的早期历史，解释为什么事务研究在看似已解决的情况下仍然持续活跃，并展望未来发展方向。

Method: 通过作者的亲身经历和视角，对事务研究的历史进行回顾性叙述，分析其持续活力的原因，并基于当前趋势进行未来展望。

Result: 事务研究虽然看似是一个已解决的问题，但由于新兴应用场景（如分布式系统、新硬件、云环境等）带来的新挑战，该领域仍然充满活力。

Conclusion: 事务研究并未终结，它将继续随着计算环境的变化而演变，未来仍有广阔的研究空间。

Abstract: In this short paper, I recount some early history of transaction research (including some of my own), explain why transaction research continues to this day (even though it seems to be a solved problem), and speculate about its future. This is an extended version of the paper that appeared in the Companion of the 2025 International Conference on Management of Data (SIGMOD-Companion '25).

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [214] [Adaptive Probe-based Steering for Robust LLM Jailbreaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20286)
*Junxi Chen,Junhao Dong,Xiaohua Xie*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模型提取的自适应对比引导方法，通过动态调整引导强度来改进大语言模型的对齐破坏攻击，将平均危害分数从6%提升至70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比引导方法依赖有限的、存在固有偏见的对比提示，且需要繁琐的手动引导强度调节，限制了其鲁棒性和有效性。

Method: 利用模型提取的思想引导学习到的引导向量逼近理想向量，并基于对比激活的统计特征自适应调整引导强度。

Result: 该方法显著提升了基于探测的引导的有效性和鲁棒性，无需额外的对比提示或手动调参，将平均危害分数从6%提升至70%。

Conclusion: 本文通过揭露强化后LLM的防御漏洞，证明自适应对比引导是一种高效且鲁棒的对齐破坏攻击方法。

Abstract: Recent work has demonstrated the potential of contrastive steering for jailbreaking Large Language Models (LLMs). However, existing methods rely on limited and inherently biased contrastive prompts and require laborious manual tuning of steering strength, limiting their robustness and effectiveness. In this paper, we leverage the idea of model extraction to guide the learned steering vectors to approximate the ideal one and propose tuning the steering strength adaptively based on contrastive activations' statistics. Experiments demonstrate that our method notably improves the effectiveness and robustness of probe-based steering, without any extra contrastive prompts or laborious manual tuning. Being an attack paper, this paper focuses on revealing the breakdown of fortified LLMs, raising the average harmfulness score from 6\% to 70\%. Our code is available at https://github.com/fhdnskfbeuv/adaptiveSteering.

</details>


### [215] [Pramana: A Protocol-Layer Treatment of Claim Verification in Autonomous Agent Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20312)
*Ravi Kiran Kadaboina*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Pramana 定义了一种标准化的验证工件格式（ClaimAttestation），将智能体输出分为四类（测量、推理、类比、引用），每类附带可复现的 verify() 操作。通过 TLA+ 形式化验证（38,563 个可达状态，零违规）和 Python 参考实现（84 项测试）保证其正确性。初步实验显示 LLM 评判的假阳性率存在 40 个百分点的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自主智能体的验证实践分裂为两类：概率性判决模式（如自一致性投票）只产生判断而非可审计工件；产生工件的模式（如 RAG、工具增强追踪）则使用供应商特定记录，外部审计者无法在不进行定制集成的情况下重构验证过程。需要一种标准化的验证工件格式。

Method: 提出 Pramana，定义 ClaimAttestation 数据类型，包含四种变体（MeasurementClaim、InferenceClaim、AnalogyClaim、CitationClaim），每种附带 verify() 操作。使用 TLA+ 进行形式化建模和 TLC 模型检验，在三个对称缩减模型上验证。提供 Python 参考实现。通过 A2A 和 MCP 协议扩展提供部署层不变量。

Result: TLA+ 形式化验证覆盖 38,563 个不同可达状态，零不变性违规。Python 参考实现通过 84 项测试。探索性实验（n=100, 2,275 次评判调用）发现 LLM 评判的原始假阳性率跨语料库存在 40 个百分点的差异，表明参考解质量是重要影响因素。

Conclusion: Pramana 提供了自主智能体输出的标准化验证工件格式。其结构性论证和形式化验证证明了其正确性。实验部分的目的并非验证 Pramana 本身，而是揭示当前 LLM 评判中的问题。该工作为受监管领域中智能体部署的离线可审计性奠定了基础。

Abstract: Autonomous agents deployed in regulated domains must produce a verification artifact per consequential output: a record an auditor can re-execute offline, capturing what was claimed, against what source, by whom, when, and how. Production verification today splits into two unstandardized halves. Probabilistic verdict patterns (self-consistency voting, reviewer LLM ensembles) produce judgments, not artifacts. Artifact-producing patterns (RAG, tool-augmented traces, generator-verifier loops) produce vendor-specific records no external auditor can reconstruct without bespoke integration.
  Pramana defines the missing wire format. Every consequential agent output is wrapped in a typed ClaimAttestation with one of four variants (measurement, inference, analogy, citation), each paired with a verify() operation against the recorded source. verify() is deterministic for MeasurementClaim and CitationClaim. For InferenceClaim and AnalogyClaim, determinism is conditional on the oracle (audit-replayable when LLM-backed). The four-way typology derives from classical Indian epistemology (pramana, valid means of knowledge).
  The lifecycle is specified in TLA+ and exhaustively verified under TLC across three symmetry-reduced models: 38,563 distinct reachable states, zero invariant violations. The Python reference implementation passes 84 tests. An A2A and MCP wire-extension manifest layers three deployment-grade invariants: reachability, SLA bound, and offline re-verifiability.
  An exploratory pilot (n=100, 2,275 reviewer calls) probes LLM-as-judge in code generation. The strongest observation is a 40-percentage-point raw FPR delta across corpora, consistent with reference-solution quality contributing significantly. The pilot does not validate Pramana on its own; the structural argument and formal verification do that.

</details>


### [216] [Domijn: The Security of Domain Registrars and the Risk of a Domain Name Takeover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20984)
*Koen van Hove,Jeroen van der Ham-de Vos,Roland van Rijswijk-Deij*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究对.nl顶级域名下十大注册商的安全控制措施进行了实证分析，发现注册商在基础安全措施上较为有效，但在双因素认证等高级安全控制方面存在不足。研究表明域名劫持的潜在影响可与勒索软件攻击相匹敌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 域名是组织的关键资产，恶意劫持可能造成重大损失。注册商在防止域名劫持中扮演重要角色，但目前缺乏对其安全控制措施实施情况的系统性实证研究。

Method: 选取.nl ccTLD下最受欢迎的十大注册商，通过实证方法研究其安全控制措施的实施情况，并构建了域名劫持影响模型，与勒索软件和DDoS攻击进行对比分析。

Result: 所有注册商都实施了相对有效的安全措施，但在双因素认证等高级安全控制方面存在不足。域名劫持的潜在影响可与勒索软件攻击相当。

Conclusion: 注册商需要在高级安全控制方面加强，特别是双因素认证的适当实施。域名劫持是一种高风险威胁，其影响不容忽视。

Abstract: Domain names are key assets for organisation. They anchor an organisation's online presence and reputation, and serve as linking pin for web services and, e.g., email. Consequently, a malicious takeover of a domain can lead to significant damages. Organisations register domain names through so-called registrars, a type of business that plays a key role in the domain name industry. This implies that registrars play an important part in safeguarding against malicious takeovers of domains. In this paper we empirically study how registrars implement security controls to prevent against such takeovers. We focus on the top 10 most popular registrars for the .nl ccTLD. We present the results of this study in light of a model for the impact of domain takeovers, that analyses the possible consequence of a takeover. We contrast this against the impact of two other well-known threats: ransomware and DDoS attacks. We find that all registrars in our study implement relatively effective security measures, but that they fall short in more advanced security controls, such as the proper implementation of two-factor authentication. We also find that a domain takeover can have significant impact, potentially equalling that of a ransomware attack.

</details>


### [217] [Security Document Classification with a Fine-Tuned Local Large Language Model: Benchmark Data and an Open-Source System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20368)
*Ivan Dobrovolskyi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了TorchSight，一个基于微调Qwen 3.5 27B模型的开源本地安全文档分类系统，在78,358个样本上训练，在1000份文档上达到95.0%的分类准确率，优于商业云服务（75.4-79.9%），同时保持数据处理在本地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织在扫描文档以识别敏感信息时面临两难：云服务需要将数据发送到外部基础设施，存在隐私风险；而基于规则的本地工具常因缺乏上下文而漏检威胁。因此需要一种既能保持本地控制又能实现高精度分类的解决方案。

Method: 基于Qwen 3.5 27B模型进行微调，训练数据包含78,358个样本，来自13个许可证开放的源和GPT-4生成的合成数据，覆盖7个安全类别和51个子类别。

Result: 在1000份文档的主评估中，模型达到95.0%的类别级准确率（95% CI: 93.5-96.2），而商业云模型在同一提示协议下仅达到75.4-79.9%。在500份独立留出样本上达到93.8%准确率。

Conclusion: 微调的本地模型能够在保持文档处理本地化的同时，实现比商业云服务更准确的安全文档分类，证明本地化方案在性能和隐私控制上具有优势。

Abstract: Organizations that scan documents for sensitive information face a practical problem. Cloud services require data to be sent to external infrastructure, while rule-based tools often miss threats that depend on context. This study presents TorchSight, an open-source local system for security document classification built around a fine-tuned Qwen 3.5 27B model. The model was trained on 78,358 samples from 13 permissively licensed sources and GPT-4 synthetic data covering seven security categories and 51 subcategories. In the main evaluation on 1,000 documents, the model reached 95.0% category-level accuracy (95% confidence interval: 93.5-96.2). The tested commercial models scored 75.4-79.9% under the same prompting protocol. On a separate external set of 500 held-out samples, the model reached 93.8% accuracy, which suggests that performance extends beyond the main benchmark, although the margin depends on dataset composition and difficult boundary cases. The results show that a fine-tuned local model can support accurate security document classification while keeping document processing under local control.

</details>


### [218] [Trusted Weights, Treacherous Optimizations? Optimization-Triggered Backdoor Attacks on LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20641)
*Yifei Wang,Tianlin Li,Xiaohan Zhang,Yida Yang,Xiaoyu Zhang,Li Pan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次揭示LLM编译优化存在数值副作用，可被恶意利用植入隐蔽后门：在编译前后模型行为不一致，特定输入或通用触发器仅在编译优化后劫持模型输出，攻击成功率达90%，而干净准确率几乎不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM部署广泛使用编译优化技术，但默认假设编译前后图语义等价，忽略了数值差异可能被利用的安全风险。

Method: 提出统一优化触发攻击框架，包含两种互补策略：1) 特定输入触发，仅在编译后翻转预测；2) 通用触发器，未编译时休眠，编译后劫持任意输入。两种攻击均无需修改编译器或硬件。

Result: 在四种主流开源LLM和四个任务上，攻击成功率达90%，干净准确率保持近100%。

Conclusion: 揭示了LLM部署管线中优化与安全交叉处的新型攻击面，并研究了实用防御措施。

Abstract: Inference optimization is a vital technique for deploying LLMs at scale. Compilation is the most widely adopted optimization technique for LLMs. While it assumes semantic equivalence between the original and compiled graphs, we first uncover its numerical side effects can be maliciously exploited to implant stealthy backdoors in LLMs. We propose a unified optimization-triggered attack framework comprising two complementary strategies. Without any modification to the compiler or hardware, one strategy flips predictions for specific inputs only when the model is compiled, while the other uses a universal trigger that remains dormant under uncompiled execution but hijacks arbitrary inputs once compilation optimization is applied. Both attacks bypass standard safety evaluations run without compilation. We empirically demonstrate that these optimization-triggered backdoors achieve attack success rates averaging 90% across four mainstream open-source LLMs and four tasks, while clean accuracy is preserved at nearly 100% under all settings. Our findings reveal a novel attack surface at the intersection of optimization and security in the LLM deployment pipeline, and we investigate practical defenses to mitigate this threat.

</details>


### [219] [Rethinking Fraud Safety Evaluation: Multi-Round Attacks Reveal Safety-Utility Tradeoffs in Graph-Context LLM Defenders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20759)
*Laura Jiang,Reza Ryan,Qian Li,Nasim Ferdosian*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明单轮安全评估不足以反映真实欺诈防御场景，提出多轮攻击下的评估框架。通过图上下文防御器在重放和自适应攻击下能提前安全拒绝，但会导致良性过度拒绝。归因分析表明，问题出在LLM对结构化上下文的消费方式而非图编码器质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单轮安全评估无法模拟真实欺诈攻击中攻击者多轮升级的行为，论文旨在建立更贴近实际的多轮防御评估方法。

Method: 基于Fraud-R1构建冻结多轮套件，对比图上下文防御器与纯文本基线在重放和自适应多轮攻击下的表现；通过探针分析、shuffle-risk消融实验定位性能瓶颈。

Result: 图上下文防御器提前安全拒绝能力更强，但良性过度拒绝显著增加；图编码器能清晰区分欺诈与良性样本，但LLM主要对结构化图字段的存在响应，对风险分数大小响应较弱且不对称。

Conclusion: 稳健的欺诈评估必须采用多轮设定、报告拒绝时机、同时衡量欺诈侧安全增益和良性侧误报，并定位成本来源是图信号还是LLM的消费方式。

Abstract: Single-turn safety evaluation is a poor proxy for real fraud defense, where attackers escalate across multiple rounds. This paper evaluates fraud defenders under replay and adaptive multi-round attacks and measures when a defender refuses, not just whether it eventually refuses. On a frozen multi-round suite built from Fraud-R1, graph-context defenders improve early safe refusal relative to text-only baselines under both replay and adaptive fraud pressure, but they also produce substantially more benign over-refusal. Direct probing of the trained graph encoder, together with paired shuffle-risk ablations on both fraud and benign sides replicated across two seeds on the Qwen-1.5B backbone, localises this cost to how the defender LLM consumes structured context rather than to graph-encoder quality: the encoder cleanly separates fraud from benign, while the LLM responds primarily to the presence of structured graph fields and only secondarily, and asymmetrically, to risk-score magnitude. Temporal graph context is directionally stronger than static and significantly better grounded, but is not yet conclusively superior on the main refusal metrics. The contribution is evaluative and measurement-oriented: robust fraud assessment must be multi-round, must report refusal timing, must account for benign false positives alongside fraud-side safety gains, and must localize observed costs to the graph signal or to how the LLM consumes it.

</details>


### [220] [An IoT-Enabled Smart Home Automation System for Energy Efficiency with Web-Based Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20981)
*Amaan Ahmed,Mohammed Mahir Rahman,Shahzad Memon,Tauseef Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文设计并实现了一种基于树莓派5和环境传感器的智能家居自动化系统，通过实时监测运动、温湿度、光照和烟雾等环境参数，利用PWM动态控制风扇和LED，结合Flask网页仪表盘实现手动控制与实时监控，经测试相比常开模式节能超过46%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在通过低成本、模块化的物联网设备实现家庭能源节约，同时提升用户对家居设备的控制能力与可用性。

Method: 采用迭代式开发模型（sprints），以树莓派5为核心，通过GPIO/I2C接口集成运动、温湿度、光照、烟雾等多种传感器，利用PWM动态调节风扇转速和LED亮度，基于Flask开发网页仪表盘实现手动控制与实时监控，每30秒记录CSV能耗日志。

Result: 在双房间原型测试中，相比设备始终开启模式，实现了超过46%的能耗节省。

Conclusion: 低成本、模块化的物联网方案可以有效提升家庭环境的可持续性和可用性，验证了智能家居自动化在节能方面的可行性与实用性。

Abstract: This paper illustrates the design and implementation of a smart home automation system for the conservation of energy and user control with the help of environmental sensors and Raspberry Pi 5. It monitors real-time conditions like motion, temperature, humidity, light and smoke to automatically control the device's behavior and save energy. A prototype single two-room was developed which uses GPIO/I2C interfaces to integrate sensors and actuators. The fan speed and LED brightness was dynamically controlled using PWM. Manual control and real-time monitoring are made possible through a web dashboard that was developed using Flask and graphical displays, and CSV logs of the energy are taken every 30 seconds. It was designed in an iterative model of sprints and the energy savings during testing was more than 46% over an always-on model. The results prove that with the help of these low-cost, modular devices it is possible to improve sustainability and usability in the home as part of the IoT.

</details>


### [221] [Verifiable Provenance and Watermarking for Generative AI: An Evidentiary Framework for International Operational Law and Domestic Courts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21002)
*Gustav Olaf Yunus Laitinen-Fredriksson Lundström-Imanov,Nurana Abdullayeva*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的证据框架，将加密内容溯源、鲁棒统计水印和零知识证明映射到国际操作法、国内程序和产品监管三种法律体系，并发布包含72000个评估样本的公开基准，对四种代表性方案进行测评，将检测结果转化为各法律体系的充分性阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI创建的逼真多媒体内容使传统取证手段失效，而现有研究将国际操作法、国内程序和产品监管三个法律领域孤立研究，缺乏统一的证据框架来应对AI生成内容带来的法律挑战。

Method: 构建五层威胁模型（朴素再生、对抗清洗、跨模型再生、主动水印移除、内部溯源伪造），跨图像/音频/视频三种模态生成12000个样本，经六种清洗管线处理得到72000个评估样本，评估四种方案并计算TPR@固定FPR、鲁棒性AUC、计算开销和法律充分性评分。

Result: 将经验性检测边界转化为三种法律体系下的法律充分性阈值：武装冲突法下的指挥决策、国内程序中的刑事和民事可采性、欧盟AI法案及类似制度下的持久性审计。

Conclusion: 提供了一个可复现的参考管线、公开基准和模型附件，使律师、工程师和操作人员能够协同部署。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence now synthesizes photorealistic imagery, audio, and video at a cost that defeats traditional forensic intuition. The legal consequences span three regimes studied so far in isolation: international operational law, domestic procedure, and product regulation. This article presents a unified evidentiary framework that maps cryptographic content provenance, robust statistical watermarking, and zero knowledge attestation to the proof requirements of each regime. We define a five tier threat model spanning naive regeneration, adversarial laundering, cross model regeneration, active watermark removal, and insider provenance forgery. We release a public benchmark of 12000 generated items across image, audio, and video modalities under six laundering pipelines for 72000 evaluation samples. We evaluate four representative schemes and report true positive rate at fixed false positive rate, robustness area under the curve, computational overhead, and a regime conditioned legal sufficiency score. We translate empirical detection bounds into legal sufficiency thresholds for command decisions under the law of armed conflict, for criminal and civil admissibility under domestic procedure, and for persistence audits under the European Union Artificial Intelligence Act and analogous regimes. The result is a reproducible reference pipeline, a public benchmark, and model annexes that lawyers, engineers, and operators can deploy together.

</details>


### [222] [An Evidence-driven Protocol for Trustworthy CI Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21089)
*Fernando Castillo,Eduardo Brito,Pille Pullonen-Raudvere,Sebastian Werner,Stefan Tai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种证据驱动的CI管道协议，结合确定性构建系统（DBS）与可信执行环境（TEE），为软件制品提供加密可验证的完整性、真实性和证明保障，将验证开销从冗余计算降为轻量级签名与策略检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业软件供应链面临基础设施攻击威胁，现有的通过消费者重新执行构建和测试来保证制品来源的方式存在验证瓶颈和可信度降低的问题。

Method: 提出证据驱动协议，将确定性构建系统（DBS）与可信执行环境（TEEs）相结合，将确定构建与TEE证明绑定，形式化证据生命周期，并使用Nix和Intel TDX实现实际部署。

Result: 制品验证从冗余计算降为轻量级签名和策略检查，有效摊销TEE引入的初始计算开销。

Conclusion: 证据驱动的CI管道能够在数字基础设施中建立可扩展且可验证的可信机制，为分布式环境下的软件供应链安全提供高效解决方案。

Abstract: Enterprise software supply chains are increasingly vulnerable to infrastructure attacks, resulting in financial and reputational damage. Ensuring the integrity and provenance of software artifacts remains a significant challenge, where re-execution of the build and tests by every consumer to guarantee provenance produces a verification bottleneck and credibility reduction. This paper presents an evidence-driven protocol for trustworthy Continuous Integration (CI) pipelines that combines Deterministic Build Systems (DBS) with Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs). The approach provides cryptographically verifiable guarantees of integrity, authenticity, and attestation for CI artifacts in distributed environments, reducing implicit trust without requiring costly re-execution by consumers. We introduce a protocol that binds deterministic builds with TEE-based attestations, formalizing the evidence life cycle, together with a practical implementation using Nix and Intel TDX. Experimental results show that artifact verification is reduced from redundant computation to lightweight signature and policy checks. These findings demonstrate that evidence-driven CI pipelines establish scalable and verifiable trust in digital infrastructure, effectively amortizing the initial computational overhead introduced by TEEs.

</details>


### [223] [Image Encryption via Data-Identified Discrete Chaotic Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21118)
*Wenyuan Lia,Xiao-Yun Wang,Zhigang Zhu,Xiaofeng Zhang,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于SINDy-PI算法的数据驱动图像加密框架，能够从观测数据中直接学习混沌映射的完整显式动力学，包括交叉项和高阶非线性项，在三个不同混沌系统上验证了其通用性。加密密钥仅包含初始条件，映射结构本身依赖训练数据，即使固定初始条件，不同训练数据也会产生不同映射和完全不同的密文（NPCR≈99.6%，UACI≈33.5%），建立了超越固定映射的混沌密码学新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统混沌加密方案依赖预定义映射，存在映射固定、安全性受限的问题。本文旨在利用数据驱动方法直接从数据中学习混沌映射，使映射结构本身成为数据依赖的，从而增加额外的安全层。

Method: 采用SINDy-PI算法从观测数据中辨识混沌映射的完整显式动力学，包括交叉项和高阶非线性项。加密密钥仅由初始条件构成，映射结构由训练数据决定，训练数据中的微小变化（如噪声种子）会导致不同的映射。

Result: 在Hénon映射、三维Logistic映射和分段线性Lozi映射上验证了通用性。Hénon系统实验显示：信息熵接近理想值8比特；像素间相关性可忽略；对初始条件极端敏感（扰动10⁻¹⁶即导致完全解密失败）；NPCR≈99.6%，UACI≈33.5%，抗差分攻击和统计分析。

Conclusion: 本文建立了超越固定映射的混沌密码学新范式，数据驱动的映射学习为混沌加密提供了更高的安全性和灵活性。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a data-driven image encryption framework that identifies chaotic maps directly from data using the SINDy-PI algorithm. Unlike conventional encryption schemes relying on predefined maps, our method learns the full explicit dynamics -- including cross-terms and higher-order nonlinearities -- from observational data. The validity of this approach is verified on three distinct chaotic systems: the H{é}non map, the three-dimensional logistic map, and the piecewise-linear Lozi map, demonstrating its generality. The encryption key consists solely of initial conditions; the map structure itself becomes data-dependent, introducing an extra layer of security. Moreover, even when the initial conditions are fixed, different training data (e.g., with a tiny noise seed) lead to slightly different maps, which produce completely different ciphertexts (NPCR $\approx 99.6\%$, UACI $\approx 33.5\%$). Numerical experiments on the H{é}non system show near-ideal information entropy ($\approx 8$ bits), negligible inter-pixel correlation, and extreme sensitivity to initial conditions: a perturbation of $10^{-16}$ causes total decryption failure. The scheme resists both differential and statistical attacks, with NPCR and UACI values matching theoretical ideals. Our results establish a new paradigm for chaos-based cryptography beyond fixed maps.

</details>


### [224] [Detecting Trojaned DNNs via Spectral Regression Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21146)
*Samuele Pasini,Jinhan Kim,Paolo Tonella*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MIST是一种利用预激活谱分析模型微调过程中内部表征变化来检测木马后门的防御方法，将木马检测建模为模型更新的回归问题，在无需了解触发器或中毒数据的情况下，单次更新即可实现优于现有方法的检测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代DNN需要反复微调以融入新数据和功能，但更新数据可能不完全可信， adversaries可在微调过程中植入木马后门。现有方法需重构触发条件，存在假设强、效率低的问题。

Method: MIST方法通过分析模型微调过程中内部表征（预激活谱）的变化来检测木马。它先刻画良性模型演化的预激活谱特征作为参考基准，然后通过谱距离度量将待检测的模型更新与参考基准进行比较，将木马检测转化为模型更新的回归问题。

Result: 在四个数据集和八种木马攻击下的实验表明，谱距离能够可靠地区分被植入木马的更新和干净的微调更新。单次更新后检测准确率优于现有最先进方法，且在多次良性演化下仍保持有效，性能退化可控且有界。

Conclusion: 谱演化分析提供了一种稳定且假设信号轻量的恶意模型更新检测方法，不依赖对中毒数据或触发器的先验知识，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: Modern DNNs are repeatedly fine-tuned to incorporate new data and functionality. This evolutionary workflow introduces a security risk when updated data cannot be fully trusted, as adversaries may implant Trojans during fine-tuning. We present MIST, a Trojan detection approach that analyzes how a model's internal representations change during fine-tuning. Rather than attempting to reconstruct trigger conditions, MIST characterizes benign model evolution using pre-activation spectra and flags updates whose spectral deviations are inconsistent with this reference. This framing treats Trojan detection as a regression problem over model updates. An empirical evaluation across four datasets and eight Trojan attacks shows that spectral distances reliably distinguish Trojaned updates from clean fine-tuning. MIST outperforms state-of-the-art detection accuracy after a single update, without requiring any knowledge about the poisoned data or the trigger, and remains effective under multi-step benign evolution, with graceful and bounded degradation. These results indicate that spectral evolution provides a stable and assumption-light signal for detecting malicious model updates.

</details>


### [225] [Information Leakage Envelopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21185)
*Sara Saeidian,Carlos Pinzón,Catuscia Palamidessi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文在逐点最大泄漏（PML）框架下研究隐私保证，提出了PML包络概念——该定义能同时满足后处理鲁棒性和对失败概率（信息泄漏超过阈值的概率）的上界约束。作者首先检验了两种受（近似）差分隐私启发的候选定义，发现均无法同时满足这两个要求；随后引入PML包络，量化了机制输出经任意后处理后关于秘密的最大信息泄漏量。论文分析了包络的基本性质（如单调性），给出了通用上下界，并针对高隐私区间的PML极值机制和随机响应两种广泛使用的隐私机制进行了具体分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的隐私保证定义难以同时满足两个关键要求：后处理鲁棒性（隐私保证在任意下游变换下仍成立）和对失败概率的上界约束（即以高概率控制信息泄漏不超过给定阈值）。本文旨在填补这一空白，提出一个同时满足这两项要求的自然且操作性强的隐私保证定义。

Method: 首先形式化两个候选定义（受近似差分隐私启发），证明其无法同时满足后处理鲁棒性和失败概率上界约束；然后引入PML包络概念，从定义上保证同时满足两个要求；进而推导其基本性质（单调性等）和通用上下界；最后对PML极值机制（高隐私区间）和随机响应两种具体机制进行实例分析。

Result: 证明了受近似差分隐私启发的两种候选定义均无法同时满足后处理鲁棒性和失败概率上界约束；建立了PML包络作为满足这两个要求的有效定义；推导了PML包络的单调性等结构性质及通用上下界；给出了PML极值机制和随机响应下的具体分析结果。

Conclusion: PML包络为隐私保证提供了一个自然且操作性强的定义，它同时保证了后处理鲁棒性和对失败概率的上界控制，适用于任意下游变换场景下的隐私分析。

Abstract: We study privacy guarantees in the framework of pointwise maximal leakage (PML) that satisfy two requirements: they are robust under post-processing and upper bound the failure probability, i.e., the probability that the information leakage exceeds a given threshold. We first examine two candidate definitions inspired by (approximate) differential privacy and show that neither one satisfies both requirements simultaneously. We then introduce the notion of the PML envelope, which quantifies the largest amount of information leakage about a secret after arbitrary post-processing of a mechanism's output. By construction, the PML envelope satisfies both requirements. We discuss basic structural properties of the envelope, such as monotonicity, and derive general upper and lower bounds. We further analyze the envelope for two widely used privacy mechanisms: the PML-extremal mechanisms in the high-privacy regime and randomized response. Overall, this work establishes the PML envelope as a natural and operationally meaningful definition for providing privacy guarantees that are preserved under arbitrary downstream transformations.

</details>


### [226] [Onion-Routed Multi-Circuit Key Establishment for Quantum-Resilient Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21349)
*Tushin Mallick,Ashish Kundu,Ramana Kompella*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Tor洋葱路由的会话密钥建立方案，通过将密钥分割为多个独立加密的片段，经由不同的临时Tor电路分发，以抵御量子计算机的"先存储，后解密"威胁。原型系统在AWS EC2上实现，平均密钥建立时间为13-20秒，其中约88%为Tor相关延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公钥密码体制（RSA、Diffie-Hellman、椭圆曲线密码）可归约为整数分解或离散对数问题，易受Shor算法攻击。量子计算机到来后，"先存储，后解密"威胁模型将未来漏洞转化为当下风险——当前存档的密文未来可被解密。

Method: 将新生成的密钥分割为多个独立加密的片段，每个片段通过不同的临时Tor电路传输。每个片段使用NEWNYM信号建立独立的每束电路，重建密钥需要所有片段。安全性依赖于洋葱路由的标准端到端关联界限：控制部分Tor中继的 adversaries 必须独立去匿名化每条新电路以关联属于同一会话的片段，且每个片段的成功概率随片段数量呈乘法衰减。

Result: 基于Flask的AWS EC2原型中，密钥建立平均耗时13-20秒（含尾部延迟为7-50秒），约88%的延迟归因于Tor本身的开销。

Conclusion: 该方案通过密钥分片与多路径分发增强了抵抗量子计算攻击的能力，但需在隐私保护与响应速度之间进行权衡。

Abstract: Public-key primitives that today anchor session-key establishment - RSA, Diffie-Hellman, and elliptic-curve cryptography - reduce to integer factorization or discrete logarithm and are therefore vulnerable to Shor's algorithm on a sufficiently capable quantum computer. The harvest-now, decrypt-later (HNDL) threat model turns this future capability into a present liability: ciphertext archived today can be decrypted retrospectively once a cryptographically relevant quantum computer becomes available. We propose a session-key establishment scheme that distributes a freshly generated key as multiple, independently encrypted fragments across distinct, ephemeral Tor circuits between an onion-service proxy and an onion-service client. Reconstruction requires every fragment; each fragment travels its own per-bundle circuit established via a NEWNYM signal. The security argument rests on the standard end-to-end correlation bound for onion routing: an adversary controlling a fraction of Tor relays must independently deanonymize every fresh circuit to correlate the fragments belonging to one session, and the per-fragment probability of success decays multiplicatively in the number of fragments. We implement the design as a Flask-based prototype on AWS EC2, with both the proxy and the client deployed as Tor onion services, and measure end-to-end key-establishment latency. The implemented prototype completes a key establishment in 13-20 s on average (7-50 s including tails), of which approximately 88% is attributable to Tor-related delay - a cost we discuss in the context of the privacy-versus-responsiveness trade-off.

</details>


### [227] [Auditing Apple's DifferentialPrivacy.framework: Implementation Bugs, Misconfigurations, and Practical Risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21378)
*Rishav Chourasia,Ergute Bao,Uzair Javaid,Xiaokui Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对苹果macOS Sonoma 14.2和Sequoia 15.6中的差分隐私框架进行了客户端审计，通过逆向工程和运行时测试，发现9个机制中有5个存在DP违规，影响Sonoma中87%和Sequoia中68%的数据收集，主要问题包括浮点噪声采样器的不安全实现、安全聚合配置中本地DP被禁用等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 苹果自2016年声称其设备数据分析受差分隐私保护，但因其私有化算法未开源，这些隐私声明难以独立验证。因此，作者希望对苹果的差分隐私框架进行独立审计，检验其实际部署是否达到宣称的隐私保证。

Method: 对macOS Sonoma 14.2和Sequoia 15.6上的DP框架进行客户端审计：逆向工程已发布二进制文件，恢复Objective-C接口，构建运行时测试工具执行苹果部署的机制，并检验其输出是否匹配宣称的隐私保证。审计涵盖Count Median Sketch、Hadamard-CMS、随机响应机制和Prio风格安全聚合等几乎所有活跃部署机制。

Result: 发现多个实现错误和配置错误。每个依赖浮点噪声的审计机制都因不安全的采样器（存在已知浮点漏洞）而未能达到宣称的DP或零知识证明保证。安全聚合配置中本地DP被禁用，使预聚合记录暴露给任何有权访问日志的一方。总共9个审计机制中有5个存在DP违规，影响macOS Sonoma中87%和Sequoia中68%的数据收集。还发现公开泄露的iPhone日志可被解码以恢复隐私信息，包括Safari域名和键盘emoji信号。

Conclusion: 苹果的差分隐私框架在实际部署中存在严重的实现缺陷，导致其未能兑现所宣称的隐私保护承诺。这些问题普遍存在且影响范围广泛，建议苹果修复浮点噪声采样器、重新检查安全聚合配置，并对隐私关键系统进行独立审计。

Abstract: Since 2016, Apple has claimed that device analytics collected to improve user experience are protected by differential privacy (DP). Apple's DifferentialPrivacy.framework is deployed across its operating systems and handles sensitive signals such as Safari domains, keyboard events, photo attributes, and health-related reports. Because Apple has not open-sourced its privatization algorithms, these privacy claims have been difficult to verify independently.
  We present a client-side audit of Apple's DP framework on macOS Sonoma 14.2 and Sequoia 15.6. We reverse engineer the shipped binaries, recover Objective-C interfaces, build runtime harnesses that execute Apple's deployed mechanisms, and test whether their outputs match the advertised privacy guarantees. Our audit covers nearly all active deployed mechanisms, including Count Median Sketch, Hadamard-CMS, randomized-response mechanisms, and Prio-style secure aggregation.
  We find multiple implementation bugs and misconfigurations. Every audited mechanism that relies on floating-point noise fails to meet its advertised DP or zero-knowledge proof guarantee, due to insecure samplers with known floating-point vulnerabilities. We also find secure-aggregation configurations with local DP disabled, exposing pre-aggregation records to any party with access to those logs. Overall, we find DP violations in 5 of 9 audited mechanisms, affecting 87% of data collection in macOS Sonoma and 68% in Sequoia. We also identify public leaked iPhone logs that can be decoded to recover private information, including Safari domains and keyboard emoji signals.

</details>
